Abstract: Image-to-3D generation has become increasingly capable of producing objects that closely resemble the input image, and an outstanding challenge is to reproduce the depicted object itself, including the specific geometry that defines it. Inscriptions, brand marks, and repeated structures are frequently distorted or lost, despite being critical to object identity. We present Hi3D 3.0, an image-to-3D generation system targeting object-specific fidelity, with Twinkle3D as its geometry model for generating watertight triangle meshes at $2048^{3}$ resolution. Twinkle3D advances high-fidelity geometry generation along four dimensions. First, while O-Voxel/FaithC offers high representational precision, it often suffers from poor surface quality and non-watertight geometry. We address both issues while retaining its $2048^{3}$-level precision. Second, we scale diffusion generation to sequences of up to 300K geometric tokens through a redesigned DiT architecture and large-scale distributed training optimizations, reducing training time per step from approximately ten minutes to ten seconds. Third, subsequent refinement cannot fully compensate for errors introduced during initial generation; we therefore strengthen both global shape and local detail in the initial generation stage, and the resulting single-stage model surpasses prior two-stage pipelines with $512^{3}$ refinement. Finally, we introduce a fine-grained image-3D cross-modal interaction mechanism that strengthens correspondence between visual evidence and geometric tokens, improving the recovery of object-specific structures. We evaluate geometric fidelity using alignment metrics derived from silhouettes and normal fields. Hi3D 3.0 outperforms four commercial systems across all reported metrics, recovering 82.1% of inscribed characters at 98.2% precision, compared with 21.7% recall for the strongest competitor.

</details>


### [105] [Towards Unified Evaluation of Prompt Enhancers for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11736)
*Yawen Shao,Yubo Zhu,Ziyun Dai,Zixun Fang,Kai Zhu,Zeyinzi Jiang,Yufeng Ai,Siyang Sun,Haolan Xue,Yu Shang,Yuxiang Bao,Zoubin Bi,Jingming Luo,Jie Xiao,Chaojie Mao,Zhehan Kan,Hongchen Luo,Yu Liu,Sheng Zhong,Wei Tong,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Wei Zhai,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PEBench，首个面向文本、图像、参考三类视频生成提示增强（PE）的直接评测基准，包含 1,100 个专家校验案例与 1,005 个视觉素材，覆盖 35 项细粒度任务，并配套基于证据的 24 项标准评测框架，无需渲染视频即可评估 PE 质量，且与专家判断及下游视频效用高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PE 评测依赖渲染后的视频，计算与人工成本高，拖慢 PE 训练与迭代，并将 PE 质量与下游生成器行为混为一谈，缺乏对 PE 本身的直接评测手段。

Method: 构建 PEBench：1,100 个专家校验案例、1,005 个视觉素材、35 项细粒度任务，覆盖时间、电影化、视听与多参考需求；提出 PEBench evaluation，将模态感知的事实抽取与基于评分细则（24 项标准）的评估相结合的、以证据为基础的评测框架；对代表性开源与闭源 PE 方法进行系统性评测，并用人工验证与 Wan3.0、MiniMax-H3 的下游视频比对。

Result: 评测揭示 PE 方法正从细粒度描述扩展转向保留意图的电影化规划；标题重建（caption-reconstruction）与前向精炼（forward-refinement）方法分别在电影化覆盖与语义保真、内部一致性方面展现互补优势；人工验证表明 PEBench 分数与专家对增强提示及下游视频的判断高度吻合。

Conclusion: PEBench 提供了首个统一的 PE 直接评测基准与证据支撑的评测框架，证明提示层面的评测能可靠反映下游效用，为 PE 的训练、迭代与比较提供了低成本且有效的评估途径。

Abstract: Modern video generators can realize increasingly complex visual narratives, positioning the prompt enhancer (PE) as a critical bridge from concise user instructions and multimodal references to structured cinematic plans. However, existing PE evaluation relies on rendered videos, imposing substantial computational and human costs, slowing PE training and iteration, and conflating PE quality with downstream generator behavior. To address this gap, we introduce PEBench, the first unified benchmark for direct PE evaluation across text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video prompt enhancement. It comprises 1,100 expert-verified cases and 1,005 visual assets, spanning 35 fine-grained tasks with diverse temporal, cinematic, audiovisual, and multi-reference requirements. In addition, we develop PEBench evaluation, an evidence-grounded framework that combines modality-aware fact extraction with rubric-based assessment across 24 criteria. Our systematic evaluation of representative open- and closed-source PE methods reveals an emerging shift from fine-grained descriptive expansion toward intent-preserving cinematic planning, while the caption-reconstruction and forward-refinement methods show complementary strengths in cinematic coverage and semantic fidelity or internal coherence, respectively. Human validation shows that PEBench scores align closely with expert judgments of enhanced prompts and downstream videos from Wan3.0 and MiniMax-H3, indicating that prompt-level evaluation reliably reflects downstream utility.

</details>


### [106] [Streaming-Aware Diffusion for Real-Time Video Super-Resolution via Cross-Step Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11746)
*Harris Partaourides,Sotirios Chatzis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种流式感知框架，将预训练单图潜在扩散模型改造用于实时视频超分，通过跨步注意力和轨迹耦合扩散调度，在512×512下实现超40 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频超分需要在严格延迟约束下兼顾时空保真度，而扩散模型的迭代采样开销大、时间协调能力弱，难以直接应用于实时VSR。

Method: 利用视频流的序列结构，设计Cross-Step Attention在相邻帧与扩散步之间复用中间去噪特征，实现无需显式时间建模的时序信息交换；提出Trajectory-Coupled Diffusion Scheduling对齐相邻扩散状态并提供更干净的条件表示以增强时序一致性；将两者集成到流式推理管线中增量传播隐状态。

Result: 在REDS4和YouHQ40-Test上提升感知质量与时序真实感，保持逐帧稳定性；计算复杂度从O(N·S)降至O(N+S)；冷启动后在512×512分辨率下达到40 FPS以上。

Conclusion: 该框架无需显式时间建模即可实现实时视频超分，验证了复用扩散中间特征与耦合扩散调度的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time video super-resolution requires high spatio-temporal fidelity under strict latency constraints, challenging diffusion models due to their iterative sampling cost and limited temporal coordination. We propose a streaming-aware framework that adapts pretrained single-image latent diffusion models for efficient video super-resolution (VSR) by exploiting the sequential structure of video streams. Our Cross-Step Attention mechanism reuses intermediate denoising features across adjacent frames and diffusion steps, enabling temporal information exchange without explicit temporal modeling. We further introduce Trajectory-Coupled Diffusion Scheduling, which aligns adjacent diffusion states and provides cleaner intermediate representations for cross-step conditioning, improving temporal coherence. These components are integrated into a streaming inference pipeline that incrementally propagates latent states across frames, reducing the effective computational complexity from $O(N \cdot S)$ to $O(N + S)$ for $N$ frames and $S$ diffusion steps. Experiments on REDS4 and YouHQ40-Test demonstrate improved perceptual quality and temporal realism while maintaining frame-wise stability. Our method achieves over 40 FPS at $512 \times 512$ resolution after cold start, enabling real-time VSR without explicit temporal modeling.

</details>


### [107] [Dino Forcing Flow Models: Do not denoise what you can predict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11751)
*Arijit Ghosh,Lucas Degeorge,Paul Couairon,Alexei A Efros,Vicky Kalogeiton,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用直接预测预训练表征并以其预测为条件的方式，替代需要二次去噪轨迹的 co-denoising 方法，实现更快收敛和更优生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Co-denoising 预训练表征（如 DINO）能显著提升 flow matching 的训练速度与质量，但引入了第二条去噪轨迹，并需要精心设计调度策略，增加了复杂性。作者希望寻找更简单的替代方案，同时保留表征引导的收益。

Method: 直接预测预训练表征，然后将模型条件建立在其自身预测之上。该方法去除了第二条 ODE 以及任何针对表征的去噪调度需求。

Result: 收敛速度显著加快，FID 生成质量更优。在 ImageNet 上，以比先前方法少 2 倍的 epoch 超越潜空间的最先进水平；在像素空间，FID 相比同类先前方法提升超过 20%。

Conclusion: 结果支持一个简单原则：不要对可以预测的东西进行去噪（do not denoise what you can predict）。代码已开源。

Abstract: Co-denoising pretrained representations such as DINO can substantially improve the training speed and quality of flow matching models, but it introduces a second denoising trajectory and requires carefully designed schedules. We propose a simpler alternative: predict the pretrained representation directly, then condition the model on its own prediction. This removes the need for a second ODE and any representation-specific denoising schedules, while retaining the benefits of representation guidance. Our approach converges substantially faster and achieves better generation quality as measured by FID score. On ImageNet, it outperforms the state of the art in latent space at 2x fewer epochs than prior methods; in pixel space, it improves FID over comparable prior methods by more than 20%. These results support a simple principle: do not denoise what you can predict. Our code is openly available at https://github.com/arijit-hub/dino_forcing.

</details>


### [108] [Memory Forcing: Attendable Mid-Horizon History for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11756)
*Jiaming Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Huafeng Shi,Gangshan Wu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Memory Forcing 少步流式视频扩散方法，用固定大小缓存保存中程历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步自回归视频扩散系统仅保留开头和最近帧的固定 KV 缓存，导致事件离开窗口后无法被后续帧关注，即中程遗忘问题。

Method: 采用 Archive &amp; Working Banks 将缓存划分为 sink、archive 和 working 区域，在固定显存下保留多样化中间事件与近期运动；并用 Bank-aware RoPE 在注意力时重分配时间索引，避免绝对索引超出训练范围。

Result: 在 1.3B 规模下长片段表现领先，5s 到 60s 的性能下降最小，并在离开-返回场景中保持主体和场景一致；该设计可扩展至 Wan2.2 5B，生成更符合物理、真实且动态的视频。

Conclusion: Memory Forcing 在不增加缓存大小的前提下缓解中程遗忘，兼顾长时一致性与可扩展性，并发布了首个该路线的公开 5B 模型。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables causal video streaming without a bidirectional pass over the full clip, but existing few-step systems usually retain only the opening and most recent frames in a fixed-size KV cache. Once an event leaves this window, later frames can no longer attend to it, a failure we term mid-horizon forgetting. We present Memory Forcing, a few-step streaming method that preserves this missing history without increasing the cache size. Its Archive \& Working Banks partition the cache into sink, archive, and working regions, retaining diverse intermediate events alongside recent motion under fixed memory. Because absolute temporal indices drift outside the training range, Bank-aware RoPE reassigns indices at attention time so each bank remains distinguishable. At 1.3B, Memory Forcing leads on longer clips, shows the smallest drop from 5s to 60s among methods reporting all four lengths, and preserves subjects and scenes through leave-and-return. The same design scales to Wan2.2 5B, producing more physically plausible, realistic, and dynamic videos and, to our knowledge, the first public 5B model on this forcing line.

</details>


### [109] [From Video Clips to Creation Trajectory: Sora100K for AI-Native Video Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11770)
*Sicong Yang,Ruihuan Yang,Jian Lu,Jianfei Yuan,Xiaodong Cun,Xiuli Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sora100K，一个将 AI 原生视频创作建模为结构化"视频创作轨迹"的数据集，包含生成根节点、单轮编辑边和多轮编辑完整轨迹，并通过 VLM 语义标注与严格构建流程重建源到编辑的谱系关系，实验表明其能提升视觉质量、多镜头生成与跨镜头一致性，而多轮编辑指令遵循仍是难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集大多只包含孤立的视频片段，把视频生成与视频编辑当作两个独立任务处理，而非相互连接的工作流阶段。然而 AI 原生视频创作正从孤立片段转向迭代式创作流程，缺乏能够刻画这种连续创作轨迹的数据基础。

Method: 作者首先识别视频创作轨迹，并按结构角色分解为三个子集：文本生成视频记录作为根节点，单轮视频编辑记录作为编辑边，多轮编辑记录作为完整轨迹；随后使用 VLM 为生成根分配语义标注、为编辑边分配编辑操作标注；再通过严格的构建流程重建源到编辑的谱系、编辑顺序与中间视频状态，以保证数据质量；最后对 LTX-2 模型进行轻量适配以评估 Sora100K 的监督价值。

Result: 实验结果显示，在视觉质量、多镜头生成和跨镜头一致性方面均有提升；而连续轮次评估表明，遵循多轮编辑指令仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: Sora100K 为超越孤立视频片段、走向结构化视频创作轨迹的 AI 原生视频创作建立了新的数据基础，数据集与补充材料已公开。

Abstract: AI-Native video creation is shifting from isolated video clips toward iterative video creation workflows. However, existing datasets remain largely video clips, representing video generation and editing as separate tasks rather than connected stages of a video creation workflow. In this paper, we introduce Sora100K, a dataset that represents the AI-Native video creation workflow as a structured video creation trajectory. Specifically, we first identify video creation trajectories and decompose them into three subsets according to their structural roles: text-to-video generation records as roots, single-turn video editing records as editing edges, and multi-turn video editing records as complete trajectories. Then, we use a VLM to assign semantic annotations for generation roots and editing-operation annotations for editing edges. A strict construction pipeline further reconstructs source-to-edit lineage, editing order, and intermediate video states while ensuring data quality. Finally, we perform lightweight adaptation on LTX-2 models to assess the supervision value of Sora100K. The results show improvements in visual quality, multi-shot generation, and cross-shot consistency, while successive-turn evaluation reveals that following multi-turn editing instructions remains challenging. Sora100K establishes a new data foundation for AI-Native video creation beyond isolated video clips and toward structured video creation trajectory. The dataset and supplementary materials are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ysicong/Sora100K.

</details>


### [110] [Skill-V: Verifiable Self-Evolving Skill Library for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11781)
*Jie Ma,Zhipeng Qian,Yufei Ma,Zihan Liang,Jiayi Ji,Qingpeng Cai,Ben Chen,Peng Jiang,Xiaoshuai Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Skill-V：可验证的自演化技能库，将技能表示为可证伪的版本化契约，利用环境结果驱动技能新增与边界修订，并通过回放验证与适用性过滤实现可靠演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化技能库主要靠累积新知识来提升：失败会催生新技能，而已存储的技能很少随新证据被重新审视。但仅靠增长无法保证可靠性——检索到的技能可能在当前任务条件下不适用，已有技能也可能编码了错误设定的操作边界。因此可靠的技能演化不仅需要增加知识，还需要对已有知识进行检验与修订。

Method: 提出 Skill-V 可验证自演化技能库。将技能表示为版本化、可证伪的契约，把语义意图与可观测的行为准则关联起来，使存储知识可被检验。以环境结果驱动库演化：任务失败促成技能新增，契约评估与任务结果之间的不一致则引导对已有技能边界的修订。修订须保留受保护的语义约束，并满足在历史回放证据上评分-结果指标的非退化准则；最后用适用性感知过滤器排除被判定为高置信不适用于当前任务的候选。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上，Skill-V 成功率分别达到 95.3% 和 85.9%，且技能库比面向增长的基线更紧凑。适用性感知过滤减少了错误的技能调用，基于结果的修订纠正了错误设定的技能边界，且不降低在既有观测证据上的表现。

Conclusion: 可靠的技能演化不止于积累经验：技能库必须学会保留哪些知识、何时修订它、以及何时应当应用它。

Abstract: Interactive agents can turn experience into reusable skills, yet existing self-evolving skill libraries primarily improve by accumulating new knowledge. Failures may lead to new skills, while previously stored skills are less often revisited as new evidence arrives. However, growth alone does not ensure reliability, as a retrieved skill may be inapplicable under the current task conditions, and an existing skill may encode a mis-specified operational boundary. Reliable skill evolution therefore requires not only adding knowledge, but also testing and revising what is already stored. We introduce Skill-V, a verifiable self-evolving skill library. To make stored knowledge testable, we propose representing skills as versioned, falsifiable contracts that link semantic intent to observable behavioral criteria. We use environment outcomes to drive library evolution. Specifically, task failures motivate skill addition, while disagreements between contract evaluations and task outcomes guide revisions to existing skill boundaries. To validate these revisions, we require them to preserve protected semantic constraints and satisfy non-regression criteria for rubric-outcome metrics on historical replay evidence. Finally, we employ an applicability-aware filter to exclude candidates judged confidently inapplicable to the current task. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, Skill-V achieves success rates of 95.3% and 85.9%, respectively, while maintaining a more compact skill library than growth-oriented baselines. Applicability-aware filtering reduces incorrect skill invocations, and outcome-grounded revisions correct mis-specified skill boundaries without degrading performance on previously observed evidence. These results show that reliable skill evolution requires more than accumulating experience: the library must learn which knowledge to retain, when to revise it, and when it should be applied.

</details>


### [111] [Phase-aware video generation for physics-grounded dynamics and interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11791)
*Jingfeng Ou,Kun Wang,Rui Zhao,Jingwei Guan,Limin Wang,Chao Dong,Xingyu Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAVG，一种面向固-气动力学的相感知视频生成器，采用双分支架构分别建模固相与气相动力学，并通过时空交叉注意力捕捉两相物理交互，同时构建了含70万+物理轨迹的仿真语料库，实验表明其在运动一致性、物理合理性和视觉质量上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固-气动力学视频生成具有挑战性：不同相具有各自的运动特性，同时两相又通过物理相互作用耦合在一起，现有方法难以同时兼顾相特异性运动与跨相物理一致性。

Method: 提出PAVG（Phase-Aware Video Generator）：采用双分支架构显式建模固相与气相各自的动力学；使用时空交叉注意力（spatiotemporal cross-attention）捕捉两相之间的物理交互；并构建了覆盖多种固、气及固-气交互场景、超过70万条物理轨迹的仿真语料库以支撑该任务。

Result: 大量评估表明，与现有方法相比，PAVG生成的视频在运动一致性（motion adherence）、物理合理性（physical plausibility）和视觉质量方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过相感知的双分支设计与跨相交互建模，并辅以大规模物理仿真数据，PAVG能够生成兼具相特异性运动与跨相物理一致性的固-气动力学视频，验证了显式相分离建模与交互建模的有效性。

Abstract: Generating physically plausible videos for solid-gas dynamics is challenging as different phases exhibit distinct dynamics yet remain coupled through physical interactions. We present PAVG, a Phase-Aware Video Generator for solid-gas dynamics and interactions. It employs a dual-branch architecture to explicitly model the distinct dynamics of solids and gases, while spatiotemporal cross-attention captures their physical interactions. This design enables PAVG to preserve phasespecific motion characteristics while producing physically consistent responses across phases. To facilitate this task, we further construct a simulation corpus comprising over 700K physical trajectories across diverse solid, gas, and solid-gas interaction scenarios. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our PAVG produces videos with improved motion adherence, physical plausibility, and visual quality compared with existing approaches.

</details>


### [112] [Seek-and-View Reasoning for Multi-View Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11810)
*Qixiang Chen,Cheng Zhang,Fucai Ke,Chi-Wing Fu,Jianfei Cai,Jingwen Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vantage，一种免训练、模型无关的推理框架，通过"寻视-观看"方式为VLM定位问题相关视角并合成视觉证据，提升多视角空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角空间推理方法主要依赖稀疏输入视角，视觉语言模型只能在这些固定视角内理解场景和推断空间关系，导致跨视角对齐脆弱，并存在从几何到语言的转换瓶颈。

Method: 提出Seek-and-View推理范式，通过定位与问题相关的视角来寻找隐含的跨视角空间证据；实现为Vantage框架，将VLM与3D基础模型配对，包含两个阶段：视角锚定的推理阶段（问题分析与视角规划），以及几何锚定的证据增强阶段（合成并融入视觉证据），全程无需训练。

Result: 在六个VLM和五个基准上进行了全面实验，均取得一致提升，且无需微调。

Conclusion: 通过基于视角锚定的推理揭示空间证据，Vantage可大幅减少对基于语言的跨视角对齐的依赖，提升多视角空间理解能力。

Abstract: Existing approaches to multi-view spatial reasoning operate largely on sparse input views. Vision-language models (VLMs) are thus restricted to understand a scene and infer spatial relations within these fixed views, leading to fragile cross-view alignment and geometry-to-language bottleneck. To address these issues, we formulate a novel Seek-and-View reasoning approach to find implicit cross-view spatial evidence by locating a question-relevant view to support the spatial reasoning. To realize this approach, we propose Vantage, a training-free model-agnostic reasoning framework that pairs a VLM with a 3D foundation model: a viewpoint-grounded reasoning stage for question analysis and view planning, followed by a geometry-grounded evidence augmentation stage to effectively synthesize and incorporate visual evidence into the final reasoning. Comprehensive experiments on six VLMs demonstrate consistent improvements on five benchmarks without fine-tuning. Overall, by revealing spatial evidence through view-grounded reasoning, Vantage can largely reduce reliance on language-based cross-view alignment and improve multi-view spatial understanding. Our code is available at https://github.com/q1xiangchen/Vantage.

</details>


### [113] [Fast Pose Tracking of Rigid Objects with Compact Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11815)
*Xiaojie Zhang,Tom Fischer,Viktor Larsson,Eddy Ilg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级模块，通过在位姿图中仅使用按几何对齐不确定性加权的相对位姿约束（而非基于点的优化），叠加在多种对应估计方法上，实现快速、鲁棒的长时程6D目标位姿跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对新物体的长时程6D位姿跟踪要么需要昂贵的预处理（onboarding），要么需全程维护重建，难以满足机器人操作与增强现实中"即用且实时"的需求。

Method: 关键思路是避免位姿图中基于点的优化，仅对相对位姿约束进行优化，并用从几何对齐中推导出的不确定性对这些约束加权，使优化不依赖于对应点数量，同时避免直接引入噪声点测量。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准上，方法达到了与基于重建的跟踪器相当的跟踪精度，而优化成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 紧凑且可靠的位姿图优化能以显著更低的计算成本实现长时程一致性，适用于即用型实时跟踪场景。

Abstract: Tracking a novel object's 6D pose over long horizons currently requires either expensive onboarding or a reconstruction maintained throughout the sequence. This makes current trackers impractical for robotic manipulation and augmented reality, which need trackers that are ready to use and run in real time. We show that a lightweight tracking module can be applied on top of a wide range of correspondence estimation methods to keep drifts bounded while maintaining fast runtime. Our key idea is to avoid point-based optimization in the pose graph and operate only on relative pose constraints, which we weight by a derived uncertainty from the geometric alignment. This makes optimization independent of the number of correspondences while avoiding the direct inclusion of noisy point measurements, leading to fast and robust long-term tracking. Across four real-world benchmarks, our approach achieves tracking accuracy comparable to reconstruction-based trackers with a fraction of the optimization cost. Overall, these results suggest that a compact and reliable pose graph optimization can provide long-horizon consistency at substantially lower computational cost.

</details>


### [114] [From Pixels to Structure: Lightweight Vision-Language Models for Document OCR and Structured JSON Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11818)
*Uddipan Basu Bir,Vincent Christlein,Andreas Maier,Mathias Zinnen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统性评测8个开源轻量级VLM（≤7B）在三个高校文化遗产馆藏的OCR到结构化JSON任务中的表现，探索零样本、少样本与微调策略，并在微调基线上分别检验超参优化、经典图像预处理和多阶段训练的独立贡献，同时比较单数据集微调与多数据集联合训练的性能权衡，为遗产机构提供可控、私有、可持续的高性能抽取方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模闭源VLM虽在文档理解上表现优异，但依赖商业API带来数据自主性、复现成本与超大规模计算环境足迹等问题，在机构档案（尤其遗产数字化）场景中尤为突出。遗产文档包含历史手写、领域术语（珠宝、史前、建筑）与非标准版式，需要高维结构化抽取，因此亟需评估开源轻量模型能否成为可持续、私密且高性能的替代方案。

Method: 在三个高校遗产馆藏上，对8个开源轻量级VLM（≤7B）进行OCR到结构的对比研究：输入文档图像，输出文本并生成符合schema的JSON以支持自动验证与下游使用。采用约束感知协议，在零样本、少样本和微调三种设置下评测，使用CER、ANLS*、mAP-F1衡量抽取保真度与结构化输出质量。在微调基线上分别检验(i)超参优化、(ii)经典图像预处理（光照平坦化、去噪、CLAHE）、(iii)多阶段训练的独立影响。此外分析单数据集微调与单一多数据集checkpoint之间的权衡。

Result: 结果显示，经过精心适配的≤7B参数VLM能够提供可持续、私有且高性能的替代方案，优于人工转录或商业黑盒系统；联合训练可使单一模型跨馆藏运行，但可能引起数据集间性能的转移。经典图像预处理、超参优化与多阶段训练各自带来可测的增益。

Conclusion: 研究证明轻量开源VLM在遗产OCR到JSON抽取任务中具备可行性与实用价值，并为寻求机构自主可控OCR-to-JSON抽取的遗产机构提供了可操作的实践指南。

Abstract: While massive, closed-source Vision-Language Models (VLMs) set strong benchmarks for document understanding, their dependence on commercial APIs limits adoption in institutional archives due to data autonomy concerns, recurring costs, and the environmental footprint of hyperscale computing. This is especially acute in heritage digitization, where documents include historical handwriting, domain-specific terminology (e.g., jewelry, prehistory, architecture), and non-standard layouts requiring high-dimensional structured extraction. We present a comparative study of eight open-source lightweight VLMs (up to 7B parameters) for Optical Character Recognition (OCR)-to-structure across three university heritage collections. Given a document image, models must extract text and generate schema-compliant JSON, enabling automatic validation and downstream use. We evaluate models under a constraint-aware protocol across zero-shot, few-shot, and fine-tuning settings, measuring extraction fidelity and structured-output quality using Character Error Rate (CER), Approximate Normalized Levenshtein Similarity (ANLS*), and mean Average Precision F1 (mAP-F1). Against a fine-tuning baseline, we further test the independent impact of (i) hyperparameter optimization, (ii) classical image preprocessing (illumination flattening, denoising, and CLAHE), and (iii) multi-stage training. Finally, we analyze the trade-off between dataset-specific fine-tuning and a single multi-dataset checkpoint, where joint training enables one model to operate across collections but can shift performance between datasets. Overall, we show that carefully adapted VLMs with up to 7B parameters can provide a sustainable, private, high-performing alternative to manual transcription or commercial black-box systems, and we offer actionable guidance for heritage institutions seeking institution-controlled OCR-to-JSON extraction.

</details>


### [115] [From Suppression to Repair: Mitigating Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models via Localized Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11826)
*Chen Zhao,Xingping Dong,Jiachun Shi,Liang Peng,Chong Wang,Zhen Lei,Ran He,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法ResOT，通过局部化分布对齐在推理时修复表征，减轻大型视觉语言模型的物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体幻觉是大型视觉语言模型生成可靠内容的主要障碍。直觉性的抑制幻觉相关分量策略可能损失有用信息，削弱模型的多模态能力。

Method: ResOT将主导的幻觉方向从忠实子空间中投影移除，形成低维残差子空间进行干预；在该子空间内使用高斯最优传输（OT）将幻觉分布对齐到忠实分布，所得映射在最小改变原始表征的前提下定义修复目标；推理时自适应控制每个token状态向OT目标移动的程度。

Result: 在三个代表性LVLM上的实验表明，ResOT大幅减少物体幻觉，同时在多个基准上提升图像描述质量与多模态性能。

Conclusion: ResOT是一种免训练、推理时修复表征的方法，能在不削弱多模态能力的前提下有效缓解物体幻觉，代码将开源。

Abstract: Object hallucination remains a major obstacle for large vision-language models (LVLMs) to generate reliable content. An intuitive mitigation strategy is to suppress hallucination-related components in hidden representations. However, these components may also contain useful information, and suppressing them can weaken the model's multimodal capabilities. In this paper, we propose ResOT, a training-free method that repairs representations at inference time through localized distribution alignment. Specifically, ResOT projects dominant hallucinated directions away from the faithful subspace, forming a low-dimensional residual subspace for intervention. Within this subspace, ResOT uses Gaussian optimal transport (OT) to align the hallucinated distribution with the faithful one. The resulting map defines repair targets with minimal changes to the original representations. At inference, ResOT adaptively controls how far each token state moves toward its OT target. Experiments on three representative LVLMs show that ResOT substantially reduces object hallucination while improving image caption quality and multimodal performance across multiple benchmarks. Code will be released.

</details>


### [116] [Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Event Localization via Complex-Valued Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11846)
*Anirudh Praveen,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula,Pratik Joshi,Aveen Dayal,Neela Sawant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出用复数神经网络学习视觉-文本与音频-文本相似度的融合，替代固定规则，在开放词汇音视频事件定位任务上取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇音视频事件定位方法将视觉-文本和音频-文本两个余弦相似度用固定规则（几何平均、加权平均）合并为单一分数再取argmax，这种静态融合无法自适应地学习模态间关系。

Method: 将各模态标准表示作为实部，并引入配对伴随流作为虚部：视觉模态使用iHSV的虚部，音频模态使用CycleGAN转换的相位谱图，从而得到两个复数相似度，再用复数神经网络（CVNN）进行融合。视觉与音频编码器保持冻结，仅训练时序注意力模块和融合CVNN。此外还提出一个两流替代方案。

Result: 在OV-AVEBench开放（未见类）划分上达到66.5/59.1/54.1%的Acc/Seg-F1/Event-F1，较此前微调基线提升+1.6/+4.1/+6.6，已见类也有稳定增益；在修改后的AVE数据集上达到60.7/51.9/50.4%Acc/Seg-F1/Event-F1，同样取得SOTA。两流替代方案也显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 用复数表示与CVNN学习模态融合，有效替代固定融合规则，在两个OV-AVEL基准上均刷新SOTA，验证了复数四流架构的有效性。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Event Localization (OV-AVEL) labels each video segment with an event class, including classes that were never seen during training. The dominant pipeline uses a frozen multimodal foundation model (e.g. ImageBind) to embed the visual frame, the audio mel-spectrogram, and each candidate class name into a shared space, then computes two cosine similarities for each segment against each class: visual-text and audio-text. Existing methods then collapse this pair into a single scalar score with a fixed rule (geometric mean, weighted average) before taking the argmax. Instead, we compute complex-valued similarities and learn their fusion using a complex-valued neural network (CVNN). Each modality's standard representation becomes the real part of our pipeline, and a paired companion stream supplies the imaginary part. We use imaginary part of iHSV for visual modality and CycleGAN-translated phase spectrogram for audio modality as these companion streams. This results in two complex similarities, which are then fused. While the vision and audio encoders remain frozen, only the temporal-attention blocks and the fusion CVNN are trained. The four-stream complex architecture sets a new state of the art on both OV-AVEL benchmarks. On the open (unseen-class) split of OV-AVEBench we reach 66.5/59.1/54.1% Acc/Seg-F1/Event-F1 (+1.6/+4.1/+6.6 over the previously reported fine-tuned baseline), with consistent gains for seen classes as well. We also modify AVE dataset for this task and observe that our architecture reaches 60.7/51.9/50.4% Acc/Seg-F1/Event-F1, achieving state-of-the-art OV-AVEL results on it as well. We also propose a two-stream alternative, which also sees great improvements over the baseline.

</details>


### [117] [VEDJE: Video-Efficient Discriminative Joint Encoder for Scalable Video-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11850)
*Shahaf Wagner,Gabriele Serussi,Dan Ben Ami,Tomer Galanti,Chaim Baskin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEDJE通过压缩视频特征并缓存可复用表示，在不增加查询时开销的情况下提升视频检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频检索需要区分包含不同事件的相似场景，联合匹配(联合第一阶段的检索与重排)能提升效果，但为每个查询处理丰富的视频表示代价高昂。

Method: VEDJE在采样帧内压缩特征，同时在可复用缓存中保持其表示分离；引入特征变化预测作为辅助训练信号，在不增加查询时计算量的前提下改善从压缩缓存的检索。

Result: 在MSR-VTT、MSVD、DiDeMo和ActivityNet上，VEDJE在双向检索中均较匹配的第一阶段检索器提升R@1。MSR-VTT上配合微调VideoCLIP-XL第一阶段达到59.8的文本到视频R@1；VideoPrism配置下将每视频缓存缩小四倍至12 KiB，文本到视频召回损失在0.2个点以内。

Conclusion: 准确的视频搜索可基于紧凑证据运行，证据编码一次即可在新查询到来时复用。

Abstract: Finding the right video often requires distinguishing similar scenes in which different events occur. Joint matching improves retrieval, but processing rich video representations for each query is costly. VEDJE compresses features within sampled frames while keeping their representations separate in a reusable cache. Feature-change prediction supplies an auxiliary training signal that improves retrieval from the compressed cache without adding work at query time. On MSR-VTT, MSVD, DiDeMo, and ActivityNet, VEDJE improves R@1 over matched first-stage retrievers in both retrieval directions. On MSR-VTT, it reaches 59.8 text-to-video R@1 with a fine-tuned VideoCLIP-XL first stage. In the VideoPrism configuration, shrinking the per-video cache fourfold to 12 KiB preserves text-to-video recall within 0.2 points. These results show that accurate video search can operate on compact evidence, encoded once and reused as new queries arrive.

</details>


### [118] [Pose-Free Feed-Forward 3D Inpainting via Learnable Mask Attention and Support Token Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11857)
*Jingyi Pan,Dan Xu,Qiong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FreeInpaint，一个无需相机位姿的前馈式 3D 场景修复框架，可直接从未标定多视角图像的掩码区域生成完整且 3D 一致场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景修复方法通常依赖精确标定的相机位姿，限制了其在随意拍摄的自然场景中的适用性，并带来额外预处理开销。

Method: 将 3D 基础模型扩展至掩码输入：1) 提出可学习掩码注意力（Learnable Mask Attention），在保留可靠观测空间锚定的同时让掩码区域逐层吸收上下文，缓解掩码对跨视角对应推理及位姿/几何恢复的干扰；2) 提出支持令牌精炼（Support Token Refinement），将扩散生成的证据作为置信度加权的辅助令牌注入，以改善严重遮挡下单次前向传播的细节恢复。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，FreeInpaint 取得优异的修复质量，同时摆脱对预计算相机位姿的依赖并保持快速推理速度。

Conclusion: FreeInpaint 通过前馈方式统一了 3D 重建与场景修复，在无位姿条件下实现高质量、3D 一致的场景补全，拓展了 3D 修复的实际应用范围。

Abstract: 3D scene inpainting aims to recover missing or occluded regions in edited 3D scenes, while ensuring geometric and textural consistency. Existing approaches, however, typically require accurately calibrated camera poses, which restricts their applicability in casual, in-the-wild scenarios and introduces additional preprocessing overhead. To overcome this limitation, we present FreeInpaint, a novel feed-forward framework that generates complete and 3D-consistent scenes directly from unposed multi-view images with masked regions. At its core, FreeInpaint extends a 3D foundation model to propagate masked regions from a reference view to other unposed views, bridging 3D reconstruction and scene inpainting while preserving the model's native ability to recover camera poses and scene geometry. Our method addresses two key challenges in adapting feed-forward 3D foundation models to masked inputs. First, masked regions can corrupt cross-view correspondence reasoning, degrading pose estimation and geometry recovery. To address this, we introduce a Learnable Mask Attention mechanism that preserves the spatial anchoring of reliable observations while allowing masked regions to progressively absorb useful context in deeper layers. Second, under severe occlusions, a single forward pass often lacks sufficient appearance evidence for high-fidelity completion. Therefore, we propose a Support Token Refinement strategy, which injects diffusion-generated support evidence as confidence-weighted auxiliary tokens to refine under-observed regions while preserving the original spatial anchor. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that FreeInpaint achieves superior inpainting quality, eliminating the reliance on pre-computed camera poses while keeping a fast inference speed. The project page is https://rorisis.github.io/FreeInpaint/.

</details>


### [119] [Relative Patch Response Learning for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11876)
*Tianyu Wang,Ouxiang Li,Yanbin Hao,Zhenhua Tang,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出相对块响应学习（PRL），通过比较同一块在两个混合视图间的响应变化来检测AI生成图像，解决自注意力引起的上下文偏移问题，在多个基准上超越先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型能合成高度逼真的图像，增加了虚假信息和视觉伪造风险，因此检测AI生成图像愈发重要。检测器需泛化到未见的生成器并对野外未见扰动保持鲁棒。现有检测器要么在独立收集的真假图像上训练，要么在对齐的真假图像对上训练以缓解内容偏差。近期基于对齐对的方法通过将真实图像的某些块替换为生成对应块形成混合视图，但自注意力会让真实块与生成块相互交互，导致每个块的特征不再仅反映其自身来源，这种上下文偏移使逐块来源标签成为不精确的目标。

Method: 提出相对块响应学习（PRL），不对每个块打标签，而是比较同一块在对齐对的两个混合视图中的表现，学习其块响应（即该块得分在两个视图间的变化）。包含三个目标：(i) 相对响应目标，用源未变块（仅响应偏移）来度量源改变块的响应，以在上下文偏移下提供精确监督；(ii) 参考一致性目标，让每组源未变块作为整体移动，为偏移提供可靠参考；(iii) 区域排序目标，由于两个视图包含的生成内容量不同，要求生成面积更大的视图具有更高的平均块得分。

Result: 大量实验表明PRL性能优越，在八个标准基准和三个野外基准上，平均平衡准确率分别比最佳先前方法高出4.3%和5.9%。

Conclusion: PRL通过相对块响应学习替代逐块来源标签，有效应对自注意力导致的上下文偏移，显著提升了AI生成图像检测的泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generative models can now synthesize highly realistic images, simultaneously increasing the risks of misinformation and visual forgery. Therefore, detecting AI-generated images becomes more essential, and a reliable detector must generalize to unseen generators and stay robust to unseen perturbations in the wild. Existing detectors are typically trained on either independently collected real and generated images or aligned real-generated pairs designed to mitigate content bias. Building on aligned pairs, recent methods form a mixed view by replacing some patches of the real image with their generated counterparts. However, we find that self-attention lets real and generated patches interact, so the feature of each patch no longer reflects its own source alone. This contextual shift makes a per-patch source label an imprecise target. To this end, we propose Relative Patch Response Learning (PRL). Instead of labeling each patch, PRL compares the same patch across two mixed views of an aligned pair and learns from its patch response, the change of its score between the views. (i) To give precise supervision under the contextual shift, a relative response objective measures the responses of source-changed patches against those of source-unchanged patches, which respond to the shift alone. (ii) To provide a reliable reference for the shift, a reference coherence objective keeps each group of source-unchanged patches moving as a whole. (iii) Since the two views contain different amounts of generated content, an area ranking objective asks the view with the larger generated area to have a higher mean patch score. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of PRL, which surpasses the best prior methods by 4.3% and 5.9% in average balanced accuracy across eight standard and three in-the-wild benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Visual Enhancement: Adaptive Multi-Context Steering to Mitigate LVLM Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11907)
*Shuran Ma,JiaLe Li,Yuxin Dong,Shan Zheng,Qingyun Jiang,Xiang Chen,Qi Zhu,Deyi Ji,Yifan Yang,Jianfeng Pan,Yu Tian,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AIMS，一种无需训练的轻量框架，通过自适应协调视觉、预填充文本与生成上下文三类来源来抑制LVLM幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM幻觉问题严重，现有免训练方法多依赖对比解码或视觉增强，通常只是提高视觉证据的相对影响，缺乏对多上下文来源贡献的动态调控探索。

Method: 先量化LVLM解码时如何协调多种上下文来源；发现其存在内在视觉关注倾向且文本上下文也能帮助抑制幻觉；据此构造视觉、预填充文本、生成文本三类上下文的紧凑原型，通过估计其与当前查询的亲和度确定逐头引导权重，将多源引导方向施加于查询表示，无需额外训练或辅助前向传播。

Result: 在多种LVLM和多种解码策略上的大量实验表明，AIMS能有效缓解物体幻觉，同时保持有竞争力的通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: LVLM可通过动态调节不同上下文来源的贡献来抑制幻觉，AIMS以轻量免训练方式实现了自适应多源引导并取得良好效果。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a significant challenge in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). Existing training-free methods generally mitigate hallucinations through contrastive decoding or visual enhancement, often increasing the relative influence of visual evidence during generation. This raises a fundamental question: Can LVLMs dynamically regulate the contributions of different context sources to suppress hallucinations? In this work, we investigate and quantify how LVLMs coordinate multiple context sources during decoding and examine how this intrinsic behavior can guide hallucination mitigation. We find that LVLMs exhibit an intrinsic vision-attending tendency that can guide adaptive visual steering, while textual contexts can also contribute to hallucination mitigation. Motivated by these findings, we propose AIMS (Adaptive Information Multi-source Steering), a lightweight training-free framework that adaptively coordinates visual, prefilled textual, and generated contexts during decoding. Specifically, AIMS constructs compact prototypes for the three context domains and estimates their affinities with the current query to determine head-wise steering weights. The resulting multi-source steering direction is applied to the query representation, enabling adaptive context integration without additional model training or auxiliary forward passes. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLMs and decoding strategies demonstrate that AIMS effectively mitigates object hallucination while maintaining competitive general-purpose multimodal capabilities.

</details>


### [121] [Revisiting Identity and Spectra Dispersion in Media-Bridged Time Series Forecasting: Linking Multivariate Signals and Narrative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11924)
*Jierui Lei,Wenjian Zhang,Qingyi Yang,Yuyang Hong,Fangzheng Chen,Zhengbo Zhang,Haina Tang,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的时空预测骨干MIDAPN，通过多媒体身份感知图与谱棱镜卷积，兼容多变量与多模态时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型依赖特定范式的关系、融合与时间模块，难以在数值型"多变量"与预对齐叙事流"多模态"设定间共享统一骨干。

Method: (1) 多媒体身份感知图(MIDAG)从静态本质、动态行为与潜在共性重构身份，跨媒体扩展变量依赖，并由上下文身份调制(CIM)优化判别性聚合；(2) 谱棱镜卷积(SPConv)自动进行分层时间分析，平衡粗粒度趋势与细粒度细节，其自适应搜索引导配置尺度高效架构。

Result: 在13个"多变量"与12个"多模态"数据集上与16个SOTA模型对比，以及与14个时间序列基础模型和融合预训练语言模型的定向长上下文比较中，MIDAPN均表现出一致优越性与广泛的骨干兼容性。

Conclusion: 解耦协同的组件共同解决媒体身份解缠与时间尺度失配问题，验证了统一预测骨干的可行性。

Abstract: Media-bridged time series forecasting is expanding to encompass traditional "multivariate" and emerging "multimodal" (e.g., through textual assistance). Existing Time Series Forecasting (TSF) models still rely on paradigm-specific relation, fusion, and temporal modules, hindering a common forecasting backbone across numerical and pre-aligned narrative-flow settings. To explore this, we propose the Multimedia Identity-Aware Prism Network (MIDAPN), a unified spatiotemporal forecasting backbone based on media-general graph adaptation and automatic temporal learning: (1) Following media pre-alignment, our Multimedia Identity-Aware Graph (MIDAG) revisits identity through static essence, dynamic behavior, and latent commonality, inducing affinities that extend variable-specific dependencies across media. Contextual Identity Modulation (CIM) further refines discriminative aggregation. (2) We develop Spectral Prism Convolution (SPConv) to automatically perform hierarchical temporal analysis, balancing coarse trends and fine-grained details. Meanwhile, its Adaptive Search Guidance configures a scale-efficient architecture for temporal-dimension reconstruction. These decoupled yet synergistic components jointly address media identity disentanglement and temporal-scale mismatch. Comprehensive evaluations involving 16 SOTA TSF models across 13 "multivariate" and 12 "multimodal" datasets, alongside targeted long-context comparisons against 14 time series foundation models and fused pretrained language models, demonstrate MIDAPN's consistent superiority and broad shared backbone compatibility. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/leijieruilq/MIDAPN/tree/main}{https://github.com/MIDAPN}.

</details>


### [122] [Does Target Alignment Mean Target Recovery? An Evidence-Ladder Study of Adversarial Claims on Contrastive Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11938)
*Tao Yang,Jianying Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑对抗攻击中常用的"受害模型目标相似度"(VTS)能否预测独立模型对目标的可恢复性，通过构建测量工具和两项预注册研究，发现VTS仅在固定鲁棒编码器内部具有参考价值，并提出了替代性报告协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型的对抗攻击通常以受攻击模型的相似度分数(VTS)作为攻击成功的证据，但该分数是否真能反映独立模型对目标的恢复能力尚不清楚。作者希望检验VTS作为成功指标的效力，避免因依赖受攻击模型自身几何空间而产生误导性结论。

Method: 构建测量工具：使用对抗几何之外的监督评判器、真实目标混合对照、打乱目标负样本，以及由50%目标-图像混合导出的参考水平。随后进行两项预注册研究，在匹配攻击下、三种扰动预算下比较六种对比编码器(包括FARE、TeCoA、PMG、TRADES等鲁棒训练编码器与vanilla CLIP、SigLIP)。

Result: 鲁棒训练的编码器比vanilla CLIP或SigLIP传递出显著更多的独立证据，全部八组对比在bootstrap下限处被拒绝；但没有一个单元格达到混合参考水平，最好的三个单元格落在其重复带内，实际可恢复性未定。在鲁棒编码器内部，逐样本对齐增益与证据增益相关(ρ=0.24-0.51)，而vanilla CLIP中相关性与零一致；跨编码器则不存在单调的对齐-证据关系。

Conclusion: VTS仅在固定鲁棒编码器内部具有信息价值，不能跨模型作为攻击成功的通用证据。作者据此提出了替代的举报/报告协议，以更可靠地评估对抗攻击的目标恢复效果。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on vision-language models optimize an image toward a text target, then cite the attacked model's similarity score as evidence of success. We ask whether that score - victim-space target alignment (VTS) - predicts recovery of the target by an independent model. We first build a measurement instrument: supervised judges outside the attacked geometry, real-target blend controls, shuffled-target negatives, and a reference level derived from a 50% target-image blend. Two preregistered studies then compare six contrastive encoders under a matched attack at three perturbation budgets. Robustly trained encoders (FARE, TeCoA, PMG, TRADES) transfer substantially more independent evidence than vanilla CLIP or SigLIP; all eight contrasts reject at the bootstrap floor. However, no cell reaches the blend-derived reference level. The three best cells fall within its replication band, leaving practical recovery undecided. Within robust encoders, per-sample alignment gain correlates with evidence gain ($ρ= 0.24-0.51$); within vanilla CLIP the correlation is consistent with zero. Across encoders we find no monotone alignment-evidence relation. VTS is therefore informative only within a fixed robust encoder, and we provide a reporting protocol in its place.

</details>


### [123] [Right Screen, Wrong Transition: World Models as Verifiers for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11942)
*Jiaming Zhang,Xuan Wang,Fuyao Zhang,Yang Cao,Lingjuan Lyu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出用于验证的GUI世界模型LGWM，直接在表示空间预测下一屏状态，将安全校验简化为向量比较，在RSWT-BENCH上以极低延迟达到领先检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI安全本质上是"转场"属性而非"屏幕"属性：合法屏幕在异常转场后即为攻击，因此仅检查屏幕的监控可被复用合法屏幕绕过。判断转场需要"下一步应是什么"的预期，现有GUI世界模型以文本、代码或图像输出预期，需第二个模型比对，效率与准确性受限。作者认为面向验证的世界模型应在观测编码空间中进行预测。

Method: 提出LGWM：无解码器、动作条件化的世界模型，直接预测下一屏的表示；在185万条真实GUI转场上训练，无需语义标注。验证被化简为向量比较，并利用残差方向区分有害与良性偏差。

Result: 在RSWT-BENCH（每条凭证屏幕同时出现于合法与劫持转场，仅看屏幕的检测器AUC天然为0.5）上，免训练评分达0.987 AUC、每次决策17 ms，与最强闭源VLM相当，比生成式GUI世界模型高约10个AUC点且延迟低三个数量级以上；残差方向区分有害/良性偏差达0.953 AUC，而提示VLM接近随机。

Conclusion: 世界模型此前多作为模拟器或规划器，本文指出其第三种角色——验证，而在表示空间中预测是实现该角色的自然设计。

Abstract: A login screen that appears after a tap on Sign in is expected; the same screen after a tap on View order is an attack. For GUI agents, safety is therefore a property of the transition rather than of the screen, and a monitor that inspects only screens can be defeated by reusing a legitimate one. Judging a transition requires an expectation of what should have followed the action. Existing GUI world models provide one, but they output it as text, code, or images, so checking it against the observed screen requires a second model to judge the two. We argue that a world model meant for verification should instead predict in the space in which observations are encoded, and present LGWM, a decoder-free, action-conditioned world model that predicts the representation of the next screen directly, trained without semantic annotation on 1.85M real GUI transitions. Verification reduces to a vector comparison, and the same signal reveals whether a mismatch is harmful. We evaluate on RSWT-BENCH, a diagnostic where each credential screen appears under both a legitimate and a hijacked transition, so detectors that see only the screen are at chance by construction. The training-free score reaches 0.987 AUC at 17 ms per decision, on par with the strongest closed-source VLMs and about ten AUC points above generative GUI world models at over three orders of magnitude lower latency. The residual direction reaches 0.953 AUC at separating harmful from benign violations, where prompted VLMs are near chance. Further analyses show that the prediction is a usable future state rather than an anomaly score. World models have mostly served as simulators or planners; our results point to a third role, verification, for which predicting in representation space is the natural design.

</details>


### [124] [Look Where You Can: Active View Selection for CAD Reconstruction under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11954)
*Kartik Bali,Mahish Guru,Yiderigun Borjigin,Alexandra Starostina,Christian J. Cyron,Roland Aydin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SightCAD 将"视角可行性"作为参数化CAD重建的一等约束，在真实室内场景的可见性限制下联合训练视角选择器与VLM生成CadQuery代码，显著优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD重建方法假设可以无限制地从任意角度拍摄物体，但现实中的物体往往嵌入场景（贴墙、卡在角落、放在地上），导致视角球面上大量视角不可达，且可达视角的信息量差异很大。论文希望把视角可行性作为建模的核心约束，解决这一"奢侈假设"与现实不符的问题。

Method: SightCAD 将标准CAD基准物体嵌入真实室内场景，并在离散视角球上施加物理推导的可见性约束。框架包含一个可学习的视角选择器，为视觉-语言模型（VLM）挑选 K 个可行视角，VLM 据此生成可执行的 CadQuery 代码，并用执行后实体的几何保真度作为奖励。由于奖励只在离散视角选择、自回归生成和CAD内核执行之后才出现，作者提出联合训练范式，让视角选择器与CAD生成VLM针对该奖励共同训练。

Result: 学到的选择策略明显区别于随机、均匀和覆盖贪心策略，在 K∈{1,…,5} 的预算下比表面积最大化（SA-max）高出最多 6.4 个 IoU 点。完整系统在 DeepCAD 与 Fusion360 场景嵌入、遮挡多视图渲染上分别超过最佳基线 +21 和 +17 有效 mIoU 点，并在工业 T-LESS 基准的测试时域规范化真实图像、以及 MP6D 工业金属零件基准的合成与真实图像上均表现优异，同时可执行程序率最高（无效代码率 ≤1.5%）。

Conclusion: 将视角可行性显式纳入建模并与生成模型联合优化，能有效提升场景嵌入物体的参数化CAD重建质量与代码可执行率，为真实受限观测条件下的CAD逆向工程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: CAD reconstruction methods assume a luxury reality rarely grants: unrestricted visual access to the object, photographed from any desired angle. Real objects, however, are scene-embedded, bolted against walls, wedged into corners, resting on floors, where the scene renders much of the view sphere unreachable and the remaining views unequally informative. We introduce \textbf{SightCAD}, a framework for parametric CAD reconstruction that treats view feasibility as a first-class constraint. In this work we consider objects from standard CAD benchmarks embedded in realistic indoor scenes with physically derived visibility constraints over a discrete view sphere. A learned view selector must choose $K$ feasible views for a vision--language model (VLM) that generates executable CadQuery code, scored by geometric fidelity of the executed solid. Because reward arrives only after discrete view selection, autoregressive generation, and CAD-kernel execution, we propose a joint training paradigm in which the view selector and the CAD-generation VLM are trained together against this reward. The learned selection policy departs sharply from random, uniform, and coverage-greedy alternatives, outperforming surface-area maximization (SA-max) by up to $6.4$ Intersection-over-Union (IoU) points across budgets $K\in\{1,\dots,5\}$. The full system surpasses strong external baselines on scene-embedded, occluded multi-view renders of DeepCAD and Fusion360 objects ($+21$ and $+17$ effective-mIoU points over the best baseline, respectively), as well as on test-time domain-canonicalized real images from the industrial T-LESS benchmark and on both synthetic and real images from the MP6D industrial metal-parts benchmark, while producing the highest rate of executable programs of any method compared (invalid-code rate ${\leq}1.5\%$).

</details>


### [125] [Learning Which Correspondences to Trust: Confidence-Weighted Event-Camera Localization in LiDAR Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11967)
*Panagiotis Kiousis,Kuangyi Chen,Jun Zhang,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CELL方法，学习每个3D-2D对应关系的置信度以改进事件相机在LiDAR地图中的定位，在M3ED和DSEC上中位平移误差降低最多26.9%，中位旋转误差降低最多15.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机对LiDAR地图定位的流程依赖几何一致性进行位姿估计，但未显式建模各对应关系的可靠性或位姿信息量（即该对应关系对相机位姿的约束强度）。直接用逐对应误差来学习置信度会受深度偏差影响：小像素误差多出现在大深度处，并不带来高位的姿信息量。

Method: 通过可微概率PnP端到端学习逐对应置信度，其对数配分项鼓励产生位姿分布约束更好的权重配置；学习到的置信度用于三方面：(i)在解耦训练中重加权光流监督，避免位姿梯度进入光流/边缘主干；(ii)测试时进行概率对应选择；(iii)结合网络边缘概率加权最终的边缘匹配精化。此外设计部分补全深度表示，在不跨大间隙虚构的前提下增加信号。

Result: 在M3ED和DSEC上，完整系统在多数评估序列上优于LEAR基线，中位平移误差最多降低26.9%，中位旋转误差最多降低15.8%。

Conclusion: 以位姿为导向、端到端学习的逐对应置信度可有效克服深度偏差，显著提升事件相机对LiDAR地图的定位精度。

Abstract: Localizing an event camera against a pre-built LiDAR map can be cast as dense optical-flow estimation between a rendered depth view and an event image, followed by a Perspective-n-Point (PnP) solver over the induced 3D-2D correspondences. Existing pipelines rely on geometric consensus during pose estimation, but do not explicitly model the reliability or pose informativeness, i.e., how strongly a correspondence constrains the camera pose, of individual correspondences. We show that the natural way to learn it -- using the per-correspondence error to constrain the learning of confidence -- suffers from a depth-dependent bias: small pixel errors reside predominantly at large depths and do not lead to high pose informativeness. Instead, in our method (CELL), we learn a per-correspondence confidence end-to-end through the pose, using a differentiable probabilistic PnP whose log-partition term encourages weight configurations that yield a better-constrained pose distribution. The learned confidence is used in three ways: (i) it reweights the flow supervision in a decoupled training scheme that keeps pose gradients out of the flow/edge backbone; (ii) it drives a probabilistic correspondence selection at test time; and (iii) together with the network's edge-probability it weights a final edge-matching refinement. We further design a partial-completion depth representation that adds signal without hallucinating across large gaps. On M3ED and DSEC our full system improves over the LEAR baseline on the majority of the evaluated sequences: it reduces the median translation error by up to 26.9% and the median rotation error by up to 15.8%.

</details>


### [126] [FearCaut-Qwen: Affective Steering in a Vision-Language Model Shifts the Decision Criterion for Hazard Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11986)
*Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究用信号检测论诊断视觉语言模型在灾害损伤评估中"过度保守"的决策缺陷，并借助机制可解释性定位一个与恐惧相关的情绪回路，通过激活引导在推理时调整决策标准，显著提升危险召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在灾后损伤评估中，即使整体准确率尚可，却倾向于不宣告危险、召回率偏低。作者希望区分这一缺陷究竟源于感知能力不足还是决策标准过于保守，并寻求一种无需重新训练的纠正方法。

Method: 用信号检测论将决策行为分解为感知能力（d'）与决策标准（c）；受"恐惧使人风险规避"启发，寻找与恐惧相关的情感表征方向。通过机制可解释性定位因果相关的情感回路，用稀疏神经元剔除与分布式引导在情感数据上验证，再用激活引导将该方向注入基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct的两阶段SeisMLLM建筑损伤评估流程。

Result: 基线模型在SeisMLLM-1K测试集上仅标记27.0%的真实不安全建筑，且从不误报Red（c=+1.354），而证据质量充足（d'=1.521）。注入恐惧方向后Red召回升至75.7%（p<0.001）；减去同一方向则完全抑制Red预测；等范数随机方向与匹配的快乐方向均无显著效果。该机制表现为标准移动（c=-1.515），而辨别力并未提升（d'=-0.493）。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的决策可在推理时被调整而无需重训练，说明机制可解释性可用于诊断并控制工程应用中的VLM决策行为。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) show great potential for damage assessment after a disaster, but a recurring deficiency is that they are reluctant to declare a hazard; that is, recall is low even when overall accuracy appears adequate. This study examines that deficiency by using signal detection theory to decompose the decision behavior into perceptual capability and decision-criterion placement. We then propose a novel method for correcting the over-conservative decision policy, inspired by the finding that fear makes humans risk-averse, and ask whether an affective representation associated with fear can be causally manipulated to similarly alter a VLM's decision tendency. Using mechanistic interpretability, we localize a causally implicated affective circuit in the model and use activation steering to manipulate it while observing the effect on downstream prediction. The method is tested on a two-stage SeisMLLM pipeline built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct, which flags only 27.0% of genuinely unsafe buildings on the SeisMLLM-1K test split and never issues a false Red, an SDT criterion of c = +1.354, despite adequate evidence quality (d' = 1.521). An affective direction is localized on emotion-rich natural scenes, causally validated by sparse-neuron knockout and distributed steering on held-out emotion data, and then injected into the building task. Fear-direction injection raises Red recall to 75.7% (p<0.001), and subtracting the same direction suppresses Red predictions entirely, whereas norm-matched random and matched happiness directions show no significant effect. The mechanism is a shift in criterion (c=-1.515) while discrimination is not improved (d'=-0.493). These results show that VLM decisions can be adjusted at inference time without retraining and demonstrate how mechanistic interpretability can be used to diagnose and control VLM decision behaviors in engineering applications.

</details>


### [127] [Do Not Train Away Uncertainty: Early Uncertainty Anchored Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12048)
*Yutong Xie,Jiawei Tang,Zhenglin Hua,Yuxiang Ma,Si Qin,Yaxin Hou,Hui Liu,Junhui Hou,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现模型在训练或微调早期校准性更好，后期虽精度提升有限却显著增加校准误差；提出EUA-Cal，利用早期模型作为不确定性锚点，通过预测正则化和原型结构正则化来缓解过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络（含大语言模型）在训练或微调过程中容易过度自信。作者观察到一种一致现象：早期模型校准性更好，而后期训练虽带来边际精度增益，却大幅增加校准误差。分析表明早期模型在预测和特征中保留了不确定性意识，但这种意识会随训练逐渐丧失。因此需要一种方法避免训练过程中丢失这种不确定性意识。

Method: 提出EUA-Cal方法，将早期模型作为不确定性锚点用于校准。具体包含两项正则化：1）早期预测正则化，用于保留早期预测中的不确定性；2）原型结构正则化，用于利用早期特征空间所反映的不确定性。二者联合作用以缓解过度自信。

Result: 在图像分类和多选题问答任务上，跨八种不同模型的广泛实验表明，EUA-Cal优于当前最先进的校准方法。

Conclusion: 早期模型具有更好的校准性和不确定性意识，通过EUA-Cal将其作为锚点可有效缓解训练过程中的过度自信问题，在多种任务和模型上取得优于SOTA的校准表现。

Abstract: Deep neural networks, including large language models, have achieved remarkable performance across various tasks. However, they are prone to overconfidence during training or fine-tuning. In this work, we observe a consistent phenomenon across different models that the early model is better calibrated, while later training or fine-tuning yields marginal accuracy gains but substantially increases calibration errors. Our analysis suggests that the early model retains uncertainty awareness in both its predictions and features, which is gradually lost with continued training. To avoid training away this uncertainty awareness, we propose \textbf{EUA-Cal}, a novel method that exploits the \textbf{E}arly model as an \textbf{U}ncertainty \textbf{A}nchor for \textbf{Cal}ibration. EUA-Cal introduces early prediction regularization to preserve early predictive uncertainty and prototype structure regularization to exploit uncertainty reflected in the early feature space, jointly mitigating overconfidence. Extensive experiments on image classification and multiple-choice question answering across eight diverse models demonstrate that EUA-Cal outperforms state-of-the-art calibration methods.

</details>


### [128] [Look Back, Think Ahead: Visual Memory on Demand for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12060)
*Yicheng Xue,Han Wu,Jufeng Yang,Minjing Dong,Xinghao Chen,Hanting Chen,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViMoD轻量框架，通过DART构建可恢复的粗细粒度视觉token表示、TRACE动态调度细粒度证据，在20%视觉token预算下于Qwen3-VL-4B上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像产生的长视觉token序列使多模态大模型的多步推理计算开销巨大。现有一次性剪枝与聚合方法在解码前将视觉token压缩为固定上下文，但推理过程中视觉证据需求会动态变化，固定压缩上下文难以保留各阶段所需细节。

Method: ViMoD维持紧凑视觉上下文并保留对原始细粒度证据的访问。DART（可变形区域token聚合）学习内容自适应分组与聚合能力，构建与可恢复原始细粒度token关联的紧凑粗粒度表示；TRACE（自适应上下文证据时序路由）结合解码历史预测后续证据需求，选择、保留或替换活跃的细粒度token组；被选中的细粒度token增强冻结骨干中的持久粗粒度上下文，实现阶段特定证据访问而无需持续关注全部视觉token。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-4B上，20%目标视觉token预算下，ViMoD在全部八个推理基准上优于所有评估基线，平均归一化分数较最强一次性基线提升39.0%；仅需相对冻结骨干0.0546%的额外可训练参数。

Conclusion: 动态、可恢复的视觉token压缩策略优于固定压缩方案，能以极小参数代价实现推理阶段的证据自适应访问。

Abstract: Processing long visual token sequences from high-resolution images makes multi-step reasoning computationally expensive for multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing one-shot pruning and aggregation methods compress visual tokens into a fixed context before decoding. However, visual evidence needs can shift as reasoning unfolds, making it difficult for a fixed compressed context to retain all the details needed across stages. To address this challenge, we propose ViMoD, a lightweight framework that maintains a compact visual context while preserving access to original fine-grained evidence as reasoning needs evolve. Deformable Aggregation of Region-wise Tokens (DART) learns content-adaptive groups and aggregation capacities, constructing compact Coarse representations linked to recoverable original Fine tokens. Temporal Routing for Adaptive Contextual Evidence (TRACE) integrates decoding history to anticipate upcoming evidence needs and select, retain, or replace active Fine-token groups. Selected Fine tokens augment the persistent Coarse context in the frozen backbone, enabling stage-specific evidence access without continuously attending to all visual tokens. On Qwen3-VL-4B, ViMoD outperforms all evaluated baselines on all eight reasoning benchmarks at a 20% target visual token budget, improving the mean normalized score by 39.0% over the strongest evaluated one-shot baseline. These gains are achieved with only 0.0546% additional trainable parameters relative to the frozen backbone.

</details>


### [129] [LIVIN: Benchmarking Spatial and Embodied Intelligence in Digital Twins of Lived-In Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12069)
*Peijun Xu,Chuansen Nie,Yiyang He,Yinuo Bai,Jingyang Liu,Kuixiang Shao,Yuyang Jiao,Kuanhao Xia,Jiayi Zhu,Zitian Yang,Yanqi Zhang,Tianye Tan,Shuwei Di,Junyi Xu,Jingyi Yu,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LIVIN，一个基于30个真实居住家庭数字孪生的空间与具身智能基准，用于评估3D检测、重建、导航和移动操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实家庭模拟需同时捕捉多样环境、真实物品布局与空间约束。现有资源在规模、真实对应性和交互就绪性之间难以兼顾，缺乏对真实家庭实际布置的忠实交互复刻。

Method: 构建30个多样真实居住家庭的数字孪生，保留房间布局、家具配置和日常物品。采用人在回路工作流：实例识别、建筑重建、物体生成与放置，各阶段中间结果由人工对照原始观测进行审查和修正。

Result: 在LIVIN上评估四项任务：3D检测、3D重建、导航和移动操作。结果显示，现有方法在密集物品布置、遮挡、有限自由空间和受限交互区域方面仍面临挑战。

Conclusion: LIVIN有望推动真实家庭环境中的具身AI研究，从空间理解到机器人交互，最终将具身智能带入日常家庭环境。

Abstract: Realistic household simulation must capture not only diverse environments but also the lived-in object arrangements and spatial constraints that shape robot motion and interaction. Existing resources often trade off scale, real-world correspondence, and interaction readiness, leaving a gap in faithful, interactive replicas of how real homes are actually arranged. To this end, we introduce LIVIN, a benchmark for spatial and embodied intelligence built on digital twins of 30 diverse lived-in homes. These replicas preserve observed room layouts, furniture configurations, and everyday belongings. To construct them, we design a human-in-the-loop workflow comprising instance recognition, architectural reconstruction, and object generation and placement, with intermediate results reviewed and corrected by humans against the source observations at each stage. We evaluate four tasks in LIVIN: 3D detection, 3D reconstruction, navigation, and loco-manipulation. Our evaluations show that current methods remain challenged by the dense object arrangements, occlusions, limited free space, and constrained interaction regions found in realistic lived-in homes. We hope LIVIN will help advance embodied AI in real-world homes, from spatial understanding to robotic interaction, and ultimately bring embodied intelligence into everyday home environments.

</details>


### [130] [Perception Test 2026: Challenge Summary and Extension to City-scale Audio-Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12081)
*Fedor Kitashov,João Carreira,Shiry Ginosar,Dima Damen,Andrew Zisserman,Viorica Pătrăucean*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了第四届Perception Test挑战赛（ECCV 2026 workshop），聚焦空间智能，包含四个赛道，并总结了各赛道获胜方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续Perception Test挑战赛系列，本届聚焦空间智能，并新增基于城市尺度步行视频的赛道以拓展评测范围。

Method: 在ECCV 2026举办workshop，设置四个赛道：原基准的统一多选视频QA与接地的视频QA，以及两个基于城市尺度步行视频的新赛道KilometerAudio和KilometerVision；介绍新基准并汇总各赛道获胜方案。

Result: 各赛道产生获胜方案，其中一个通用模型跨所有赛道参赛并取得令人满意的表现；新增城市尺度赛道的获胜方案依赖昂贵的智能体流水线。

Conclusion: 复杂的空间与多模态推理可通过昂贵的智能体流水线解决，但对单独使用的多模态模型而言仍然困难。

Abstract: Continuing the Perception Test challenge series, we organised the fourth edition as a workshop at the European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026 in Malmö, Sweden. This edition focused on spatial intelligence and featured four different tracks: unified multiple-choice videoQA and grounded videoQA from the original Perception Test benchmark, alongside two new tracks based on city-scale walking-tour videos (KilometerAudio and KilometerVision). In this report, we describe the new benchmarks used for the city-scale tracks and summarise the winning solutions across all tracks, including a generalist model that competed across all tracks with satisfactory performance. The winning solutions in the newly added city-scale tracks demonstrated that complex spatial and multimodal reasoning can be solved by expensive agentic pipelines, but remains difficult for multimodal models used standalone.

</details>


### [131] [DVLA-RL++: Dual-Level Vision-Language Alignment with Reinforcement Learning Gating for Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12095)
*Wenhao Li,Xianjing Meng,Qiangchang Wang,Zhongyi Han,Yilong Yin,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DVLA-RL++，通过互补语义净化（CSP）与反事实强化学习门控（CRG）缓解小样本学习中的上下文污染问题，在多个基准上取得最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本学习方法借助文本语义弥补视觉样本不足，但图像与文本的高一致性可能同时来自物体内在属性与偶然上下文，导致支持集原型易受上下文污染。

Method: 在DVLA-RL基础上扩展：CSP从有标签支持样本生成内在描述与干扰描述，比较其与各支持token的一致性，用依赖歧义的拒绝边界引导稀疏证据分配，并以内在语义锚填补未分配质量；CRG通过平衡识别性能与干扰暴露的奖励学习逐层语义融合强度，并用同一episode上独立参考轨迹提供配对学习信号。

Result: 理论分析将保留证据与锚质量关联到原型稳定性，并给出无偏同策略梯度估计的条件；在标准、细粒度与跨域基准上达到最优精度，平均较DVLA-RL提升1.4%。

Conclusion: DVLA-RL++有效抑制上下文污染，提升小样本类表示的鲁棒性与识别性能。

Abstract: Few-shot learning aims to recognize novel categories from limited labeled examples. Recent studies incorporate textual semantics to compensate for limited visual observations and improve class representations. However, high image-text agreement may reflect both intrinsic object properties and incidental context, making support prototypes susceptible to contextual contamination. To address this problem, we propose DVLA-RL++, which extends DVLA-RL with complementary semantic purification (CSP) and counterfactual reinforcement-learning gating (CRG). Specifically, CSP generates intrinsic and nuisance descriptions from labeled supports and compares their agreement with each support token. An ambiguity-dependent rejection margin guides sparse evidence allocation, while an intrinsic semantic anchor fills the unassigned mass to provide a fallback when visual evidence is unreliable. CRG learns layer-wise semantic fusion strengths using a reward that balances recognition performance and nuisance exposure. An independently executed reference trajectory on the same episode provides a paired learning signal. Theoretical analysis relates retained evidence and anchor quality to prototype stability and establishes conditions for unbiased on-policy gradient estimation. Experiments on standard, fine-grained, and cross-domain benchmarks show state-of-the-art accuracy, with an average gain of 1.4% over DVLA-RL. The project page is available at https://peacelwh.github.io/TPAMI27-DVLA-RLpp/.

</details>


### [132] [VINCIE-NExT: Unlocking Video Editing from Images via In-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12104)
*Leigang Qu,Feng Cheng,Ziyan Yang,Bangbang Yang,Zhaoyang Huang,Wei Chow,Yicong Li,Wenjie Wang,Tat-Seng Chua,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VINCIE-NExT通过"视频→图像→图像→视频"的可组合子任务链，将图像编辑能力迁移到视频编辑，利用上下文视觉示范实现像素级外观编辑传播，无需大规模配对视频数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑比视频生成更具挑战，因为构建视频编辑器需要(源视频, 指令, 编辑后视频)三元组，这类标注成本极高且难以规模化合成；相比之下，图像编辑已成熟，拥有数百万可用的配对数据。因此作者希望借助丰富的图像编辑数据来缓解视频编辑配对数据稀缺的问题。

Method: 提出统一框架VINCIE-NExT，将视频编辑分解为结构化可组合的子任务链（Video → Image → Image → Video），把编辑意图经由图像域进行路由，从而在统一的扩散目标下对异构的图像与视频语料进行可扩展的联合训练。通过将模型合成或用户提供的图像编辑对作为上下文视觉示范前置，作为每一输出帧的空间外观蓝图。引入新型位置编码，将图像示范与视频帧链接到共享空间坐标系，实现像素忠实的外观修改传播。Chain-of-Editing在测试时可将子任务链作为渐进式扩散阶段执行，无需重训练即可通过增加计算量提升编辑质量。

Result: 在OpenVE-Bench上的综合实验表明，该方法在多种编辑类别上达到最先进性能，消融实验证实了各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过图像到视频的上下文视觉示范迁移编辑能力，VINCIE-NExT在无需大规模配对视频编辑数据的条件下实现了高质量、可扩展且可通过测试时计算投入持续提升的视频编辑，为视频编辑提供了一条切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Building a capable video editor remains significantly harder than a video generator: editing requires (source, instruction, edited) triplets that are prohibitively expensive to annotate and difficult to synthesize at scale, whereas image editing has already reached maturity with millions of such pairs readily available. In this work, we introduce VINCIE-NExT, a unified framework that transfers editing capability from images to videos through in-context visual demonstrations, alleviating the need for large-scale paired video editing data. VINCIE-NExT decomposes video editing into a structured chain of composable sub-tasks (Video -> Image -> Image -> Video), routing editing intent through the image domain and enabling scalable joint training from heterogeneous image and video corpora under a unified diffusion objective. An image editing pair, synthesized by the model or supplied by the user, is prepended as an in-context visual demonstration that serves as a spatial appearance blueprint for every output frame. To ground appearance edits across the interleaved context, we introduce a novel position encoding that links image demonstrations and video frames in a shared spatial coordinate system, enabling pixel-faithful propagation of appearance changes to every output frame. Chain-of-Editing further provides principled test-time scaling: by executing the sub-task chain as progressive diffusion stages, editing quality can be improved by investing additional compute without retraining. Comprehensive experiments on OpenVE-Bench demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across diverse editing categories, with ablations confirming the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [133] [ContourVLA: A Closed-Loop Perception-Action Contour Policy for Generalized Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12107)
*Ruicheng Zhang,Kaiwen Shen,Jiaqi Hou,Shuhan Yang,Junchao Huang,Kewei Zhang,Jun Zhou,Li Jiang,Shen Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContourVLA 将广义指称表达分割（GRES）重构为闭环视觉运动过程，用可编辑轮廓作为策略状态，通过轮廓引导感知调度与实例级信用强化学习，在 gRefCOCO 与 RefCOCO 系列上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRES 需要同时动态平衡高层语义（识别数量可变的指称目标）与细粒度视觉证据（精确边界刻画），而现有级联视觉-语言架构依赖静态特征接口与单次掩码预测，导致自适应感知与几何校正能力受限。

Method: 提出 ContourVLA，一种视觉-语言-动作策略，将 GRES 建模为闭环视觉运动过程：可编辑轮廓作为显式策略状态，条件化多模态感知并由几何动作块更新。其中 Evolution-Aware Semantic Scheduling（EASS）将轮廓引导的双向边界采样与状态条件化的多层级多模态特征路由耦合；监督初始化后，采用 Dustbin-Augmented Entropic Credit Transport GRPO（DECT-GRPO）以实例级信用联合优化离散定位与连续轮廓动作，其 rollout 奖励与信用由考虑误检与漏检的软预测-目标对应关系导出。

Result: 在 gRefCOCO val、testA、testB 上，gIoU 较最强评估基线分别提升 8.7、2.8、2.7 个百分点；在 RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg 全部八个划分上取得最高 mIoU。

Conclusion: 将 GRES 重构为以轮廓为策略状态的闭环视觉-语言-动作过程，并结合演化感知语义调度与实例级信用强化学习，可有效提升指称表达分割的边界精度与自适应感知能力。

Abstract: Generalized referring expression segmentation (GRES) requires dynamically balancing high-level semantics for identifying a variable number of language-specified referents with fine-grained visual evidence for precise boundary delineation. This requirement challenges existing cascaded vision-language architectures, which typically rely on static feature interfaces and single-pass mask prediction, limiting adaptive perception and geometric correction. We introduce ContourVLA, a vision-language-action policy that recasts GRES as a closed-loop visuomotor process, in which editable contours serve as explicit policy states that condition multimodal perception and are updated by geometric action chunks. Evolution-Aware Semantic Scheduling (EASS) couples contour-guided bidirectional boundary sampling with state-conditioned routing of multilevel multimodal features, adapting perception to each contour state. Following supervised initialization, Dustbin-Augmented Entropic Credit Transport GRPO (DECT-GRPO) jointly optimizes discrete grounding and continuous contour actions with instance-level credits. Its rollout rewards and credits are derived from soft prediction-target correspondences that account for false positives and missed targets. ContourVLA improves gIoU over the strongest evaluated baselines by 8.7, 2.8, and 2.7 points on gRefCOCO val, testA, and testB, respectively, and achieves the highest mIoU across all eight RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and RefCOCOg splits.

</details>


### [134] [LVS: Local View Synthesis from Relative Camera Pose by Reusing Previous Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12127)
*Qizhou Huo,Xuan Sun,Yongfei Guo,Zhipeng Wang,Yuanhao Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种逐场景的RGB-D图像复用框架，用相对位姿引导几何warping加轻量多尺度网络预测残差，替代邻近视图的重复3D高斯渲染，实现低延迟交互式场景探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式场景探索需要频繁更新视图，但相邻视图间可见内容高度重叠。传统3D Gaussian Splatting对每个目标视图都完整渲染，未利用这种图像重叠；而单靠几何warping无法恢复新暴露内容，且对深度误差敏感。

Method: 逐场景框架：用深度和相对位姿将已渲染源图像warping到目标视图，替代重复渲染；用轻量多尺度网络预测RGB残差以修正伪影并推断缺失外观；同时缓存源特征以减少重复计算。

Result: 在GS-render上，残差精修相比纯warping提升PSNR约0.72 dB；在真实拍摄与合成渲染场景上均验证了低查询延迟。

Conclusion: 将场景渲染与局部视图更新解耦，可支持响应式场景探索，在AR/VR等场景具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Interactive scene exploration requires frequent view updates, although small camera motions preserve much of the visible content. Conventional 3D Gaussian Splatting nevertheless renders each target view, leaving this image overlap unexploited. Reusing rendered images offers an alternative. Geometric warping alone cannot recover newly exposed content and remains sensitive to depth errors. We propose a per-scene framework that replaces repeated scene rendering for nearby views with relative-pose-guided RGB-D image reuse. Geometric warping uses depth and relative pose to transport source content, while a lightweight multiscale network predicts RGB residuals to correct artifacts and infer missing appearance. Cached source features further reduce repeated computation. On GS-render, residual refinement improves PSNR by 0.72~dB over pure warping; evaluations on captured and rendered scenes demonstrate low query latency. This separation of scene rendering from local view updates supports responsive scene exploration, with potential applications in augmented and virtual reality.

</details>


### [135] [Healthy Counterfactual Generation via Diffusion Inpainting for Mammography Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12147)
*Inês Cruchinho Garcia,Mariana Mourão,Francisco Maria Calisto,Carlos Santiago,Jacinto Nascimento*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种反事实数据增强策略，利用扩散模型从异常乳腺X线图像中"擦除"病灶生成健康图像，以缓解CAD系统的假阴性问题，并在多种分类器架构上验证了敏感度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机辅助诊断（CAD）系统在乳腺癌筛查中因检测与治疗延迟而存在严重的假阴性问题，需要更可靠的诊断方法来改善敏感度。

Method: 在BI-RADS 1（健康）乳腺X线图像上训练去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM），采用基于RePaint的采样策略在标注的病灶边界框内修复逼真的正常组织，从而生成健康反事实图像以扩充训练分布。

Result: 生成的健康反事实图在保留患者特异性解剖结构的同时用逼真健康组织替换病灶区域，相似度指标及放射科医生评估均支持其真实性与原数据集质量一致。在VinDr-Mammo数据集上，该方法在ConvNeXt、ViT、Mammo-CLIP和FPN-MIL四种架构上均提升了敏感度，尤其在80%固定特异度下。

Conclusion: 反事实数据增强有效提升了多种分类器架构的敏感度，有助于构建更可靠的乳腺癌筛查CAD系统。代码已开源。

Abstract: False negatives remain a critical limitation of computer-aided diagnosis (CAD) systems for breast cancer screening due to delayed detection and treatment. To address this issue, we propose a counterfactual data augmentation strategy that generates healthy mammograms by "erasing" lesions from anomalous images, thereby enriching the training distribution. We train a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model on BI-RADS 1 (healthy) mammograms and use a RePaint-based sampling strategy to inpaint realistic normal tissue within annotated lesion bounding boxes. The resulting healthy counterfactuals replace annotated lesion regions with realistic healthy tissue while preserving patient-specific anatomical structure, as supported by similarity metrics between real and generated images. Image realism was further assessed by radiologists and found to be consistent with the original dataset quality. We evaluate counterfactual augmentation across four representative classifier architectures: a convolutional neural network (ConvNeXt), a vision transformer (ViT), a vision-language model pre-trained on mammogram-report pairs (Mammo-CLIP) and a multi-scale attention-based multiple-instance learning framework (FPN-MIL). Experiments conducted on the VinDr-Mammo dataset show improvements in sensitivity across all architectures, particularly at 80\% fixed specificity, contributing towards more reliable CAD systems for breast cancer. Code is available at: https://github.com/ines03garcia/diffusion-based-counterfactual-generation.

</details>


### [136] [Connected Self Forcing: Beyond Local Learning in Video Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12156)
*Dongbin Zhang,Chaoda Zheng,Kangjie Chen,Xiangyu Li,Shijia Chen,Jinhao Deng,Yuqi Zhang,Guangfeng Jiang,Hongbin Lin,Choo Sin Wai,Minqi Wang,Puyi Wang,Jingye Zhang,Yu Zhang,Xianming Liu,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Connected Self Forcing训练框架，通过重新连接自回归分块间的梯度路径，让后续预测的反馈指导早期上下文的生成，并配合shortcut gradient replay实现内存高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self Forcing通过自生成历史的自rollout训练缓解曝光偏差，但为控制内存会detach历史缓存，保留了前向依赖却切断了反向梯度路径，导致早期上下文的生成无法获得后续预测的反馈。

Method: 提出Connected Self Forcing，重新连接自回归分块间的梯度路径，使梯度经由生成的latents回到产生它们的计算过程，从而把早期上下文的生成与其在后续预测中的使用关联起来；并开发shortcut gradient replay，在不保留完整rollout计算图的情况下恢复跨块梯度，结合distribution matching distillation进行训练。

Result: 在自回归视频生成实验中，长时程视觉质量和时间一致性均有提升，且不改变推理过程。

Conclusion: 通过恢复跨块梯度连接，历史分块能同时依据直接监督和对后续生成的贡献进行训练，为长视频流式生成提供了一种内存高效且推理无关的训练方案。

Abstract: To stream long videos while maintaining visual quality and temporal consistency, Self Forcing mitigates exposure bias through self-rollout training on self-generated histories with key-value (KV) caching. To keep memory manageable, it detaches historical caches, preserving forward dependencies between chunks but severing the backward gradient paths. We introduce Connected Self Forcing, a training framework that reconnects gradient paths across autoregressive chunks, allowing feedback from later predictions to guide how earlier context is generated. These connections go beyond historical KV-writing: gradients pass through generated latents into the computations that produced them, linking the generation of earlier context to its use in later predictions. To make this connected training memory-efficient, we develop shortcut gradient replay, which recovers cross-chunk gradients without retaining the full rollout computation graph. Integrated with distribution matching distillation, Connected Self Forcing trains historical chunks according to both their direct supervision and their contribution to subsequent generation. Experiments on autoregressive video generation show improvements in long-horizon visual quality and temporal consistency, without changing the inference procedure.

</details>


### [137] [AI-Based On-Board Maritime Object Detection for Earth Observation Payload Data Reduction on Versal Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12182)
*Thomas Goudemant,Aurélien Bobey,Omar Hlimi,Marjorie Bellizzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究星上船只检测，用于按内容筛选下行数据，涵盖数据集构建、嵌入式部署与多种下行模式的数据缩减权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甚高分辨率地球观测卫星采集的数据量超过其存储与下行能力，而海事监视中船只仅占每个场景的极小部分。因此需要一种基于内容而非逐像素编码的星上筛选方式，作为传统星上压缩的补充。

Method: 研究分三条主线：（i）数据与算法：基于68幅带标注的Maxar场景、43类船只构建受控数据集，并训练YOLOX-S检测器；（ii）嵌入式部署：将检测器量化后部署到Versal VC1902的DPU上；（iii）数据缩减：提出多种下行模式，从仅元数据（检测框、类别、分数）到船只周围裁剪图、包含检测的瓦片，或背景降质的整幅场景，并根据实测检测误差估计各模式在保留船只数量与下行数据量之间的权衡。

Result: 检测器部署后检测质量损失有限，每幅场景处理时间仅数秒。在密集港口与海岸场景上，瓦片模式以29%的场景数据量保留98%的船只，裁剪模式以3%的数据量保留83%的船只。

Conclusion: 星上船只检测可有效按内容筛选下行数据，不同下行模式在船只保留率与数据量之间存在可量化的权衡，瓦片与裁剪模式表现突出，可作为传统星上压缩的补充方案。

Abstract: Very-high-resolution Earth-observation satellites acquire more data than they can store and downlink, while in maritime surveillance the vessels cover a tiny fraction of each scene. We study onboard vessel detection as a way to select what is downlinked, which reduces the data according to its content rather than coding every pixel; it is complementary to conventional onboard compression. The work follows three axes. (i) Data and algorithm: a controlled dataset is generated from 68 annotated Maxar scenes with 43 vessel classes, and a YOLOX-S detector is trained on it. (ii) Embedded deployment: the detector is quantized and deployed on the DPU of a Versal VC1902, with a limited loss of detection quality and a processing time of a few seconds per scene. (iii) Data reduction: we propose several downlink modes, from metadata only (box, class and score of each detection) to image crops around vessels, tiles holding detections, or the whole scene with a degraded background, and estimate from the measured detection errors the trade-off each offers between the vessels kept and the volume downlinked. On our dense harbor and coastal scenes, tiles keep 98% of the vessels with 29% of the scene volume, and crops 83% with 3%.

</details>


### [138] [Stride Independent Patching for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12216)
*Olivier Rukundo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出半自动、与步长无关的图块裁剪方法SSP，用专家/用户输入定位预定义图块，与重叠、非重叠裁剪对比，在三种骨干网络上验证其分割性能与训练效率更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动裁剪技术通常依赖步长，可能影响基于图块的分割效果；本文探索用半自动、与步长无关的裁剪方式作为替代方案。

Method: SSP利用用户或专家输入在一个或多个目标对象上放置预定义图块；据此构建SSP、重叠裁剪(Overlap)和非重叠裁剪(Noverlap)三个图块数据集；分别以ResNet50、ResNet18、MobileNetV2为骨干训练DeepLabV3+模型，并在各自测试集和统一外部测试集上评估。

Result: SSP在各自测试集上总体分割分数更高，且三种骨干下训练时间最短；外部测试集上SSP在三种骨干下平均精确率和F1最高，Noverlap平均召回率最高。

Conclusion: Noverlap和Overlap更大的空间覆盖并不一定带来更好分割性能，SSP在分割性能与训练时间之间提供了更好的平衡。

Abstract: This paper presents semi-automatic stride-independent patching (SSP) as an alternative to automatic stride-dependent patching techniques. SSP uses user or expert input to position predefined patches over one or more objects of interest. To evaluate its effectiveness, three patch-based datasets were created using SSP, overlapping patching (Overlap), and non-overlapping patching (Noverlap). DeepLabV3+ models with ResNet50, ResNet18, and MobileNetV2 backbones were trained sepa-rately on each dataset. Quantitative evaluations were performed on the respective test splits and a common external test set. SSP generally achieved higher segmentation scores on the test splits and required the shortest model training time across all three backbones. On the external test set, SSP achieved the highest average precision and F1-score across backbones, whereas Noverlap achieved the highest average recall. These preliminary results demonstrate that the potentially greater spatial coverage of Noverlap and Overlap does not generally translate into better segmentation perfor-mance and that SSP offers a favorable balance between segmentation performance and model training time.

</details>


### [139] [VibeEdit: Image Editing with Canvas Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12229)
*Jinjing Zhao,Fangyun Wei,Yitong Wang,Xiuyu Wu,Yunuo Chen,Yang Yue,Sirui Zhang,Wenbo Wang,Hongyang Zhang,Dong Chen,Yan Lu,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV
