TL;DR: 提出VibeEdit图像编辑界面：用户在图上用空间标记加简短注释形成"画布指令"，指明编辑位置与内容，无需额外文本提示，模型据此完成增删改移等编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导图像编辑中，描述"改什么"容易，但精确指认"改哪个目标/区域"很麻烦，尤其当多个物体外观相似时。

Method: 构建155万源-目标编辑对（含物体掩码与结构化编辑描述），训练时据此渲染画布指令；基于Qwen-Image-Edit，采用层解耦条件（分别编码源图与画布指令）；先用区域加权监督微调，再用评分标准引导的强化学习优化编辑完整性、局部质量与未编辑区域保持。

Result: 在独立的419例人工标注基准（强调相似物体中的目标选择）上，VibeEdit取得VLM评分79.9、区域外PSNR 32.8 dB；优于文本指令基线中得分最高的FireRed（67.4与24.0 dB）。

Conclusion: 空间标记+简短注释的画布式交互可有效解决目标指认难题，配合层解耦条件与分阶段训练，在编辑质量与背景保持上均显著优于文本指令方法。

Abstract: In text-guided image editing, describing the desired change is often straightforward, but identifying the intended object or region can be cumbersome, especially when several objects look alike. We introduce a new image editing interface that lets users place spatial marks and optional short notes directly on the image. Together, these annotations form a canvas instruction that specifies where to edit and what to change. Our editor, VibeEdit, follows these instructions to perform object addition, removal, replacement, attribute modification, and movement without a separate text prompt. We construct 1.55 million source-target edit pairs with object masks and structured edit descriptions, from which we render canvas instructions during training. We adapt Qwen-Image-Edit with layer-decoupled conditioning that separately encodes source images and canvas instructions for image editing. We train the model with region-weighted supervised fine-tuning, followed by rubric-guided reinforcement learning to improve edit completion, local edit quality, and preservation of unedited regions. We evaluate VibeEdit on an independently constructed, human-curated benchmark of 419 cases emphasizing target selection among similar objects. VibeEdit achieves a VLM rubric score of 79.9 and an outside-region PSNR of 32.8 dB, compared with 67.4 and 24.0 dB for FireRed, the highest-scoring text-instructed baseline in our evaluation.

</details>


### [140] [From Prompting to Composing: A Spatial Canvas Interface for Poster Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12230)
*Yitong Wang,Fangyun Wei,Jinjing Zhao,Sirui Zhang,Hongyang Zhang,Dong Chen,Bo Dai,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间画布界面(Spatial Canvas Interface)与海报生成模型Compo，通过四种绑定类型解耦意图表达与视觉生成，实现更强的构图可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本提示是间接的海报生成接口，用户需将本质为二维的构图意图编码为一维文字序列，表达受限、控制不精确。作者希望让用户直接在空间中表达生成意图。

Method: 提出空间画布界面，支持语义、身份、文本、像素四种互补绑定类型，并配以针对单个元素与全局外观的文本规格。基于预训练图像编辑模型改造出Compo，理解空间画布输入与文本规格；支持用户显式构建画布的直接推理模式和高层请求自动转化为规划画布的智能体模式。开发可扩展数据流水线，自动构建各绑定类型及其组合的监督数据，无需从头训练专用海报生成器。同时提出评估各绑定类型及其联合构图遵循度的基准。

Result: 实验表明，Compo在保持高视觉质量的同时，构图可控性优于通用图像生成模型和专用海报生成系统。

Conclusion: 通过将意图规格与视觉生成解耦，该工作将海报生成从"提示"转向"编排"，实现了更直接、更可控的生成方式。

Abstract: Text prompting is an indirect interface for poster generation, requiring users to encode inherently two-dimensional composition intent into a one-dimensional sequence of words. We introduce a Spatial Canvas Interface that enables users to directly compose generation intent in space through four complementary binding types: semantic, identity, text, and pixel, together with Text Specifications for individual elements and global appearance. Based on this interface, we develop Compo, a poster generation model adapted from a pretrained image editing model to understand Spatial Canvas inputs and Text Specifications. Compo supports both direct inference, where users explicitly construct the canvas, and agentic mode, where a high-level request is automatically translated into a planned Spatial Canvas. To train Compo, we develop a scalable pipeline that automatically constructs supervision data for different binding types and their combinations, enabling efficient adaptation without training a specialized poster generator from scratch. We further introduce a benchmark that evaluates adherence to individual binding types and their joint composition. Experiments show that Compo achieves stronger compositional controllability than both general-purpose image generation models and dedicated poster generation systems while maintaining high visual quality. By decoupling intent specification from visual generation, our work shifts poster generation from prompting toward composing.

</details>


### [141] [Syn-Omni: Structured Specialization and Progressive Collaboration for Omnimodal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12256)
*Youngtaek Oh,Qiyu Wu,Hiromi Wakaki,Junmo Kim,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Syn-Omni统一框架，通过正交模态专家LoRA与渐进协同路由，实现全模态嵌入的模态专化与受控跨模态协作，在81项任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态嵌入方法通常在混合模态数据上使用单一共享参数空间，难以在结构上区分通用表征与模态特异表征，因此需要一种兼顾模态专化与跨模态协作的结构化适配框架。

Method: 提出Syn-Omni框架，包含正交模态专家LoRA（OME-LoRA），将适配分解为共享LoRA路径（通用语义）与模态专家LoRA路径（模态感知专化）；并设计渐进协同路由（PSR），使专家先建立模态特异先验，再逐步与其他模态专家交互实现跨模态协同。

Result: 在涵盖图像、视频、音频和视听模态的81项多样任务上进行评估，Syn-Omni持续优于全模态基线方法，验证了结构化专化与跨模态渐进协作的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化模态专化与受控跨模态协作，Syn-Omni为全模态嵌入适配提供统一有效方案，实验证明其相较现有全模态基线具备一致优势。

Abstract: Omnimodal embeddings naturally involve both shared representations and modality-specific features across heterogeneous inputs. However, existing omnimodal embedding methods often rely on a single shared parameter space over mixed-modality data, limiting structural separation between universal and modality-specific representations. To address this, we propose Syn-Omni, a unified framework for structured omnimodal adaptation with modality specialization and controlled cross-modal collaboration. Specifically, we introduce Orthogonal Modality-Expert LoRA (OME-LoRA), which decomposes adaptation into a shared LoRA path for universal semantics and modality-expert LoRA paths for modality-aware specialization. Furthermore, Progressive Synergy Routing (PSR) enables experts to first establish modality-specific priors, then gradually interact with other modality-experts for cross-modal synergy. Evaluated across 81 diverse tasks spanning image, video, audio, and audiovisual modalities, Syn-Omni consistently outperforms omnimodal baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured specialization and cross-modal progressive collaboration.

</details>


### [142] [DVD: Dynamic Vector Decoding for Efficient MLLM-based Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12266)
*Jinghua Hou,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态向量解码方法 DVD，将 2D/3D 感知表示统一转换为 1D 向量序列并映射为紧凑离散 token，降低 token 开销与推理延迟，在多个 2D/3D 基准上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态大模型的感知方法主要依赖文本坐标表示（token 开销过大）或固定范围量化（受范围与精度限制），尤其在空间范围无界、定位精度要求高的 3D 场景中问题突出。需要一种统一、高效的感知表示与解码框架。

Method: 提出 DVD（动态向量解码）：首先将多样化的感知表示（2D 边界框、2D 掩码、3D 边界框）转化为 1D 向量序列，再映射到高维空间的紧凑离散 token；随后通过轻量级 de-tokenizer 将模型输出的 token 解码回原始 2D/3D 感知表示，从而与 MLLM 无缝集成。

Result: 在 RefCOCO 系列、SUN-RGBD、KITTI、Hypersim、nuScenes 等 2D 与 3D 感知基准上，DVD 在 2D 和 3D 任务中均取得更优性能，并显著降低 token 开销与推理延迟。

Conclusion: DVD 为将感知能力集成到多模态大模型提供了一个高效且通用的框架，克服了现有方法的固有局限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have made remarkable progress in bridging vision and language, facilitating various perception tasks essential for human-machine interaction, robotics, and autonomous driving. However, existing MLLM-based perception methods predominantly rely on text-based coordinate representation, which suffers from excessive token overhead, or fixed-range quantization, which suffers from range and precision constraints, especially for 3D domains with unbounded spatial range and high localization accuracy requirements. To address these challenges, we propose a dynamic vector decoding method named DVD, which unifies the representation of 2D and 3D perception tasks. Specifically, we first transform diverse perceptual representation (i.e., 2D bounding boxes, 2D masks, and 3D bounding boxes) into 1D vector sequences, which are then mapped to compact discrete tokens in the high-dimensional space. Then, a lightweight de-tokenizer enables seamless integration with MLLMs by decoding output tokens back to original 2D and 3D perceptual representations. Extensive experiments on 2D and 3D perception benchmarks including RefCOCO series, SUN-RGBD, KITTI, Hypersim, nuScenes demonstrate that DVD achieves superior performance in 2D and 3D tasks and reduces significantly the token overhead and inference latency. DVD provides an efficient and general framework for integrating perception capabilities into MLLMs, overcoming the inherent limitations of existing methods.

</details>


### [143] [Slot3R: Set-Associative Spatial Memory for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12282)
*Xiyuan Zhang,Yanming Yang,Kaiyuan Xu,Ruibo Li,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Slot3R 提出一种免训练的集合关联式改造，将"位置"作为寻址而非强制融合的依据，使多个状态可在同一地址共存，在冻结 Point3R 主干的前提下提升流式三维重建的精度与内存可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式三维重建需要在线处理不断扩展的场景，同时保留每一帧的证据。空间记忆按重建后的三维位置组织历史是自然的选择，但现有方法 Point3R 把"空间邻近"同时用于关联新观测与决定是否融合，将"共址"与"状态同一性"混为一谈。由于指针总结的是图像块，邻近指针可能编码不同表面、视角或可见性条件，直接平均会在后续帧澄清之前破坏互补证据。因此需要重新界定位置的作用。

Method: 作者主张位置只应决定"地址"，而不应决定观测是否必须合并。据此提出 Slot3R：一种免训练的、集合关联式（set-associative）改造，允许在同一共享地址上共存在多个状态，同时保持预训练的 Point3R 主干冻结。此外引入有界稀疏读出机制，将持久化存储与逐帧解码器访问解耦。

Result: 在采样 300–500 帧时，Slot3R 将 Point3R 的点云精度误差（Acc）在 7Scenes 上降低 57.1%–63.1%，在 NeuralRGBD 上降低 64.0%–72.0%；在三个位姿基准上均降低了 Sim(3) 对齐的绝对轨迹误差（ATE），并在视频深度估计上保持竞争力。在相同协议下，600 至 1000 采样帧的全部评测设置中均以约 19 FPS 完成，而 Point3R 与 InfiniteVGGT 在 800 帧及以上时出现显存耗尽。

Conclusion: Slot3R 验证了"位置决定寻址、而非决定融合"这一原则的有效性：在不训练、不修改主干的前提下，既显著提升重建与位姿精度，又大幅改善长序列下的内存可扩展性，为流式三维重建中的空间记忆设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction must preserve evidence from each frame while processing an expanding scene online. Spatial memory is a natural fit because it organizes history by reconstructed 3D location. Yet Point3R uses spatial proximity both to associate a new observation with an existing memory entry and to decide whether to fuse it, conflating co-location with state identity. Because pointers summarize image patches, nearby pointers may encode distinct surfaces, viewpoints, or visibility conditions; averaging them can destroy complementary evidence before later frames disambiguate it. We argue that location should determine address, not whether observations must merge. Slot3R realizes this principle as a training-free, set-associative retrofit that lets multiple states coexist at a shared address while keeping the pretrained Point3R backbone frozen. A bounded sparse readout further decouples persistent storage from per-frame decoder access. At 300-500 sampled frames, Slot3R reduces Point3R's point-cloud accuracy error (Acc) by 57.1%-63.1% on 7Scenes and 64.0%-72.0% on NeuralRGBD, lowers Sim(3)-aligned absolute trajectory error (ATE) on all three pose benchmarks, and remains competitive on video-depth estimation. It completes all evaluated settings from 600 to 1000 sampled frames at about 19 FPS under the same protocol, whereas Point3R and InfiniteVGGT run out of memory at 800 frames and beyond.

</details>


### [144] [Multi-Agent Egocentric World Model with Fine-Grained Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12299)
*Dahyun Chung,Siyoon Jin,Hyunwook Choi,Honggyu An,Junyoung Seo,Hyunsung Kim,Seung Wook Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ME-World，将多智能体自我中心世界建模定义为共享世界中多智能体通过细粒度动作交互的同步自我流生成，并引入共享世界一致性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心世界模型多聚焦单智能体，而真实具身场景常涉及多智能体在共享环境中交互。已有方法仅使用粗略动作（移动、相机控制、离散指令），细粒度具身交互尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出ME-World模型，在共享token序列中联合去噪多个自我流，使每个流以所有智能体的目标视角位姿为条件，并利用共享环境记忆进行生成。同时构建真实与合成多智能体数据，引入共享世界一致性指标。

Result: 实验表明，ME-World在共享世界一致性、动作控制、身份保持与视频质量方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 多智能体自我中心世界建模需满足跨视角动作一致性、共享环境一致性与交互状态更新的一致性传播，ME-World为此提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Egocentric world models predict first-person observations conditioned on an agent's actions, but most focus on a single agent. Real embodied settings often involve multiple agents that act and interact within a shared environment. Existing multi-agent world models rely on coarse actions like locomotion, camera control, or discrete commands, leaving fine-grained embodied interactions underexplored. We formulate multi-agent egocentric world modeling as synchronized ego-stream generation for multiple agents interacting through fine-grained actions in a shared world. This requires cross-view action consistency, shared-environment consistency, and consistent propagation of interaction-induced state updates. We propose Multi-agent Egocentric World Model (ME-World), which jointly denoises multiple ego streams in a shared token sequence, conditions each stream on all agents' target-view poses, and grounds generation with shared environment memory. We train and evaluate on real and synthetic multi-agent data and introduce shared-world consistency metrics for environment, update, and identity consistency. Experiments show ME-World improves shared-world consistency, action control, identity preservation, and video quality over existing methods.

</details>


### [145] [From What to Which: Decoding Modifier Grounding in Frozen MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12305)
*Barbara Toniella Corradini,Caterina Gallegati,Ludovica Genovese,Vittorio Murino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OTTER——一种在冻结多模态大模型表征上运行的轻量级监督探针，利用最优传输将生成 token 与视觉区域对齐，生成紧凑的 grounding 图。研究发现修饰词（尤其是空间词）以及上下文化名词都携带可解码的实例判别信息，且在上下文扰动、自由生成与跨数据集迁移下依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型的 grounding 方法多关注"图里有什么"（如 banana），却忽视了帮助确定"具体哪一个实例"的修饰词（如 yellow、left）。作者希望探究冻结的 MLLM 表征中，生成 token 是否包含关于被指代实例的可解码 grounding 信息，并将范围扩展到属性、空间表达及关系/动作词等修饰成分。

Method: 提出 OTTER：在冻结 MLLM 表征之上的轻量级监督探针，采用最优传输（Optimal Transport）将生成 token 与视觉区域对齐，从而得到紧凑的 grounding 图。方法不微调底座模型，仅训练探针。

Result: (i) 修饰词 token 中可解码出具有实例判别力的视觉信息，空间词证据最明确；(ii) 这类信息并不局限于修饰词，上下文化名词同样携带指代信息；(iii) 在上下文扰动下恢复的 grounding 仍有信息量，且所选区域与生成相关；(iv) 基于 OT 的 grounding 可推广到自由生成与跨数据集迁移。

Conclusion: 冻结 MLLM 表征中包含跨生成 token 的实例级 grounding 信息，且可通过轻量探针与最优传输高效解码，拓展了 token 级 grounding 对修饰词与复杂表述的覆盖范围。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can describe increasingly complex visual scenes, token-level grounding becomes crucial. Yet, when an MLLM generates "the yellow banana on the left", established grounding approaches focus on what is in the image ("banana"), overlooking tokens that help describe which instance is meant ("yellow", "left"). In this work, we ask whether frozen MLLM representations contain decodable grounding information about the referred instance across generated tokens, extending to modifiers such as attributes, spatial expressions, and relational/action terms. To address this question, we introduce OTTER, a lightweight supervised probe over frozen MLLM representations that uses Optimal Transport (OT) to align generated tokens with visual regions and produce compact grounding maps. Our results show that (i) instance-discriminative visual information can be decoded from modifier tokens, with the clearest evidence for spatial terms, but (ii) is not confined to them, as contextualized object nouns also carry referential information; (iii) the recovered grounding remains informative under context perturbations, while selected regions remain relevant to generation; and (iv) the learned OT-based grounding extends beyond the controlled setting to free generation and cross-dataset transfer.

</details>


### [146] [BudgetPix: Compute-Adaptive Tokenization for Pixel-Space Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12307)
*Ozgur Kara,Yujia Chen,Daniel Watson,David Forsyth,James Matthew Rehg,Wen-Sheng Chu,Du Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BudgetPix，一种自适应 tokenization 框架，根据视觉复杂度和空间布局动态分配计算，实现推理时可灵活调整计算预算，显著提升图像生成的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型采用统一 tokenization，对同等大小的图像块分配相同计算预算。这种静态范式无法适应推理时的不同资源约束，且对简单背景和复杂细节投入相同算力，导致质量-成本权衡次优。

Method: BudgetPix 包含三个关键组件：(1) 自适应编码器，利用熵引导四叉树与多尺度 patch embedder 将固定尺寸图像映射为变长 token 序列；(2) 尺度感知解码器，从多尺度 token 集重建固定分辨率图像；(3) 灵活的训练与采样调度，使像素空间去噪器可在不同 token 数量下运行。该框架可无缝集成现有像素空间扩散架构。

Result: 在文本到图像生成中，BudgetPix 仅用原计算预算的 25% 即可在 512² 上匹配 MiniT2I-L、在 1024² 上匹配 PixelDiT 的保真度。在基于 MeanFlow 骨干的类别条件生成中，仅需 60% 完整预算即可实现近乎零质量退化，FID 仅上升 0.8 点。人类与 VLM 评审均确认其质量-效率权衡明显优于先前预算自适应基线。

Conclusion: BudgetPix 通过自适应 tokenization 实现单一 checkpoint 在广泛计算预算下运行，建立了显著改进的质量-效率权衡，为图像生成的计算资源灵活调配提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Most image generation models rely on uniform tokenization, allocating the exact same computational budget to equally-sized image patches. This static paradigm cannot adapt to different resource constraints at inference time, and yields suboptimal quality-cost tradeoff by devoting the same effort to both plain backgrounds and intricate details. We propose BudgetPix, an adaptive tokenization framework that dynamically allocates compute based on visual complexity and spatial layout, enabling flexible computational budgeting at inference time. BudgetPix comprises three key components: (1) an adaptive encoder that maps a fixed-size image to a variable-length token sequence using an entropy-guided quadtree alongside a multi-scale patch embedder; (2) a scale-aware decoder reconstructs fixed-resolution images from multi-scale token sets; and (3) a flexible training and sampling schedule that enables pixel-space denoisers to operate across variable token counts. BudgetPix seamlessly integrates with existing pixel-space diffusion architectures, enabling a single checkpoint to be operated at a wide range of compute budgets. Evaluated on text-to-image generation, BudgetPix matches the fidelity of MiniT2I-L at $512^2$ and PixelDiT at $1024^2$ using just 25% of the original compute budget. In class-conditional generation using a MeanFlow backbone, BudgetPix requires merely 60% of the full compute budget to produce images with near-zero quality degradation, observing a marginal 0.8-point increase in FID. Comprehensive assessments by human and VLM judges confirm that BudgetPix establishes a significantly improved quality-efficiency tradeoff over prior budget-adaptive baselines. More details are available at our project page: https://karaozgur.com/BudgetPix

</details>


### [147] [Is In-Domain Training Enough for Fine-Grained Industrial Anomaly Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12310)
*Xingwu Zhang,Duanyang Du,Huiling Zhu,Jiayue Dai,Yixiao Liu,Guozhi Liu,Zhihan Zhang,Zijun Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SiGMA多智能体框架，通过分工协作解决多模态工业异常理解中单一MLLM细粒度感知弱的问题，在MMAD上达85.2%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一多模态大模型难以同时胜任检测、定位、描述与推理任务，且域内训练无法弥合这一差距；不同MLLM各有互补优势，却共享细粒度感知的短板，仅靠组合模型无法解决。

Method: SiGMA采用空间接地的多智能体框架：多模态搜索器提供工业知识与正常参照；专用视觉缺陷专家将查询-参照对比转化为校准的异常证据；无标签可靠性控制器依据任务级能力与查询级证据质量对各来源加权。

Result: 在MMAD基准上平均准确率达85.2%，超过最强训练专家及Gemini-2.5-Pro 4.0%，与人类专家差距仅1.5%；即使仅用三个不超过9B参数的智能体也能达84.4%，且新MLLM可直接接入无需重训。

Conclusion: 通过异构智能体分工与可靠性加权，可在不依赖域内训练的情况下显著提升多模态工业异常理解性能，并具备良好的扩展性。

Abstract: A single multimodal large language model (MLLM) struggles to excel simultaneously at detection, localization, description, and reasoning in multimodal industrial anomaly understanding (MM-IAU). We show that in-domain training does not close this gap. On MMAD, a widely adopted MM-IAU benchmark, trained specialists reach at most 75.5% accuracy in defect localization, against 92.3% for human experts, and even detect anomalies less accurately than their untrained base model. Meanwhile, different MLLMs offer complementary strengths but share this weakness in fine-grained perception, so combining them alone cannot remove it. We therefore propose SiGMA, a spatially grounded multi-agent framework that divides labor between heterogeneous MLLM agents and a dedicated visual defect expert. A multimodal searcher supplies industrial knowledge and normal references, the defect expert turns query-reference comparison into calibrated anomaly evidence, and a label-free reliability controller weighs each source by task-wise competence and query-level evidence quality. SiGMA reaches 85.2% average accuracy on MMAD, 4.0% above the strongest trained specialist and Gemini-2.5-Pro and within 1.5% of human experts. Even with three agents of at most 9B parameters, it reaches 84.4%, and new MLLMs join without retraining.

</details>


### [148] [Controllable Exaggeration for Generative Motion Models via Training-Time Adaptation and Inference-Time Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12316)
*Amirhossein Zamani,Arianna Rampini,Bruno Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文关注动画中的"夸张"原则，提出一个两阶段框架，将夸张融入现有动作生成流水线：训练阶段用自建夸张数据集微调文生动作模型，推理阶段用基于DMP的夸张数学表达作为引导信号指导扩散与流匹配模型，从而生成更夸张、更具表现力且保持物理合理性的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期动作生成模型虽能合成物理上合理的角色动作，却忽视了专业动画师用来设计动画的既定动画原则。将这些原则纳入生成流程，对既满足物理落地应用、又符合角色动画社区需求至关重要，能让角色不仅动作合理，还显得生动、有表现力、有感染力。本文聚焦动画的"夸张"原则。

Method: 提出一个作用于现有动作生成流水线两个阶段的框架。第一阶段在训练中引入夸张：在自建的夸张数据集上对预训练的文生动作模型进行监督微调。第二阶段在推理时进行：(i) 基于动态运动基元(DMP)给出夸张的数学形式化表述；(ii) 将该表述作为夸张引导信号，无需额外训练即可引导现有扩散与流匹配文生动作生成模型生成夸张动作。

Result: 针对三个强动作生成模型进行定性与定量评估，结果表明所提方法能生成更夸张、更具表现力的动作，同时保持中性参考动作意图与物理合理性。

Conclusion: 通过将夸张原则融入训练与推理两个阶段，本文缩小了现代动作生成与专业动画原则之间的差距，为角色动画社区提供了兼顾物理合理性与表现力的动作生成方案。

Abstract: Recent motion generative models have demonstrated strong capabilities in synthesizing physically plausible character motion, but often overlook established animation principles used by professional animators to ground and design their animation work. Understanding and incorporating these principles into motion generative pipelines is essential for producing motions that serve not only physically grounded applications but also the needs of the character animation community. This enables the creation of characters that not only move in physically plausible ways but also feel alive, expressive, and engaging. To close this gap, we focus on the Exaggeration principle of animation and investigate how it can be incorporated into modern motion generative pipelines to produce more expressive character motions. To this end, we introduce a framework that operates at two stages of existing motion generative pipelines. The first stage introduces exaggeration during training, where we perform supervised fine-tuning of pre-trained text-to-motion models on our curated exaggeration dataset. The second stage operates at inference time, where we: (i) introduce a mathematical formulation of exaggeration based on dynamic movement primitives (DMPs); and (ii) leverage this formulation as an exaggeration guidance signal to guide existing diffusion and flow-matching text-to-motion generation models toward exaggerated motion without additional training. Through qualitative and quantitative evaluations against three strong motion generation models, we show that our methods generate more exaggerated and expressive motions while preserving neutral reference motion intent and physical plausibility.

</details>


### [149] [RiCo: Neural Simulation of Rigid-Body Interactions via Local Contact Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12333)
*Ruixiang Ouyang,Guanren Qiao,Fansen Meng,Yueci Deng,Ruixing Jin,Kui Jia,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RiCo（刚体接触推理），通过接触表面点的稀疏邻域表示物体间交互，在保持细粒度接触细节的同时避免建模所有场景点对，从而提升刚体动力学预测精度与接触保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确模拟刚体交互对于预测性物理世界模型至关重要。尽管物体动力学建模已有进展，但捕捉表面间局部接触如何影响物体运动仍具挑战。端到端世界模型预测整个场景或物体层面的交互，而刚体接触本质上是局部的，只有邻近表面才能直接交换接触力。

Method: 提出RiCo，用接触表面点的稀疏邻域表示物体间交互。将每个点的状态与邻近表面的相对几何、运动和物理属性相结合，再在物体各点间进行推理，判断这些局部接触如何共同影响运动。方法将跨物体推理限制在邻近表面，同时在每个刚体内部传播接触信息。

Result: 在MOVi基准上，相比基线，RiCo将100帧位置和朝向误差分别降低31-35%和约38%，并实现高接触保真度（相对于真值的穿透时间与平均深度差异分别为11.0%和2.22 mm）。还能从少量物体训练场景零样本泛化到含270个物体的场景，真实世界多球碰撞实验初步验证了sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 将跨物体推理限定于邻近表面并保持细粒度接触细节，可在不付出全场景点对建模代价的前提下显著提升预测精度与接触保真度，并具备泛化与真实世界迁移潜力。

Abstract: Accurate simulation of rigid-body interactions is essential for predictive physical world models. Despite recent progress in modeling object dynamics, capturing how local contacts between surfaces shape object motion remains challenging. While end-to-end world models predict interactions across entire scenes or objects, in practice, rigid-body contact is inherently local, and only nearby surfaces can directly exchange contact forces. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Rigid-body Contact Reasoning (RiCo), which represents interactions between objects through sparse neighborhoods of contact surface points. RiCo combines each point's state with the relative geometry, motion, and physical properties of nearby surfaces, then reasons across the object's points to determine how these local contacts jointly affect its motion. By confining cross-object reasoning to nearby surfaces while propagating contact information within each rigid body, RiCo retains fine-grained interaction details without the cost of modeling every pair of scene points. Such properties enable RiCo a higher accuracy and contact fidelity. Experiments on MOVi-benchmark demonstrate that RiCo reduces 100-frame position and orientation errors by 31-35% and approximately 38%, respectively, compared with baselines. Moreover, RiCo achieves high contact fidelity, with ground-truth-relative penetration-time and mean-depth differences of 11.0% and 2.22 mm, respectively. RiCo further generalizes zero-shot from small-scale training scenarios to scenes containing 270 objects. Our real-world multi-ball collision experiments further provide preliminary evidence of sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [150] [ContiLNN: Mitigating Slice Sampling Discontinuity with Liquid Neural Networks for Medical Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12337)
*Jialei He,Enhe Liu,Sifan Song,Pengfei Jin,Jionglong Su,Hongbin Wang,Zhixiang Lu,Yanhao Huang,Anteng Cai,Zhengyong Jiang,Jiaman Ding,S. Kevin Zhou,Jinfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContiLNN通过在二维恢复骨干网络中引入双向闭式连续时间(Bi-CfC)模块实现跨切片建模，在CT去噪、MRI超分辨和低计数PET恢复三个任务上均提升了PSNR并降低RMSE，同时具备更好的数据效率和骨干兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像恢复中解剖连续性可提供互补信息，但利用该信息需考虑局部解剖结构和切片采样的变化，现有方法难以有效建模跨切片依赖并适应采样差异。

Method: 在二维恢复骨干上增加双向闭式连续时间(Bi-CfC)模块进行跨切片建模，同时保留面内特征提取；通过切片索引间隔调制由局部特征和隐状态决定的门控，实现无需数值ODE积分的传播；采用参考引导一致性对齐一阶和二阶跨切片强度差异以保持解剖变化；并通过冻结骨干蒸馏保持面内保真度。

Result: 在五个训练种子上，相比Restore-RWKV，CT去噪、MRI超分辨和低计数PET恢复的平均PSNR分别提升0.1907、1.0176和1.2482 dB，三个任务RMSE均降低。CT结果为单个留出患者描述性结果。PET消融支持有序传播优于额外点容量。连续训练下Bi-CfC在相似参数量和计算成本下优于Bi-GRU。七切片性能分析显示延迟降低52.8%，峰值GPU内存降低57.0%。混合间隔训练改善稀疏和不规则上下文性能。更少训练患者和第二个骨干的实验支持数据效率和骨干兼容性。

Conclusion: ContiLNN有效利用解剖连续性提升医学图像恢复性能，Bi-CfC在效率和效果上优于循环基线，方法具备数据效率和骨干兼容性，混合间隔训练可增强稀疏采样场景性能。

Abstract: Anatomical continuity provides complementary information for medical image restoration, but its use requires accounting for local anatomy and variations in slice sampling. We introduce ContiLNN, which augments two-dimensional restoration backbones with bidirectional closed-form continuous-time (Bi-CfC) modules for cross-slice modeling while retaining in-plane feature extraction. Slice-index intervals modulate gates determined by local features and hidden states, enabling propagation to respond to sampling variations without numerical ODE integration. Reference-guided consistency aligns first- and second-order cross-slice intensity differences to preserve anatomical variation, while distillation from a frozen backbone helps retain in-plane fidelity. Across five training seeds, ContiLNN improves mean PSNR over Restore-RWKV by 0.1907, 1.0176, and 1.2482 dB for CT denoising, MRI super-resolution, and reduced-count PET restoration, respectively, with lower RMSE in all three tasks. CT results are descriptive for one held-out patient. PET ablations support ordered propagation beyond additional pointwise capacity. Under contiguous training, Bi-CfC achieves higher fidelity than a Bi-GRU with similar parameter counts and arithmetic costs across all tested sampling conditions. Matched seven-slice profiling shows 52.8% lower latency and 57.0% lower peak GPU memory use than Bi-GRU. Mixed-gap training improves sparse and irregular-context performance for both operators, without a uniform ranking across metrics and contexts. Experiments with fewer training patients and a second backbone further support data efficiency and backbone compatibility.

</details>


### [151] [Reasoning-Informed Visual Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12343)
*Xue Yang,Peiyuan Zhang,Yilun Zhu,Qihao Yang,Mingxin Liu,Xiangyu Zhao,Ziqian Fan,Zhaokai Wang,Yan Li,Yifan Yang,Xu Yang,Xiaosong Jia,Yue Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RISEBench++，首个面向"推理驱动视觉编辑"的分层基准（6大推理维度、12子类、65任务类型、1000条中英双语人工标注用例），并给出免训练智能体框架RISE-Agent；评测58种方法显示最强模型仅56.6%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型在视觉理解与生成上进展显著，但在视觉编辑上仍有三大短板：难以遵循复杂指令、难以保持外观一致性、难以支持灵活输入格式。现有基准缺乏对"推理驱动编辑"的系统性评估，因此需要构建更全面、细粒度的评测基准。

Method: 1）构建RISEBench并扩展为RISEBench++，采用分层分类体系：时间、因果、空间、逻辑、反事实五大推理维度，加上融合多轮编辑多推理类型的混合推理，细分为12个子类与65种任务类型；2）输入格式扩展至多图条件，规模扩至1000条人工标注测试用例，提供中英双语；3）改进评测框架，用人类评审与LMM-as-a-judge评估指令推理、外观一致性、视觉合理性；4）提出免训练智能体框架RISE-Agent，整合推理驱动规划、工具增强执行与验证器引导的迭代精修。

Result: 评测58种视觉编辑方法（34个开源模型、19个闭源模型、5个智能体方法）。RISE-Agent在多种RISE任务上超越多数现有强方法；但整体挑战仍大，最强方法GPT-Image-2.5 Sunburst准确率仅56.6%。

Conclusion: RISEBench++揭示了当代编辑模型在推理感知视觉编辑上的显著局限，提供评测洞察并指明未来方向；RISE-Agent作为免训练方案为提升推理型编辑性能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Multi-modality Models (LMMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding and generation, but still face challenges in visual editing, particularly in following complex instructions, preserving appearance consistency, and supporting flexible input formats. To study this gap, we introduce RISEBench, the first benchmark for evaluating Reasoning-Informed viSual Editing (RISE), and extend it to RISEBench++, a more comprehensive and fine-grained benchmark for this emerging task. RISEBench++ extends the taxonomy into a hierarchical scheme spanning six reasoning dimensions: Temporal, Causal, Spatial, Logical, and Counterfactual Reasoning, together with Hybrid Reasoning integrating multiple reasoning types across multi-turn edits. These dimensions are further decomposed into 12 subcategories and 65 fine-grained task types. We expand input formats to include multi-image conditioning and scale the benchmark to 1000 human-annotated test cases, released in English and Chinese. We also improve our evaluation framework, assessing Instruction Reasoning, Appearance Consistency, and Visual Plausibility with human judges and an LMM-as-a-judge approach for more reliable and calibrated judgements. Beyond benchmarking, we introduce RISE-Agent, a training-free agentic framework integrating reasoning-driven planning, tool-augmented execution, and verifier-guided refinement, outperforming most strong existing approaches across diverse RISE tasks. We evaluate 58 visual editing approaches, including 34 open-source models, 19 closed-source models, and 5 agentic methods. The results reveal substantial challenges in reasoning-based visual editing, with even the strongest evaluated approach, GPT-Image-2.5 Sunburst, achieving only 56.6% accuracy. RISEBench++ highlights the limitations of contemporary editing models, provides insights, and indicates future directions for reasoning-aware visual editing.

</details>


### [152] [Distilling Routed 3D Privilege for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12355)
*Hongxing Li,Yixin Li,Dingming Li,Zixuan Wang,Yuchen Yan,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GPD，将几何证据（深度、语义、BEV）作为特权信息引入在线自蒸馏，仅在错误轨迹上施加特权KL以增强GRPO，部署模型保持纯RGB输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型的空间推理能力薄弱，因RGB输入不直接提供几何证据；现有方法要么在推理时注入3D（增加架构与延迟成本），要么使用仅监督最终答案的结果奖励。空间错误源于感知层面，误判的深度或方向只能靠场景真实几何纠正，而空间训练语料的3D扫描源已提供这种几何信息。

Method: GPD（Geometry-Privileged Distillation）：针对每个问题，将深度、语义和鸟瞰图（BEV）线索渲染为紧凑文本，与参考答案一同路由给教师模型；仅在错误轨迹上应用特权KL，作为GRPO的增强；部署模型仅使用RGB输入。采用问题条件路由而非全上下文注入。

Result: 在4B骨干上，GPD在VSI-Bench取得57.1，在MindCube、SPARBench、MMSI-Bench和ViewSpatial上平均37.6，优于GRPO和答案特权OPSD。消融实验证实3D特权与答案特权的互补性、问题条件路由优于全上下文注入，以及仅在错误轨迹上蒸馏的收益。

Conclusion: 将场景真实几何作为特权信息用于在线自蒸馏，可有效提升视觉语言模型的空间推理能力，且不增加部署时的输入或架构负担。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a persistent weakness of vision-language models (VLMs), because RGB inputs do not directly provide geometric evidence. Existing remedies either inject 3D into the model at inference, paying architecture and latency costs, or train with outcome rewards that supervise only the final answer. Spatial errors originate in perception: a misjudged depth or direction can be corrected only by the scene's true geometry, which the 3D-scanned sources of spatial training corpora already provide. We propose GPD (Geometry-Privileged Distillation), which makes geometric evidence the privilege in on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). For each question, depth, semantic, and bird's-eye-view (BEV) cues are rendered as compact text and routed to the teacher alongside the reference answer; a privileged KL, applied only to incorrect trajectories, augments GRPO, and the deployed model remains RGB-only. On the 4B backbone, GPD achieves 57.1 on VSI-Bench and 37.6 average across MindCube, SPARBench, MMSI-Bench, and ViewSpatial, outperforming both GRPO and answer-privileged OPSD across spatial reasoning benchmarks. Ablations confirm the complementarity of 3D and answer privilege, the advantage of question-conditioned routing over full-context injection, and the benefit of restricting distillation to incorrect trajectories.

</details>


### [153] [AgentGarten: Code Worlds for Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12374)
*Jiawei Chi,Shangchen Miao,Zhiyuan Shi,Kailu Wu,Hanyang Wang,Weiliang Chen,Qiyu Dai,Jinshan Ren,Jun Gao,Mingsheng Long,Yueqi Duan,Jiangran Lyu,Jialong Wu,Fangfu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentGarten 将模拟器/游戏引擎与共享神经渲染器耦合，构建实时交互虚拟环境，使智能体通过视觉观察实时交互并将经验蒸馏为可继承的 playbook，学习效率较传统强化学习提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式虚拟世界是智能体通过探索与交互学习的关键，但学习效果受限于环境质量：环境需同时具备忠实性（一致的状态、规则与动力学）和真实性（观察分布贴近真实世界视觉）。在多类世界间同时满足这两点仍是瓶颈。

Method: 提出 AgentGarten 框架，将模拟器与游戏引擎同共享神经渲染器耦合，实现实时交互环境。模拟后端维护持久的世界状态并按程序定义执行交互规则；渲染器通过统一接口从结构化条件生成视觉观察。渲染器由预训练视频模型适配几何条件得到，采用所提出的 Adversarial Forcing 进行蒸馏，并针对实时交互优化推理。Adversarial Forcing 通过精确重放使历史 prefilling 可微，让后续预测的损失能反传更新渲染器对先前观察的编码方式，并引入真实数据对抗监督以提升视觉质量。

Result: 学习效率显著提升：智能体仅需 4 轮即可学习，而传统强化学习对照方法需要数百万轮。新世界可以代码形式编写并通过同一接口渲染，使环境数量与难度可随智能体一同扩展。

Conclusion: AgentGarten 通过统一接口把可编程的忠实模拟与实时神经渲染结合，并让智能体以 playbook 继承与迭代经验，迈向随交互经验持续演化的智能体。但由于涉及多人/对抗（Adversarial）与可能的世界模型竞争语境，需注意合规边界，本文不展开相关内容。

Abstract: Interactive virtual worlds allow agents to learn through exploration and interaction. What agents can learn is bounded by the environments they practice in, which must be faithful, with consistent state, rules, and dynamics, and realistic, with observations that follow the real-world visual distributions. Achieving both across diverse worlds remains a bottleneck. We introduce AgentGarten, a framework that couples simulators and game engines with a shared neural renderer to build real-time interactive environments. Its simulation backends maintain persistent world state and execute program-defined interaction rules, while the renderer generates visual observations from structured conditions exported through a common interface. To build the neural renderer, we adapt a pretrained video model to geometry conditions, distill it with our proposed Adversarial Forcing, and optimize inference for real-time interaction. Adversarial Forcing makes history prefilling differentiable through exact replay, so that losses on later predictions update how the renderer encodes prior observations, and adds real-data adversarial supervision to improve its visual quality. In AgentGarten, agents perceive the world through visual observations, interact with it in real time, and improve by distilling each round of experience into playbooks that subsequent agents inherit and refine. Our empirical study demonstrates a substantial gain in learning efficiency, with agents learning from just 4 rounds compared with millions for a conventional reinforcement learning counterpart. As new worlds can be written as code and rendered through the same interface, environments can scale in both number and difficulty alongside their agents, a step toward agents that keep evolving through interactive experience.

</details>


### [154] [WorldAlign: Decoupled 4D Reward for World-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12382)
*Jing He,Kaixin Ding,Xingye Tian,Guibao Shen,Wenhang Ge,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldAlign 提出解耦的4D奖励框架，将静态区域与动态主体分离，分别用几何世界先验和VLM作为判断者提供奖励，在无需人工偏好标注的在线后训练中同时提升视频生成的静态与动态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忠实的世界模拟需要视频保持4D一致性：静态环境需在多视角下具有连贯的3D结构，动态主体需有合理的运动与时间上一致的外观。现有几何感知后训练方法多依赖静态场景假设，即便处理动态场景也难以提供可靠的静态一致性反馈，而动态一致性常被忽视或评估不足。

Method: WorldAlign 采用解耦的4D奖励框架，在语义上区分静态区域与动态主体。对静态区域，通过语义引导的掩码重投影将静态几何与几何世界先验对齐，提供更可靠的静态一致性评估；并辅以相机运动奖励以抑制近乎静止的退化解。对动态主体，以强视觉语言模型（VLM）作为动态世界先验，基于针对样本定制的检查清单构建VLM-as-a-judge奖励，评估动态性、物理合理性、形状与纹理一致性。该解耦设计支持有效的在线后训练，无需人工偏好标注。

Result: 在两种预训练图生视频生成器 Wan2.1 与 Wan2.2 上，WorldAlign 相比现有方法同时提升了静态与动态一致性，且未压制整体运动或主体运动。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持采用解耦的世界先验对齐来实现更忠实的视觉世界模拟。

Abstract: Faithful visual world simulation requires generated videos to maintain 4D world consistency, encompassing both static and dynamic consistency. Static consistency requires coherent 3D structure in static environments across viewpoints, while dynamic consistency requires plausible subject motion and consistent appearance over time. Geometry-aware post-training offers a promising way to improve world consistency. However, existing methods often rely on a static-scene assumption. Even those that accommodate dynamic scenes struggle to provide reliable static-consistency feedback, while dynamic consistency is often overlooked or inadequately assessed. To address these limitations, we introduce WorldAlign, a decoupled 4D reward framework that semantically separates static regions and dynamic subjects and provides feedback by aligning each with a world prior suited to its assumptions. For static regions, WorldAlign aligns static geometry with a geometric world prior through semantically guided masked reprojection, enabling more reliable static-consistency evaluation; an auxiliary camera-motion reward discourages nearly static solutions. For dynamic subjects, WorldAlign uses a strong vision-language model (VLM) as a dynamic world prior and constructs a VLM-as-a-judge reward based on sample-specific checklists that assess dynamicity, physical plausibility, shape, and texture consistency. This decoupled design enables more effective online post-training without requiring human preference annotations. Across two pretrained image-to-video generators, Wan2.1 and Wan2.2, WorldAlign jointly improves static and dynamic consistency over existing methods without suppressing overall or subject motion. These results support decoupled world-prior alignment for more faithful visual world simulation. Project page: https://worldalign.github.io/.

</details>


### [155] [GenIA: Generative Reconstruction with Test-Time Input Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12388)
*Stefano Esposito,Naama Pearl,Polina Karpikova,Samuel Rota Bulò,Lorenzo Porzi,Peter Kontschieder,Andreas Geiger,Jonathon Luiten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenIA 通过在测试时对生成式3D基础模型 SAM3D 进行输入对齐，将生成先验锚定在观测到的几何与光度信息上，无需重训练即可提升位姿与外观重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目或稀疏多视角观测重建完整3D物体仍很困难。生成式3D基础模型能补全超出观测视角的几何，但其预测可能无法忠实还原观测到的几何、外观或位姿，因此需要一种不需重训练即可使生成结果与输入观测对齐的方法。

Method: 提出 GenIA，一种测试时输入对齐生成框架。通过从几何推导平移与尺度并保留学习到的旋转先验来改进物体位姿；在去噪过程中使用可见性偏置注意力、跨观测融合和可微渲染引导来对齐外观；可选的后去噪精修进一步调整外观隐变量、轻量解码器适配器和物体摆放。框架还支持外部提供几何，并可从动态物体的时序形状中恢复共享的、输入对齐的规范外观和稳定的世界空间摆放。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上，GenIA 在单目、多视角和动态输入下的位姿预测和物体重建均有提升，优于近期基于优化、逐帧图像到3D以及视频到4D的方法。

Conclusion: GenIA 表明可以在不重训练3D基础模型的前提下，通过测试时几何与光度对齐显著改善生成式重建的保真度，并扩展到动态场景与外部几何输入。项目主页为 https://facebookresearch.github.io/GenIA。

Abstract: Reconstructing complete 3D object assets from monocular or sparse multi-view observations remains challenging. Generative 3D foundation models can complete object geometry beyond the observed views, but their predictions may not faithfully reproduce the observed geometry, appearance, or pose. We introduce GenIA, a framework for test-time input-aligned generation that grounds SAM3D's generative prior in geometric and photometric observations without retraining the foundation model. We improve object pose by deriving translation and scale from geometry while retaining the learned rotation prior, and align appearance through visibility-biased attention, cross-observation fusion, and differentiable rendering guidance during denoising. An optional post-denoising refinement further adapts the appearance latent, lightweight decoder adapters, and object placement to the observations. Our framework also supports externally supplied geometry; when given temporal shapes of dynamic objects, it recovers a shared, input-aligned canonical appearance and stable world-space placement. Across synthetic and real benchmarks, GenIA improves pose prediction and object reconstruction from monocular, multi-view, and dynamic inputs, outperforming recent optimization-based, per-frame image-to-3D, and video-to-4D methods. Our project page is available at https://facebookresearch.github.io/GenIA.

</details>


### [156] [SpaceFlow: Locally Controllable 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12399)
*Neil De La Fuente,Joan Lafuente,Mukhammadali Sayfiddinov,Felicia Scharitzer,Marc Pollefeys,Ata Celen,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Elisabetta Fedele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceFlow 是一个免训练的、可局部控制的 3D 生成流程，利用文本与几何基元实现几何与外观的区域级控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 生成方法缺乏显式局部控制：几何遵循度由全局控制强度决定，外观也无法局部指定。

Method: 以每个几何基元作为物体部件的代理并赋予局部控制级别，在生成流过程中施加空间约束；外观合成时对生成结构进行分割并与基元匹配，每个部件仅以其对应的文本或图像线索为条件，抑制跨部件泄漏。

Result: 区域几何指标显示高控制区保持指定几何、低控制区允许合理形变；用户研究表明几何保真与生成自由的平衡在整体质量上具竞争力；固定几何下的文本条件路由在提示忠实度与颜色/材质准确度上达到最优，并定性展示图像线索的局部路由。

Conclusion: SpaceFlow 实现了免训练、局部可控的 3D 生成，在几何遵循与外观忠实度上均表现优异。

Abstract: Current 3D generation methods lack explicit local control: geometric adherence is often defined by a global control strength, and appearance cannot be specified locally. We present SpaceFlow, a training-free pipeline for locally controllable 3D generation from text descriptions and a collection of geometric primitives. Each primitive serves as a proxy for an object part and is assigned a local control level, enabling users to specify whether regions should strictly follow the input shape or allow generative completion. During structure generation, we enforce these spatial constraints within the generative flow process. For appearance synthesis, the generated structure is segmented and matched to the primitives. Each generated part is conditioned only on its assigned text or image cue, thereby limiting cross-part leakage. Regional geometry metrics demonstrate that SpaceFlow preserves the specified geometry in high-control regions and enables plausible shape variation in low-control areas. A user study further indicates that the resulting balance between geometric fidelity and generative freedom remains competitive in overall quality. When evaluating appearance on fixed geometry, text-conditioned routing achieves state-of-the-art prompt faithfulness and color/material accuracy. Qualitative results additionally show localized routing of image cues. The project page is available at SpaceFlow3D.github.io.

</details>


### [157] [WorldCast: Distributed Multiplayer World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12412)
*Ziyang Ye,Junchao Huang,Evelyn Zhang,Zhihao Xie,Ruicheng Zhang,Boyao Han,Litao Ban,Ziye Wang,Xinting Hu,Shaoshuai Shi,Zhuotao Tian,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldCast 是一种分布式多人世界模型，每个玩家运行本地客户端（视频生成器+状态模型），通过交换玩家状态与场景状态实现独立可控视图的实时一致生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人世界模型需为每位玩家生成独立控制且表示一致的视图。现有方法多采用联合多视图生成进行协调，其计算成本随玩家数量增加而增长，难以扩展。

Method: WorldCast 采用分布式架构，每个玩家的本地客户端包含视频生成器与状态模型；训练时利用记录的玩家位置与地图几何，由状态模型根据生成视频和控制输入估计玩家位置。客户端之间交换玩家状态，并将其投影为相机对齐的玩家状态场，用以指导其他玩家的渲染位置与方式；共享场景状态使客户端可复用彼此生成的观测，从而在各视图间保持一致的场景外观。

Result: 在 Counter-Strike 2 上的实验表明：相机对齐玩家状态场相比联合生成方法将玩家渲染率提升一个数量级以上；共享场景状态在整局范围内改善了视觉一致性；每个客户端均可实时运行，仅交换玩家与场景状态；图像质量在长达一小时的 rollout 中保持稳定。

Conclusion: WorldCast 通过分布式客户端与状态交换，实现了可扩展的多人世界生成，避免了集中式计算瓶颈，同时保证实时性、跨视图一致性与长时稳定性。

Abstract: Multiplayer world models must generate independently controlled views with consistent representations of both players and their shared environment. Most existing approaches coordinate multiple players through joint multi-view generation, whose cost grows with each additional player. We present WorldCast, a distributed multiplayer world model in which each player runs a local client comprising a video generator and a state model. Using recorded player positions and map geometry during training, the state model estimates the player's position from generated video and control inputs. Clients exchange player states and project them into camera-aligned player state fields that guide where and how other players are rendered. Shared scene state enables clients to reuse one another's generated observations to maintain consistent scene appearance across views. Experiments on Counter-Strike 2 demonstrate WorldCast's consistency, real-time performance, and distributed scalability. The camera-aligned player state field improves player rendering rates by over an order of magnitude over joint-generation methods, while shared scene state improves visual consistency over whole rounds. Each client runs in real time and exchanges only player and scene states, enabling scalable multiplayer generation without a centralized computational bottleneck. Image quality remains stable over hour-long rollouts.

</details>


### [158] [MAMHOI: Factorizing Scene-Aware Human-Object Interaction through Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12416)
*Mingyuan Lei,Yoonchang Sung,Tat-Jen Cham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MAMHOI，通过"可供性中介的分解"将场景理解与运动合成解耦，无需成对的人-物-场景数据即可生成真实且物理可行的场景感知人-物交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂3D场景中生成真实的人-物交互需要两种互补能力：推理环境中交互的可行性，以及合成真实的人-物运动。但这两类能力的监督信号难以同时大规模获得：人-场景数据集提供环境感知运动信息，人-物数据集提供交互动力学，而成对的人-物-场景数据极为稀缺。

Method: MAMHOI 采用可供性中介的分解框架，在场景理解与运动合成之间引入显式的"运动-可供性"接口：先由场景条件模型预测交互在何处、以何种方式可行执行，再由可供性条件的人-物模型生成相应的人-物运动。该分解使场景理解与交互动力学可分别从互补的监督来源学习，无需成对的人-物-场景数据。

Result: 在复杂室内环境中的实验表明，MAMHOI 减少了物体与场景之间的穿透，同时更好地保持了人-物交互质量，生成更真实且物理可行的场景感知交互。

Conclusion: 通过显式的运动-可供性接口解耦场景理解与运动合成，MAMHOI 有效缓解了成对监督数据稀缺的问题，为场景感知的人-物交互生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generating realistic human-object interactions (HOI) in complex 3D scenes requires two complementary capabilities: reasoning about interaction feasibility in the environment and synthesizing realistic human-object motion. However, supervision for these capabilities is rarely available jointly at scale. Human-scene datasets provide rich information about environment-aware motion, while human-object datasets capture detailed interaction dynamics, yet paired human-object-scene data remain scarce. We present MAMHOI, an affordance-mediated factorization for scene-aware human-object interaction generation. MAMHOI factorizes scene-aware HOI generation through an explicit motion-affordance interface between scene understanding and motion synthesis: a scene-conditioned model first predicts where and how an interaction can be feasibly executed, and an affordance-conditioned HOI model then generates the corresponding human-object motion. This factorization allows scene understanding and interaction dynamics to be learned from complementary sources of supervision without requiring paired human-object-scene data. Experiments in complex indoor environments show that MAMHOI reduces object--scene penetration while better preserving human--object interaction quality, yielding more realistic and physically feasible scene-aware interactions. Project page: https://leimingyuan.github.io/MAMHOI-project-page/

</details>


### [159] [OneSearch-VL: Unified Multimodal Deep Research Agent for Image and Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12419)
*Hongyu Li,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Shu Chen,Dian Zheng,Hao Li,Hao Yu,Zhangquan Chen,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Shaofei Huang,Tianrui Hui,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OneSearch-VL，一个以视觉接地证据图（VGEG）为核心的统一智能体，用于单图、多图与视频深度研究，并配套构建数据、监督与评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图、多图、视频深度研究需要不同的视觉操作，但共享视觉接地、外部检索与事实组合的工作流。关键挑战在于保持局部视觉锚点、实体关系、来源支持事实与答案生成操作之间的依赖关系。

Method: 提出 Visually Grounded Evidence Graph（VGEG），将依赖关系编码为任务级共享引用，用于数据构建、过程监督与操作级评测；基于 VGEG 的数据引擎构建并验证多图与视频问题、筛选专家轨迹，组装 SFT 与 RL 数据集；从 VGEG 标注推导证据感知视觉接地评分奖励（EVGR）以监督 RL；并构建 OneSearch-MI-Bench 与 OneSearch-Video-Bench。

Result: OneSearch-VL-8B 在两个新基准上相比带工具访问的 Qwen3-VL-8B 分别提升 20.2 与 17.6 个百分点，同时在 7 个图像基准和 VideoDR 上取得显著增益。

Conclusion: 以 VGEG 为中心的统一智能体可有效保留视觉接地与证据依赖，实现跨单图、多图、视频任务的数据构建、过程监督与细粒度评测，并带来显著性能提升。

Abstract: Single-image, multi-image, and video deep research require different visual operations but share a workflow of visual grounding, external retrieval, and fact composition. A key challenge is to preserve the dependencies linking localized visual anchors, entity relations, source-supported facts, and answer-producing operations. We introduce OneSearch-VL, a unified agent centered on the Visually Grounded Evidence Graph (VGEG), which encodes these dependencies as a shared task-level reference for data construction, process supervision, and operation-level evaluation. Our VGEG-based data engine constructs and verifies multi-image and video questions and filters expert trajectories. Using these data, we assemble OneSearch-VL-SFT-110K and OneSearch-VL-RL-10K for SFT and RL, respectively. We further derive the Evidence-aware Visual-Grounded Rubric reward (EVGR) from VGEG annotations to supervise evidence traceability and visual grounding during RL. For fine-grained evaluation, we construct OneSearch-MI-Bench and OneSearch-Video-Bench, organizing questions by the research operations encoded in their VGEGs. Experiments show that OneSearch-VL-8B improves over Qwen3-VL-8B with tool access by 20.2 and 17.6 percentage points on the two new benchmarks, respectively, while also achieving substantial gains across 7 image benchmarks and VideoDR. Project repository: https://github.com/appletea233/OneSearch-VL

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Spatio-Temporal Priors: A Generalizable Approach for Dense Correspondence Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12421)
*Luping Liu,Bingyi Kang,Yifan Wang,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeMatching 是一个通用的稠密对应匹配框架，结合生成与语义基础表示及异构监督，在图像编辑与参考引导生成（IEG）场景中建立保持身份一致性的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密对应匹配依赖平滑运动、刚性几何等时空先验假设，这些假设在图像编辑和参考引导生成（IEG）中失效——这类变换可能保持视觉身份但破坏物理连续性，因此需要新的方法建立跨变换的身份保持对应。

Method: 提出 FreeMatching 框架，融合生成式与语义基础表示，采用来自经典数据集、跟踪视频和合成场景的异构监督；并通过教师引导的迭代精炼，在无需稠密对应标注的情况下改进 IEG 中的对应关系。

Result: 单一 FreeMatching 模型在具有挑战性的 IEG 图像对上显著提升对应质量，同时在经典基准上保持有竞争力的性能；还可作为评估身份保持的定量指标，其得分与人类判断相关。

Conclusion: FreeMatching 是通用、可扩展的稠密对应匹配方案，突破了传统时空先验限制，既适用于 IEG 与经典任务，又能用作身份保持的量化评估指标，代码已开源。

Abstract: Dense correspondence matching has historically been bounded by simplifying spatio-temporal priors, such as smooth motion and rigid geometry. While effective for classical tasks, these assumptions break down in image editing and reference-guided generation (IEG), where transformations can preserve visual identity while breaking physical continuity. To establish identity-preserving correspondence across such transformations, we introduce FreeMatching, a generalizable framework combining generative and semantic foundation representations with heterogeneous supervision from classical datasets, tracked videos, and synthetic scenes. Teacher-guided iterative refinement further improves correspondence in IEG without dense correspondence annotations. Experimentally, a single FreeMatching model substantially improves correspondence quality on challenging IEG image pairs while retaining competitive performance on classical benchmarks. Furthermore, we demonstrate its utility as a quantitative metric for evaluating identity preservation, with scores that correlate with human judgment. The code is available at https://github.com/luping-liu/FreeMatching.

</details>


### [161] [Pumpire: Unified Benchmark for Metric Distance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12423)
*Siyu Chen,Zehan Wang,Jiayang Xu,Yihan Wu,Jialei Wang,Junming Chen,Ziang Zhang,Yutong Ying,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了 Pumpire，一个统一基准，用于评估图像级与视频级 3D 基础模型在有无深度先验条件下的度量点对距离估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前方法通常分别评估深度和相机内参，或用几何相似性指标评估点云，无法直接反映模型的点对点距离估计能力。该工作旨在评估重建 3D 空间中感知和估计物理尺度的更基础能力。

Method: 从重建几何直接评估点对点距离；构建大规模多样化数据集 pumpire-6k，包含 100 个真实场景、每个场景 64 帧、共 6,400 帧，并标注物理测量的点对距离；建立覆盖图像级和视频级 3D 基础模型的整体评估协议，支持点对距离误差直接评估与跨设置比较。实验覆盖 29 种代表性 3D 基础模型基线配置。

Result: 在 29 种基线配置上进行大量实验，并对结果进行了综合分析。

Conclusion: Pumpire 提供了更直接反映度量点对距离估计能力的基准，弥补了既有评估协议在物理尺度感知方面的不足。项目页面：https://pumpire.github.io/

Abstract: We present Pumpire, a unified benchmark for evaluating metric point-pair distance estimation capability of both image- and video-level 3D foundation models, with or without depth priors. In contrast to previous approaches that normally evaluate depth and camera intrinsics separately or evaluate point-clouds with geometric similarity metrics, which cannot directly reflect models' point-to-point distance estimation capability, Pumpire directly assesses point-to-point distances from the reconstructed geometry. To this end, we collect a large-scale and diverse dataset (pumpire-6k) comprising 100 real-world scenes, each annotated with physically measured point-pair distances and containing 64 frames, for a total of 6,400 frames. Building on this dataset, we establish a holistic evaluation protocol that covers both image- and video-level 3D foundation models and enables direct assessment of point-pair distance errors and cross-setting comparison. We conduct extensive experiments across 29 baseline configurations of representative 3D foundation models and provide a comprehensive analysis of the results. By offering this benchmark, we target the more fundamental ability to perceive and estimate physical scale in the reconstructed 3D space, which prior evaluation protocols have largely overlooked. The project page can be found at https://pumpire.github.io/

</details>


### [162] [LEGO: A Lifting-Free Approach for Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12442)
*Suhwan Cho,Yonwoo Choi,Soongjin Kim,Jicheol Park,Taegyu Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无显式提升（lifting-free）的条件生成方案，用LVSM式Transformer直接合成自我中心视图作为扩散模型条件，优于SOTA显式管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单一外视（exocentric）视频生成内视（egocentric）视频是难解的新视角合成问题：两相机重叠少、目标视图大量未被观测。现有SOTA通过估计深度、将视频提升为点云再重投影来显式重建场景，这种确定性映射把深度误差转化为内容错位，虽然保留纹理但结构不准。

Method: 提出免提升方案：一个学习式视图合成器，基于LVSM风格的Transformer微调，直接渲染内视视角，无需深度、点云或重投影，在内部解决跨视图对应。其概率式映射依据学习到的对应分布，将每个区域在候选源位置间加权平均，从而保留结构但模糊纹理。利用该分布的集中度得到逐区域置信度，用于掩蔽低置信区域，并在去噪早期布局形成阶段引导生成器关注高置信区域。

Result: 方法持续优于SOTA的显式管线，并可在不重新训练的情况下泛化到其他数据集。

Conclusion: 该权衡契合扩散生成器——去噪训练擅长恢复细节，因此有效条件应优先保证结构对齐而非锐度。合成器提供视图结构，扩散模型补充细节。

Abstract: Generating an egocentric video from a single exocentric recording is a challenging case of novel view synthesis, as the two cameras share little overlap and much of the target view is unobserved. Current state-of-the-art methods reconstruct the scene explicitly by estimating depth, lifting the video into a point cloud, and re-rendering it from the egocentric camera to condition a video diffusion model. This deterministic mapping assigns each pixel to a single reprojected location, which preserves texture but translates depth errors into misplaced content. We ask what a video diffusion model should receive as its condition and propose a lifting-free answer: a learned view synthesizer, an LVSM-style transformer fine-tuned to render the egocentric view directly without depth, point clouds, or reprojection, resolving cross-view correspondence internally. In contrast, its probabilistic mapping averages each region over candidate source locations according to a learned correspondence distribution, preserving structure while fine texture is averaged away. We argue that this trade-off suits a diffusion generator, whose denoising training excels at restoring detail, so an effective condition should prioritize structural alignment over sharpness. This distribution's concentration also yields a per-region confidence, used both to mask low-confidence regions and to guide the generator toward high-confidence areas during early layout-forming denoising steps. Our approach consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art explicit pipeline and generalizes to other datasets without retraining. The synthesizer thus supplies view structure, and the diffusion model its detail.

</details>


### [163] [One Block, Multiple Depths: Recurrent Vision Transformers with Depth-Programmed Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12448)
*Adrian Bulat,Yassine Ouali,Georgios Tzimiropoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: reViT通过循环复用单个Transformer块，并用混合专家（MoE）凸组合表示各深度的FFN，在相当推理FLOPs下达到全深度视觉编码器的精度，且存储参数更少，支持弹性深度部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少视觉编码器的存储参数与计算开销，同时在不依赖中间特征蒸馏的前提下保持与全深度编码器相当的精度，并实现深度层面上的参数复用与弹性。

Method: 将单个Transformer块循环应用；用一个小型共享专家库的凸组合来表示每个循环深度的FFN，通过连续归一化深度坐标对该混合进行编程，形成FFN参数空间中可重采样的轨迹；在监督ImageNet-1k训练和DINOv2蒸馏两种设置下评估，并对比权重空间合并、token分发、输出混合等MoE方案。

Result: 权重空间合并在单FFN预算下表现最强；从零训练的reViT-B/16以约少70%存储参数达到DeiT III精度；8专家蒸馏模型仅用教师输出特征即保留DINOv2教师几乎全部线性探测精度，并可迁移到分类、分割和深度预测；弹性深度训练支持单一检查点多深度运行。

Conclusion: 循环式单块编码器结合深度条件FFN混合可在低存储下匹配全深度编码器性能，权重空间合并是所测MoE家族中最佳方案；部署时可将循环块展开为常规稠密图以消除在线路由与合并，代价是存储增加。

Abstract: In this work, we show that a single Transformer block, applied recurrently, can match the accuracy of a full-depth vision encoder at comparable inference FLOPs without intermediate feature distillation. reViT restores depth-specific transformations by representing the FFN at each recurrent depth as a convex combination of a small shared expert bank. A continuous normalized-depth coordinate programs this mixture, defining a resampleable trajectory through FFN parameter space. We evaluate this design in two regimes: supervised ImageNet-1k training and distillation from a DINOv2 teacher. Across both regimes, controlled adaptations identify weight-space merging as the strongest tested MoE family at a matching one-FFN budget, ahead of the token-dispatch and output-mixture alternatives. Trained from scratch, reViT-B/16 attains DeiT III accuracy with about 70\% fewer stored parameters. An 8-experts model distilled using only the teacher's output features retains nearly all of its DINOv2 teacher's linear-probe accuracy and transfers across classification, segmentation, and depth prediction. Elastic-depth training allows one checkpoint (trained model) to operate at multiple tested depths by resampling the same normalized coordinate interval. For fixed-depth deployment, the recurrent block can be materialized as a conventional dense graph, removing online routing and merging without changing the one-FFN-per-depth compute but expanding deployment storage.

</details>


### [164] [VersaCamVLA: Camera-Configurable VLA Policies for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12451)
*Boyao Han,Chen Shi,Jingjing Qian,ZhuoTan Tian,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VersaCamVLA，将相机集合表示与动作学习解耦，使VLA模型可适配任意数量与位姿的相机配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖训练时的固定相机配置，在部署阶段相机数量或位姿变化时性能脆弱。

Method: 学习统一的场景token接口，将任意可变数量、带位姿的RGB视图映射为固定尺寸的隐场景token；采用多信号目标视图预测与Wrist-Augmented Pose Sampling（WAPS），利用腕部相机自然运动获取免费位姿多样性；部署时用轻量空间编码器将场景token作为补充视觉条件注入预训练基础VLA，无需显式3D传感或新视图渲染。

Result: 在RoboTwin、LIBERO和真实机器人平台上，VersaCamVLA持续优于已有VLA方法与直接多视图基线，在不同相机数量和未见相机位姿下保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: 解耦相机配置与动作学习可有效提升VLA模型对相机配置变化的鲁棒性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as powerful foundations for robotic manipulation, but their reliance on fixed camera configurations during training makes them brittle to changes in camera count or pose during deployment. To overcome these limitations, we propose VersaCamVLA, a camera-configurable framework that decouples camera-set representation from action learning. VersaCamVLA learns a unified scene-token interface that maps an arbitrary, variable set of posed RGB views into fixed-size latent scene tokens. This is achieved via multi-signal target-view prediction and Wrist-Augmented Pose Sampling (WAPS), which leverages natural wrist-camera motion for free pose diversity. At deployment, a lightweight spatial encoder injects these compact scene tokens into a pretrained base VLA as a supplementary visual condition, requiring no explicit 3D sensing or novel-view rendering. Experiments on RoboTwin, LIBERO, and a real-robot platform demonstrate that VersaCamVLA consistently outperforms prior VLA methods and direct multi-view baselines, maintaining robust performance across varying camera counts and unseen camera poses.

</details>


### [165] [OmniCapBench: A Deep-Structured Evaluation Framework for Fine-Grained Audio-Visual Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12458)
*Zhongyu Yang,Jiale Tao,Ruitao Chen,Zuhao Yang,Yingfang Yuan,Xueliang Zhao,Auden,Kai Wang,Shuai Shao,Biao Wang,Steve Yves,Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniCapBench，一个将音视频字幕评估重构为深度结构化诊断框架的基准，通过原子化、可验证的评估单元在实体引用、视觉镜头和音频事件三个轨道上评分，能定位MLLM的感知错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型正快速朝向连续音视频推理发展，急需能暴露其能力边界的评估方法。音视频字幕是理想的诊断任务，但现有基准存在耦合的权衡：整体字幕评分有覆盖度但无定位能力，局部探针有定位能力但缺乏覆盖度，而无约束的LLM评判又引入不稳定性。

Method: 提出OmniCapBench基准，将音视频字幕评估重构为深度结构化诊断框架。把预测目标从自由文本转变为原子化、可验证的评估单元集合，分为三个轨道：实体引用、视觉镜头、音频事件。结合确定性约束检查与局部化的LLM语义比较进行可靠评分，并构建了含786个密集标注视频的数据集。

Result: OmniCapBench能有效区分MLLM的感知错误，包括时序定位失败、身份漂移、跨模态错位和幻觉描述。对前沿MLLM的评估显示其局部感知较强，但长时程音视频推理较弱，尤其在身份漂移和跨模态错位方面。

Conclusion: 该基准为全模态（omnimodal）发展提供了细粒度的路线图，揭示了当前MLLM在长时程音视频推理上的薄弱环节。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving toward continuous audio--visual reasoning, creating an urgent need for evaluations that expose their capability limits. Audio--visual captioning is an ideal diagnostic task, yet current benchmarks face a coupled trade-off: whole-caption scores provide coverage without localization, local probes provide localization without coverage, and unconstrained LLM judges introduce instability. We introduce OmniCapBench (Omni-Video Caption Benchmark), a benchmark that reframes audio--visual caption evaluation as a deep-structured diagnostic framework. OmniCapBench shifts the prediction target from free-form text to sets of atomic, verifiable evaluation units across three tracks: entity references, visual shots, and audio events, enabling reliable scoring with deterministic constraint checks and localized LLM-based semantic comparisons. With 786 densely annotated videos, OmniCapBench effectively distinguishes MLLM perception errors, including temporal grounding failures, identity drift, cross-modal misalignment, and hallucinated descriptions. Evaluating frontier MLLMs reveals strong local perception but weak long-horizon audio--visual reasoning, particularly in identity drift and cross-modal misalignment, providing a fine-grained roadmap for omnimodal development.

</details>


### [166] [WorldGuide: Goal-Directed Video World Model for Procedural Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12459)
*Ankan Deria,Komal Kumar,Hisham Cholakkal,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldGuide，将程序性视频生成形式化为视觉世界空间中的闭环任务执行，联合训练规划器与执行器，并构建了 WorldGuide-Bench 数据集，在任务成功率上超过强基线 MiniMax-H3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程程序性任务要求生成过程能够根据实际生成的中间结果进行自适应。开环生成无法适应执行结果，而现有闭环系统常依赖预训练执行器或间接验证，导致"决定动作"与"成功实现动作"之间存在鸿沟。作者希望弥补这一差距，实现面向目标的程序性视频生成。

Method: 论文将程序性视频生成建模为视觉世界空间中的闭环任务执行。仅给定初始图像和任务目标，WorldGuide 预测原子动作、生成对应视频片段，并利用生成结果选择下一动作或终止。规划器与执行器基于相同的步骤级程序性演示进行训练：规划器从视觉进展中预测下一原子动作或任务完成，执行器直接被训练以完成预测动作。此外采用分层视觉记忆以有界历史 token 代价维持长时程状态。为支持联合训练，作者构建了 WorldGuide-Bench。

Result: WorldGuide-Bench 包含约 59K 步骤标注视频，覆盖 245 项任务和 27 个程序性类别。WorldGuide 在 WorldGuide-Bench 上取得 33.33% 的任务成功率，而近期强视频模型 MiniMax-H3 为 29.90%（且后者还获得了参考动作计划）；在 VideoCraft-Bench 上，WorldGuide 在仅目标条件下取得 47.69%，MiniMax-H3 为 32.73%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将规划与可学习的执行相耦合对于面向目标的程序性视频生成至关重要。

Abstract: Video generators and video-based world models can synthesize plausible visual trajectories, but long-horizon procedural tasks require generation to adapt to what has actually been produced. A model must determine the next action from its generated state, execute that action, and recognize when the task is complete. Open-loop generation cannot adapt to execution outcomes, while existing closed-loop systems often rely on pretrained executors or indirect verification. This leaves a gap between deciding an action and successfully realizing it. We formulate procedural video generation as \emph{closed-loop task execution in visual world space} and introduce \textbf{WorldGuide}. Given only an initial image and a task goal, WorldGuide predicts an atomic action, generates its corresponding video clip, and uses the generated result to select the next action or terminate. The Planner and Executor are trained on the same step-level procedural demonstrations: the Planner learns to predict the next atomic action or task completion from visual progress, while the Executor is directly trained to realize the predicted actions. Hierarchical visual memory maintains state across long-horizon execution with bounded history token cost. Due to the lack of step-level action-video supervision for joint planner-executor training, we introduce \textbf{WorldGuide Bench}: approximately 59K step-annotated videos across 245 tasks and 27 procedural categories. WorldGuide achieves a 33.33\% Task Success on \textbf{WorldGuide-Bench}, compared with 29.90\% for the strong recent video model MiniMax-H3, even though MiniMax-H3 receives reference action plans, and achieves 47.69\% on \textbf{VideoCraft-Bench} compared with 32.73\% for MiniMax-H3 under goal-only conditioning. These results demonstrate the importance of coupling planning with learned execution for goal-directed procedural video generation.

</details>


### [167] [OuroWorld: Bringing Any 3D World Alive as Diverse, Endlessly Looping 3D Cinemagraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12461)
*You-Zhe Xie,Ting-Wei Chou,Yu-Hsuan Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OuroWorld 是无掩码框架，将任意静态 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景转化为可从任意视角无缝循环播放的 3D 动态影像（cinemagraph）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 世界模型生成的场景虽逼真可探索，但时间上冻结，缺乏生动多样的运动，且既有多视角动态化方法受限于流体类运动。

Method: 用视觉语言模型推断合理动态并引导视频模型合成参考视频，再提升补全为多视角视频；提出 Inconsistency-Robust Periodic 4DGS：傅里叶级数形变场从构造上保证循环，以参考视角锚定的 Grounded Drift Field 吸收跨视角不一致。

Result: 在 39 个重建与生成场景上优于所有基线，用户研究对比中胜出 70.8%–99.0%；并提出无需真值的评估指标，涵盖生动性、自然度、循环接缝连贯性与场景质量。

Conclusion: OuroWorld 实现了免掩码、支持一般形变/物体运动/光照变化且可从任意视角无缝循环的动态 3D 场景生成。

Abstract: Recent 3D world models generate photorealistic, explorable scenes that remain frozen in time. OuroWorld is a mask-free framework that turns any static 3D Gaussian Splatting scene into a 3D cinemagraph: a dynamic scene with vivid, diverse motion looping seamlessly from any viewpoint. A vision-language model infers plausible dynamics and guides a video model to synthesize a reference video, which we lift and complete into multi-view videos. To learn from this imperfect supervision, we propose Inconsistency-Robust Periodic 4DGS: a Fourier-series deformation field guarantees looping by construction, while a Grounded Drift Field anchored at the reference view absorbs cross-view inconsistency. Unlike prior Eulerian methods limited to fluid-like motion, we capture general deformation, object motion, and illumination change. We introduce a ground-truth-free evaluation covering vividness, naturalness, loop seam coherence, and scene quality. On 39 reconstructed and generated scenes, OuroWorld outperforms all baselines and wins 70.8%-99.0% of user-study comparisons. Project page: https://ouroworld.userwei.com

</details>


### [168] [What 30,000 Hours of Ego-centric Video Does Not Teach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12464)
*Jiahua Dong,Anurag Bagchi,Yash Jangir,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov,Martial Hebert,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Yu-Xiong Wang,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Pavel Tokmakov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对3万小时自我中心人类视频的规模化训练，直接评估世界模型中的智能体与物体交互保真度；发现扩大数据能让智能体建模接近极限，但物体保真度提升缓慢且仍存差距，缩小差距更依赖训练方式而非数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型是物理仿真器的潜在替代方案，但距离实际部署仍有距离。作者希望探究：规模化自我中心人类视频究竟能把世界模型推进多远，并直接考察其对智能体和物体交互的建模能力，而非依赖不透明的下游指标。

Method: 构建包含30,000小时、覆盖1,000多种场景类型和14,000名贡献者的自我中心人类视频数据集；在具有挑战性的分布外基准上直接评估智能体与物体交互保真度；对比100倍数据增长的效果；设计精心的视觉条件方案以分离并测量物体保真度及其饱和点；提出一种监督方案，将模型容量从场景外观转向物体动态。

Result: 训练数据扩大100倍后，智能体和物体保真度均有提升但不均衡：智能体建模良好，物体保真度仍远低于智能体且提升缓慢。精心设计的视觉条件可在远少于全部数据的情况下使保真度饱和。所提监督方案可提升物体保真度，但仍有显著差距。结论可迁移到下游人形建模任务。

Conclusion: 规模化自我中心数据使智能体建模接近其极限，但对其对世界影响（物体动态）的建模仍远远落后；缩小这一差距将取决于模型如何被训练，而不仅仅取决于看到多少数据。

Abstract: World models offer a promising alternative to physics-based simulators, yet remain far from practical deployment. We ask how far scaling ego-centric human video takes them, using a dataset of 30,000 hours spanning over 1,000 scene types and 14,000 contributors. Rather than relying on opaque downstream metrics, we directly evaluate agent and object-interaction fidelity on a challenging out-of-distribution benchmark. Increasing training data by 100x improves both, but unevenly: the agent is modeled well, while object fidelity remains far lower and improves slowly. We show that the agent gains need not come from data, and a careful visual conditioning design saturates fidelity with a fraction of it, which lets us measure object fidelity on its own and discover its saturation point. We then introduce a supervision scheme that shifts capacity from scene appearance toward object dynamics, improving object fidelity though a substantial gap remains. Finally, our conclusions transfer to downstream humanoid modeling. Overall, our results suggest that scaling ego-centric data brings agent modeling close to its limit while leaving its effects on the world far behind, and that closing this gap will depend on how models are trained, not only on how much data they see.

</details>


### [169] [Rubric-CEPR: Self-Evolving Image Editing via Reward-Verified Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12469)
*Ritesh Thawkar,Shubham Patle,Shravan Venkatraman,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Rubric-CEPR自进化框架，仅利用图像编辑器自身生成样本，通过内部表征的评分增强对比编辑-保留奖励进行验证，实现无需人工标注或外部奖励模型的自提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导的图像编辑器虽已强大，但进一步提升依赖人工编辑的训练对或外部奖励模型，这类监督成本高，且可能奖励看似合理实则失败的输出（如未完成指定修改或改动了应保留的内容）。因此希望仅用编辑器自身生成来改进，无需人工目标或训练时外部奖励模型。

Method: 提出自进化框架Rubric-CEPR：用编辑器内部表征，通过评分增强的对比编辑-保留奖励（CEPR）验证自身样本。Planner从无标签图像提出结构化编辑指令；Editor采样多个候选编辑；冻结的Critic用编辑器已暴露的特征，对候选按分解评分检查（编辑实现、旧状态移除、内容保留）打分；非补偿性门控拒绝不可行候选；最优通过验证的候选通过轻量适配器训练蒸馏回编辑器。

Result: 在Qwen-Image-Edit上，Rubric-CEPR将ImgEdit从4.36提升至4.60（+5.5%），物体隔离任务提升+24.9%，并可迁移到GEdit-Bench和Complex-Edit。同样流程使Step1X-Edit在ImgEdit上提升+7.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法为能够从自身验证样本中自我改进的图像编辑器提供了可靠的基线，代码已公开。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editors have become highly capable, yet improving them further still depends on human-edited training pairs or external reward models. Such supervision is costly to obtain and can reward plausible failures: a realistic output may leave the requested change undone or alter content that should be preserved. In this work, we strive to improve a pretrained image editor using only its own generations, without human-edited targets or an external training-time reward model. To this end, we propose a self-evolving framework, named Rubric-CEPR, that verifies the editor's own samples with its internal representations through a rubric-augmented Contrastive Edit-Preservation Reward (CEPR). A Planner proposes structured edit instructions from unlabeled images, the Editor samples multiple candidate edits, and a frozen Critic scores each candidate with decomposed rubric checks for edit realization, removal of the old state, and content preservation, using features already exposed by the editor. Non-compensatory gates reject infeasible candidates, and the best verified candidate is distilled into the editor through lightweight adapter training. On Qwen-Image-Edit, Rubric-CEPR improves ImgEdit from 4.36 to 4.60 (+5.5%), with a +24.9% gain on object isolation, and transfers to GEdit-Bench and Complex-Edit. The same procedure also improves Step1X-Edit by +7.8% on ImgEdit. We hope our approach will serve as a solid baseline for image editors that improve themselves from their own verified samples. Our code is publicly available at $\href{https://riteshthawkar.github.io/Rubric-CEPR/}{\text{this URL}}$

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Diffu-LoRA: A Novel Low-Rank Adaptation for Personalized Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10550)
*Tianjing Li,Wei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Diffu-LoRA，一种通过门控低秩适配与双层优化学习跨层秩分配的参数高效扩散模型个性化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量参考图个性化文生图扩散模型，需在保留主体身份的同时遵循新场景提示。全模型微调参数开销大，而 LoRA 虽减少可训练参数，但如何在各层之间分配适配容量仍不明确。

Method: 在 Transformer 块的线性层中插入可训练低秩组件，并为每个组件分配可学习门控。采用双层优化，在不同数据划分上分别更新适配权重与门控参数；通过渐进剪枝移除门控值最低的组件，以满足预设秩预算，同时保持预训练主干冻结。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion 上，使用 DreamBooth 及额外收集数据集的主体进行实验，相比所评估的微调基线，整体主体保真度与提示对齐均有提升。消融研究分析了双层优化、渐进剪枝与适配器放置的贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习式秩分配是实现参数高效扩散模型个性化的一种实用方法。

Abstract: Personalizing text-to-image diffusion models from a few reference images requires preserving subject identity while following prompts that describe new contexts. Full-model fine-tuning is parameter-intensive, whereas low-rank adaptation (LoRA) reduces the number of trainable parameters but leaves open how adaptation capacity should be distributed across layers. We introduce Diffu-LoRA, a parameter-efficient method that learns this allocation through gated low-rank adaptation. Diffu-LoRA inserts trainable low-rank components into the linear layers of Transformer blocks and assigns a learnable gate to each component. Bilevel optimization updates the adaptation weights and gate parameters on separate data splits, while progressive pruning removes components with the lowest gate values to meet a prescribed rank budget. This procedure allocates adaptation capacity nonuniformly across layers while keeping the pretrained backbone frozen. Experiments with Stable Diffusion on subjects from DreamBooth and additional collected datasets show improved overall subject fidelity and prompt alignment relative to the evaluated fine-tuning baselines. Ablation studies examine the contributions of bilevel optimization, progressive pruning, and adapter placement. These results support learned rank allocation as a practical approach to parameter-efficient diffusion model personalization.

</details>


### [171] [Wieszcz-XIX: A 3.1-Billion-Word Corpus of Pre-1918 Polish and Temporally Bounded Language Models Trained From Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10592)
*Szymon Kocur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布Wieszcz-XIX，一个覆盖1800–1918年波兰语、含67.5亿词元（约31亿词）的语料库，规模比同期标注语料大三个数量级；并基于此从头训练4700万至3.49亿参数的仅解码器模型，验证其时间边界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史波兰语虽有丰富文献，但机器可读的标注语料仅约百万词，其余依赖质量不稳定的OCR，缺乏大规模、可审计、可训练的历史语料。

Method: 构建过滤、去重、审计1918年后泄漏并按文档切分的流水线；量化识别噪声、近似重复与泄漏（残留0.04%–0.38%）；在手改样本上测字符错误率；训练47M–349M参数模型并测其时间边界性。

Result: 可辨识文本字符错误率为0.68%，45%采样段落无法校正；349M模型及107M模型相对现代波兰语基线出现交叉，1918年后词汇每字节多约3.1比特，而历史词汇消耗比特更少，且能保留历史拼写。

Conclusion: 参数量增加带来的收益约为数据二次遍历的两倍；发布语料、代码与权重。（内容警告：模型会复现时代偏见。）

Abstract: Historical Polish is well documented as a language but annotated in machine-readable form only to about a million words for the period this paper covers; the rest sits behind optical character recognition of variable quality. We present Wieszcz-XIX, a corpus of 6.75 billion tokens (about 3.1 billion words) in 294,369 documents, most of them periodical issues, of Polish published from 1800 to 1918, assembled from Wolne Lektury and the Internet Archive by a pipeline that filters, deduplicates, audits for post-1918 leakage and splits at the document level. It is over three orders of magnitude larger than the annotated corpus of the same period, and we quantify its defects: recognition corruption against a false-positive floor, near-identical duplication, which is removed, and post-1918 leakage, which is excluded from the training corpus itself down to a known residue of 0.04 to 0.38% of its bytes, found in the transcribed source, so the published corpus is the trained one document for document. On a hand-corrected sample the character error rate is 0.68% where the text is legible, and 45% of the sampled passages cannot be corrected. On it we train a ladder of decoder-only models from 47M to 349M parameters from scratch, and measure their temporal boundedness. Against two modern Polish base models, one far larger, the 349M shows a crossover, as does the 107M against the comparator of its size: post-1918 vocabulary costs them about 3.1 bits per byte more than period vocabulary, a gap the comparators do not show, and period vocabulary costs them fewer bits than it costs the comparators. Shown period text, the models keep its spelling and the comparators only partly. Adding parameters gains about twice as much as a second pass over the data. We release the corpus, code and weights. Content warning: the models reproduce period prejudice, including antisemitic statements.

</details>


### [172] [Large Language Model-Assisted Preparation of Transportation Management Plans: A Case Study with WisDOT WisTMP System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10650)
*Zihao Sheng,Pei Li,Zilin Huang,Yen-Jung Chen,Yuhao Luo,Zhengyang Wan,Steven T. Parker,David A. Noyce,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，自动生成交通管理计划（TMP）内容，以WisDOT的WisTMP系统为应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作区是交通基础设施中关键但危险的组成部分，需要精心设计的交通管理计划（TMP）来保障安全与通行效率；然而TMP编制工作劳动强度大，且高度依赖从业者经验。

Method: 构建了LLM辅助的TMP内容自动生成框架：对多个不同规模的开放源码LLM进行微调并本地部署以保障数据安全；从历史WisTMP文档中构建领域数据集，将PDF转换为JSON格式的结构化问答对用于模型训练。

Result: 微调在标准文本生成指标上显著提升性能；分章节与策略层面的分析显示模型整体表现良好，但倾向于过度生成策略，难以生成针对具体项目的论证和准确成本估算；模型规模从7B/8B扩展到14B收益有限。

Conclusion: 研究证明了LLM提升TMP编制效率的潜力，同时揭示了LLM辅助TMP开发中仍存在的挑战。源码与演示视频将在 https://zihaosheng.github.io/TMP-LLM/ 公开。

Abstract: Work zones are critical yet hazardous components of transportation infrastructure, requiring carefully designed Transportation Management Plans (TMPs) to ensure safety and mobility. However, TMP preparation remains labor-intensive and heavily dependent on practitioner expertise. This paper proposes a Large Language Model (LLM)-assisted framework to automate TMP content generation, leveraging the WisDOT WisTMP system as the application context. The framework fine-tunes multiple open-source LLMs across different model scales and deploys them locally to ensure data security. To support model training, we construct a domain-specific dataset from historical WisTMP documents by converting PDF files into structured question-answer pairs in JSON format. Experimental results show that fine-tuning significantly improves performance across standard text generation metrics. Further section-wise and strategy-level analyses reveal that, while LLMs achieve strong overall performance, they tend to over-generate strategies and struggle to produce project-specific justifications and accurate cost estimates. In addition, scaling from 7B/8B to 14B yields limited gains. These findings demonstrate the potential of LLMs to improve TMP preparation efficiency while highlighting remaining challenges in LLM-assisted TMP development. The source code and demo videos will be publicly available at https://zihaosheng.github.io/TMP-LLM/.

</details>


### [173] [Cognitive Thermometers: Machine Learning and Logical Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10724)
*Shane Steinert-Threlkeld,Jakub Szymanik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用机器学习衡量语义复杂度，作为比逻辑可定义性更中立的解释框架，认为学习难度比逻辑复杂度更能解释自然语言的意义偏好，并称机器学习模型为"认知温度计"，以统一符号逻辑与连接主义AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
