Abstract: An answer judge instructed to grade the final commitment should reject an explicitly wrong final value even when an earlier value matches the reference. We show that adding one colon to a candidate can violate this requirement depending on the presentation of the structured judging request. Numeric references certify the error, and paired interventions distinguish the candidate edit from the integration's presentation choices. On 200 previously unused DROP and GSM8K source clusters, the edit increased Jev's false acceptance from 1.0% to 26.0% with three output labels and from 3.0% to 26.5% with the published four-label grading instruction under sorted JSON keys. Both candidate variants were rejected under insertion presentation. These interactions passed the prespecified statistical correction even though Jev met the control thresholds under both grading configurations and presentations. Most excess acceptances occurred among candidates assigned larger numerical errors. GPT-6 Sol produced no observed cue-condition false acceptances, with missing responses unresolved. The result shows that basic judging competence can coexist with sharply different vulnerability to a fixed candidate edit across logically equivalent request presentations. The reference-aware grammar and compound ordering change limit the finding's operational scope and leave its internal cause unmeasured.

</details>


### [209] [SAIL: Scientific Agentic Intelligence via a Science-Aware Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11451)
*SAIL Model Team,Boyuan Sun,Bryan Dai,Che Liu,Chi Liu,Derek Li,Hongming Piao,Mengzhuo Chen,Xidong Wang,Yan Shu,Yinda Chen,Ziyang Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAIL，一个面向文献研究、科学编程与多步科研工作流的开放模型，总参数35B、激活参数3B。其核心是"科学感知改进循环"：用前沿AI模型构建的智能体诊断任务失败、定位能力缺口，并据此生成训练任务，经多轮循环与多种训练手段得到SAIL，在科研任务上以更少参数取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放权重模型在科研任务上往往参数规模庞大，而文献研究、科学编程和多步研究流程对模型能力有专门需求，通用训练难以精准覆盖这些能力缺口。作者希望以更小的参数规模实现有竞争力的科研表现。

Method: 构建总参35B、激活3B的开放模型SAIL。采用"科学感知改进循环"：由基于前沿AI模型的智能体分析任务失败，诊断文献任务中的检索与证据选择、编程中的科学假设与推理、长程调研中的规划与修订等能力缺口；借助论文集合与科学代码库构造问题、交互轨迹及带所需环境和工具的可执行任务。多轮循环后，用监督微调、专家训练、多教师在线策略蒸馏和智能体强化学习训练SAIL。

Result: SAIL在多项科研任务上取得有竞争力的性能，且所用参数量显著少于领先的开放权重模型。

Conclusion: 面向科学的失败诊断—任务生成—再训练循环，是提升小激活参数模型科研能力的有效路径，可在更小规模下逼近大型开放模型的表现。

Abstract: We introduce SAIL, an open model with 35B total and 3B active parameters for literature research, scientific coding, and multi-step research workflows. SAIL is developed through a science-aware improvement loop: agents built on frontier AI models analyze its task failures and construct training tasks that address the underlying capability gaps. The diagnosis examines search and evidence selection in literature tasks, scientific assumptions and reasoning in coding, and planning and revision in longer investigations. The agents draw on paper collections and scientific code repositories to build problems, interaction trajectories, and executable tasks with the required environments and tools. We repeat this loop over multiple development cycles and train SAIL through supervised fine-tuning, specialist training, multi-teacher on-policy distillation, and agentic reinforcement learning. SAIL achieves competitive performance across scientific research tasks with substantially fewer parameters than leading open-weight models.

</details>


### [210] [Beyond Sequences: Distilling Structured Decision Memory for LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11501)
*Leikun Liang,Guoshuai Wang,Xingsheng He,Yushan Han,Yunyi Xuan,Xiaoxiao Xu,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MARI框架，通过结构化决策记忆增强LLM推荐，解决多行为建模与难选择场景问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐方法多建模单一行为，或将异构行为压平为同质token序列，忽略其不同决策角色，难以捕捉语义层级与情境细节（如价格与质量的权衡），在高度相似商品的"难选择"场景下性能下降。

Method: MARI维护决策记忆库（DMB），存储用户过去推理形成的结构化决策记忆（SDMs，即目标、约束、权衡的简洁记录）。SDMs通过事后决策蒸馏（Post-Hoc Decision Distillation）从异构行为和用户生成内容中离线生成；在线推理时检索相关SDMs增强LLM推理，解耦记忆构建与在线推断。

Result: 在标准下一项预测和新增的难选择预测任务上显著优于SOTA基线，且延迟开销低；定性分析提供可读的、可操作的决策洞察。

Conclusion: MARI通过显式结构化决策证据实现可解释、可扩展的推理感知推荐，是迈向推理感知推荐系统的具体一步。

Abstract: Despite the adoption of large language models (LLMs) in recommendation systems, prevailing approaches mostly model single-type behaviors (e.g., views or purchases). Even when incorporating multiple behaviors, existing methods flatten heterogeneous actions into homogeneous token sequences, ignoring their distinct decision-making roles. This flattening fails to capture semantic hierarchies and contextual nuances in complex decision-making, such as trade-offs between price and quality. Consequently, performance degrades in critical ``difficult-choice'' scenarios involving highly similar items. To bridge this gap, we propose MARI (Memory-Augmented Recommendation with Interpretability), which grounds predictions in explicit, structured decision evidence. MARI maintains a Decision Memory Bank (DMB) that archives users' past rationales as Structured Decision Memories (SDMs): concise records of goals, constraints, and trade-offs. These SDMs are generated offline via Post-Hoc Decision Distillation from heterogeneous behaviors and user-generated content. By retrieving relevant SDMs to augment LLM reasoning, MARI achieves interpretability and scalability without the prohibitive cost of processing long raw sequences. Extensive experiments show MARI significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard next-item prediction and a newly introduced Difficult Choice Prediction task, incurring low latency overhead by decoupling memory construction from online inference. Qualitative analyses reveal actionable, human-readable insights into user decision-making, marking a concrete step toward reasoning-aware recommendation systems.

</details>


### [211] [Does Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) Dominate Arabic Dialects in LLMs? A Representation-Level Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11510)
*Abdu Sallouh,Nicholas Popovič,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文挑战"大模型内部表征被现代标准阿拉伯语(MSA)主导"这一解释,发现方言表征空间高度重叠且无MSA主导迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在被提示使用阿拉伯语方言语境下,仍倾向于生成现代标准阿拉伯语(MSA)。一个直觉解释是模型内部表征被MSA主导。作者希望检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 将 Shani 与 Basirat (2025) 的语言主导性分析框架扩展到26种阿拉伯语变体,在多层级、多模型家族上进行表征分析,并使用归一化互信息等指标衡量表征的可分性。

Result: 未发现MSA作为阿拉伯语方言内部主导表征的证据;方言表征形成密集且高度重叠的空间,归一化互信息显著下降;最强的可分性效应并不局限于中间层,会随架构不同而向更靠后的层移动。

Conclusion: 输出偏好不等于内部表征主导,MSA偏向生成不能简单归因于表征主导。多语言与方言LLM的研究应区分生成偏差与内部表征几何结构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often default to Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) when generating Arabic, even when prompted with dialectal Arabic. A natural explanation is that their internal representations are dominated by MSA. We test this hypothesis by adapting the language-dominance framework of Shani and Basirat (2025) (https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.blackboxnlp-1.7) to 26 Arabic varieties. Across layers and model families, we find no evidence that MSA acts as a dominant internal representation for Arabic dialects. Instead, dialect representations form a dense and highly overlapping space: normalized mutual information drops sharply compared to patterns reported for more distinct languages. Moreover, the strongest separability effects are not confined to intermediate layers, but can shift toward later layers depending on the architecture. These findings challenge a common interpretation of MSA-biased generation: output preference does not necessarily reveal internal representational dominance. Analyses of multilingual and dialectal LLMs should therefore distinguish generation bias from the geometry of internal representations.

</details>


### [212] [Residual Advantage: Student-Relative Teacher Guidance for RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11519)
*Xiaobing Chen,Zhiqi Pang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Residual Advantage (RA)与CoRA方法，将教师-学生概率残差作为有界一步奖励，构建标准优势并加到验证器优势上，实现步骤级信用分配，在数学推理基准上显著优于GRPO/REINFORCE++及教师-only OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR只给整条响应一个结果标签，步骤内部缺乏独立信用；OPD在token级提供指导，但其逐点信号未反映教师-学生在整个词表上的分歧模式，且稠密无界的对数比监督会过度放大教师影响，而强求解器未必是合适引导者。

Method: 提出Residual Advantage (RA)：将教师-学生概率残差视为有界的一步奖励，减去学生策略下对应状态价值形成标准优势，并在每条响应内中心化后加到验证器优势上；由于引导项在响应内均值为零，验证器优势仍为响应的平均标签，教师仅重新分配步骤间信用。进一步提出CoRA：用验证器优势在同一评分学生批次上更新教师LoRA，并在下一轮残差中使用更新后的教师，使引导适应学生的尝试。

Result: 以Qwen3-1.7B-Base与Qwen3-4B-Base为学生、Qwen3-8B为教师，RA结合GRPO或REINFORCE++在三个数学基准的全部24项比较中均优于原序列优势算法，宏平均Avg@8提升1.7–3.6分，Pass@8提升3.9–6.3分；两种组合均超过教师-only OPD，CoRA再额外提升1.0–1.5 Avg@8。

Conclusion: RA通过有界残差奖励与响应内中心化，将教师指导转化为步骤级信用重分配而不改变响应级标签信号，结合CoRA自适应引导，在数学推理后训练中稳定优于RLVR与教师-only OPD。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two main paradigms for post-training reasoning models. RLVR gives each response a single outcome label, leaving the steps inside it without separate credit. OPD provides token-level guidance at student-visited prefixes, but its pointwise signal does not directly reflect the pattern of teacher--student disagreement across the vocabulary. Dense, unbounded log-ratio supervision can amplify the teacher's influence, yet a strong solver is not necessarily a suitable guide when the student's solution paths depart from the teacher's. We propose Residual Advantage (\RA{}), which treats the teacher--student probability residual as a bounded one-step reward, subtracts the corresponding state value under the student policy to form a standard advantage, and centers the result within each response before adding it to the verifier advantage. The guidance term has zero mean within each response, so the verifier advantage remains the response's mean label and the teacher only redistributes credit among the steps within it. \CoRA{} further updates a teacher LoRA with verifier advantages on the same scored student batch and uses the updated teacher in the next iteration's residual, adapting guidance to the student's attempts. With Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Qwen3-4B-Base students and a Qwen3-8B teacher, \RA{} combined with GRPO or REINFORCE++ improves the underlying sequence-advantage algorithm in all 24 comparisons on three mathematical benchmarks, raising macro Avg@8 by 1.7--3.6 points and Pass@8 by 3.9--6.3 points. Both combinations surpass teacher-only OPD, and \CoRA{} adds a further 1.0--1.5 Avg@8 points.

</details>


### [213] [When Can You Prune Your Network? A Study of Intermediate Neurons in Multilingual Speech Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11520)
*Minnie Kabra,Benjamin Lecouteux,Maximin Coavoux*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究端到端语音句法分析中中间神经网络的作用，提出移除中间NN单元的简化架构，参数减少12%且性能相当或更优，并验证中间单元在冻结预训练编码器时可缩小表征差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音句法分析架构常使用中间神经网络，但其具体作用和有效性尚不明确，作者希望厘清这些中间层的功能并探索更简洁的架构。

Method: 提出一个更简单的端到端语音句法分析架构，移除中间NN单元，对法国、斯洛文尼亚语和奈及利亚语（中低资源语言）进行综合评估，并考察训练数据规模和预训练语音编码器中间层的影响。

Result: 简化架构在参数减少12%的情况下，在ASR和句法分析上达到与先前方法相当或更优的性能；中间NN单元在预训练编码器冻结时有助于缩小表征差距。

Conclusion: 中间NN单元的核心作用是减少冻结编码器下的表征差距，移除后仍能保持甚至提升性能，表明更简洁的端到端架构在语音句法分析中可行且高效。

Abstract: End-to-end speech parsing, a task recently proposed, consists in predicting both the transcription and the syntactic tree for a spoken utterance. Existing architectures for speech parsing often utilise intermediate neural networks. In this work, we examine the effectiveness of intermediate neural networks (NN) for parsing, and, specifically, what role do they play. We introduce a simpler end-to-end architecture for speech parsing, where we remove these intermediate NN units, reducing the parameters by 12%, while achieving comparable or better performance than prior method on both automatic speech recognition (ASR) and parsing. We demonstrate that intermediate NN units help reduce the representational gap when the pre-trained encoder is frozen. We do a comprehensive evaluation of speech parsing on French, and medium-low resource languages Slovenian and Naija. We further investigate the impact of the training data size and intermediate layers of the pretrained speech encoder on speech parsing.

</details>


### [214] [Constitutional Gating and Deterministic Recovery for Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation: Ablations Against a Stateful Adversarial Gatekeeper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11542)
*Masaaki Nakatsu,Reno Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多智能体LLM与有状态对手谈判中的模型调用浪费问题，提出"三部分控制栈"（5支柱运行宪法、4层蜂群、认知退火），在对抗性Gatekeeper测试台上验证其解锁协议合规策略与死锁恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在与有状态对手谈判时存在三类模型调用浪费：礼貌性循环无法满足对手隐藏接受条件、格式错误输出触发重试、合规死锁（对手要求智能体必须拒绝的内容）。研究目标是让控制栈执行符合宪法的策略并对抗蜂群漂移、从死锁中恢复，而非发现新策略。

Method: 采用三部分控制栈：（1）5支柱运行宪法；（2）4层蜂群（Director、三智能体多数投票、Monitor、schema硬门）；（3）认知退火（确定性死锁检测、原子清除智能体侧上下文、规范化恢复消息）。测试台为已发布的对抗性Gatekeeper，其接受规则为固定正则表达式，LLM仅生成回复文本。每配置5次运行，共30次（Gemini 2.5 Pro为智能体，Claude Haiku 4.5为Gatekeeper）。

Result: （i）宪法与Director使可接受的框架成为可能但不可靠：基线0/5解锁，加宪法后1/5和2/5；解锁时一轮内完成，7-8次调用、约15k token（比基线低67-73%）；未解锁时成本高17-38%。（ii）Monitor与硬门未减少解锁，但留下审计轨迹。（iii）在蜜罐到合规死锁下，仅LLM引导0/5逃脱，原子清除加规范化打击5/5逃脱（Fisher p=0.008），调用预算相同，打击零调用。LLM编写的打击5/5未通过确定性预检，尽管LLM Monitor已批准其中4个。关于平均调用与token减少的预注册假设未获支持。各臂成本均由确定性停止规则限制；控制栈以零额外模型成本增加恢复能力。

Conclusion: 控制栈能在不增加模型成本的前提下为多智能体LLM谈判系统提供死锁恢复，但宪法与Director仅令合规框架"可能"而非"可靠"，Monitor与硬门主要提供审计价值而非提升解锁率；确定性机制（原子清除、规范化打击、预检）显著优于纯LLM引导。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems negotiating with a stateful counterpart waste model calls in three ways: polite loops that never meet the counterpart's hidden acceptance condition, malformed outputs that trigger retries, and compliance deadlocks in which the counterpart demands something the agent must refuse. We study a three-part control stack - a 5-Pillar runtime constitution, a 4-tier swarm (Director, three-agent majority vote, Monitor, schema hard gate) and Cognitive Annealing (deterministic deadlock detection, atomic purge of the agent-side context, a canonical recovery message) - against a released adversarial Gatekeeper whose acceptance rules are fixed regular expressions and whose LLM only renders reply text. The testbed has a known solution: it measures whether the stack executes a constitution-aligned strategy against swarm drift and recovers from deadlock, not whether it discovers anything. In five runs per configuration (30 runs; Gemini 2.5 Pro agents, Claude Haiku 4.5 Gatekeeper) we find: (i) the constitution and Director make an acceptable framing possible but not reliable - 0/5 baseline unlocks versus 1/5 and 2/5 with the constitution; when the swarm unlocks it does so in one turn with 7-8 calls and about 15k tokens (67-73% below baseline); when it does not, it costs 17-38% more; (ii) the Monitor and hard gate do not reduce unlocks and leave an audit trail; (iii) under a honeytrap-to-compliance deadlock, LLM-only steering escapes 0 of 5 times while atomic purge plus a canonical strike escapes 5 of 5 (Fisher $p = 0.008$) at the same call budget, with zero calls for the strike. LLM-written strikes failed the deterministic pre-flight 5 of 5 times although an LLM Monitor had approved four. Pre-registered hypotheses on average call and token reduction were not supported. Cost is bounded in every arm by deterministic stop rules; the stack adds recovery at no extra model cost.

</details>


### [215] [Learning the Loop, Not Just the Page: Execution-Grounded Loop Learning for Web Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11543)
*Yuxin Meng,Ruixu Zhang,Junjie Wang,Yuhan Suo,Yuhan Sun,Ruining Hu,Yiyao Yu,Yubin Wang,Shouwei Ruan,Bin Wang,Yue Liao,Yuxiang Zhang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebLoop是一个执行驱动的框架，在同一策略中联合学习网页生成、批评与细化，解决Critic无直接可执行奖励的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能化网页生成方法主要优化最终页面质量，忽视了诊断与修复不完美实现的过程。核心挑战在于Generator和Refiner产生有直接环境奖励的可执行产物，而中间Critic只影响下游行为，缺乏直接可执行的反馈信号。

Method: WebLoop训练一个无执行的Critic，结合需求级可判别性与下游有用性两类互补信号，先建立可靠诊断，再引入后果感知信用分配；三个角色通过组相对策略学习联合优化。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B上，WebLoop在WebRise达到41.5 Overall，在WebGen-Bench达到38.9%准确率，分别较基础模型提升11.3和15.4分；增益可迁移至首轮生成、在27B规模持续、并从纯文本训练泛化到多模态输入。

Conclusion: 受控分析表明，该提升不能仅由额外的细化步骤解释，凸显了学习Critic及循环本身的重要性。

Abstract: Functional Web generation is increasingly optimized with executable rewards, yet existing methods largely focus on the quality of the final page and leave the process of diagnosing and repairing imperfect implementations underexplored. We identify a central challenge in this setting: the Generator and Refiner produce executable artifacts with direct environment rewards, whereas the intermediate Critic influences downstream behavior without a directly executable outcome. We introduce WebLoop, an execution-grounded framework that jointly learns generation, critique, and refinement within a shared policy. WebLoop trains an execution-free Critic with complementary signals for requirement-level discriminability and downstream helpfulness, first establishing reliable diagnosis and then introducing consequence-aware credit, while all three roles are jointly optimized with group-relative policy learning. With Qwen3.5-9B, WebLoop reaches 41.5 Overall on WebRise and 38.9% accuracy on WebGen-Bench, improving the base model by 11.3 and 15.4 points, respectively. The gains transfer to first-pass generation, persist at 27B scale, and generalize from text-only training to multimodal inputs. Controlled analyses further show that the improvement cannot be explained by an additional refinement pass alone, highlighting the importance of learning the Critic and the loop itself.

</details>


### [216] [Prosody-to-Text: Predicting text from low-pass filtered speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11544)
*David Porteš,Aleš Horák*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究"韵律→文本"方向：仅用Whisper模型的前12个最低Mel频带（约450Hz低通）微调，从韵律模式恢复原句，取得36%的词错误率（WER），10%的语句完美恢复，40%的语句WER≤25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本→韵律预测已是成熟任务，但反向的"韵律→文本"（为给定韵律模式生成匹配文本）长期被忽视。作者认为该方向可带来有趣的应用价值，因此希望探究：仅凭韵律模式能恢复多少原始句子内容，并检验低频语音特征与词汇内容之间的关系是否比以往认为的更强。

Method: 对Whisper模型进行微调，但输入仅保留12个最低的Mel频带（相当于约450Hz截止的低通滤波），从而剥离大部分音段/音色信息，仅保留低频韵律相关特征，并据此预测原始文本。此外还进行了"给定正确前缀预测下一token"的实验。

Result: 结果出乎意料地准确：整体WER为36%；10%的语句被完美恢复；40%的语句WER在25%及以下；在给定正确前缀的条件下，下一token预测正确率达79%。

Conclusion: 低频语音特征与词汇内容之间的关系远比此前认为的更强，说明韵律中蕴含相当可观的词汇信息。作者认为应给予该方向更多关注，这可能开启新的应用，例如用韵律引导现代大语言模型的文本生成。

Abstract: While predicting prosody from text is an established task in the field, the opposite direction, predicting text that fits a given prosodic pattern, remains largely overlooked. We find this unfortunate, because this opposite direction could lead to some very interesting use cases. Therefore, in this paper, we make the first steps in the prosody-to-text direction by inves- tigating how much of the original sentence can be recovered from its prosodic pattern. To this end, we fine-tune the Whis- per model using only the 12 lowest Mel bins (low-pass filter with approximately 450Hz cutoff), and obtain surprisingly accurate results (WER 36%), with 10% of utterances be- ing recovered perfectly, and 40% of utterances having Word Error Rate at or below 25%. We also find that, given the correct prefix, the next token was predicted correctly in 79% of cases. Our results suggest that the relationship between low-frequency speech features and lexical content is much stronger than previously thought, and we believe that direct- ing more attention to this topic might open the door to new applications, such as using prosody to guide text generation of modern LLMs

</details>


### [217] [SWE-Journey: Towards More Realistic Evaluation of Coding Assistants through Long-Horizon, Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11559)
*Hexuan Deng,Yue Wang,Wenyu Jiang,Cheng Yang,Haolin Yang,Zhaohua Zhang,Chenchen Zhao,Beiduo Chen,Muxi Chen,Sa Zhu,Geyuan Zhu,Jianhuan Zhuo,Qiuyong Xiao,Tianwen Jiang,Jihong Zhang,Xuebo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWE-Journey 基准，通过长时程任务合成与用户模拟交互，更真实地评估编码助手，发现非程序员用户场景下模型通过率不足 25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码助手基准与实际使用存在两大差距：任务时程短，缺少持续演进的代码库上的长链条开发任务；交互轮次少，缺乏多轮澄清需求与迭代实现。真实场景需长时程、多轮交互，因此需要更贴近现实的评测基准。

Method: 提出 SWE-Journey 基准。针对任务时程差距，设计 weak-to-strong 合成流水线，自动构造长时程编码任务；针对交互差距，从真实交互数据中挖掘四种代表性用户画像，并构建用户模拟智能体以复现真实编码协助交互。

Result: 在软件架构师画像下，模型对需求功能的测试通过率平均超过 75%；而在非程序员画像下不足 25%。表明当前编码助手尚无法为非程序员提供可靠编码支持。

Conclusion: 当前编码助手在面向非程序员时可靠性明显不足，差距源于交互能力。作者识别出"问对、找对、修对"三项交互中的关键能力，作为未来改进方向。

Abstract: Coding assistants such as Claude Code and Codex have become a major application of LLM agents, yet existing benchmarks remain far from real-world use, particularly in task horizon and interaction length. Code assistants require completing long chains of development work in continuously evolving repositories, while repeatedly clarifying requirements and adapting implementations through multi-turn interaction. To address these gaps, we introduce SWE-Journey, a benchmark for more realistic evaluation of coding assistants. To address the task-horizon gap, we propose a weak-to-strong synthesis pipeline that automatically constructs long-horizon coding tasks. To address the interaction gap, we mine four representative user personas from real interaction data and build a user-simulation agent to reproduce realistic code-assistance interactions. On average, models pass over 75% of tests for requested functionality with software architects, but fewer than 25% with non-coders. These results show that current coding assistants still fall short of enabling reliable coding for non-coders. We further analyze the reasons for this gap and identify asking right, finding right, and fixing right as key capabilities during interaction.

</details>


### [218] [Incremental Open-Ended Deep Research with Structured Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11566)
*Meilin Chen,Hongyuan Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出增量式开放式深度研究（Incremental-OEDR）新设定，将研究报告视为可演化的研究状态，并设计结构化框架 Structured Harness 与十年期时间评估框架，实现报告的增量更新，在保持质量的同时提升连续性并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放式深度研究（OEDR）系统主要从零开始生成报告，当新信息不断出现、报告需要持续维护时，这种从零生成方式效率低下，无法有效利用已有知识。

Method: 提出 Incremental-OEDR 设定，将报告视为持续演化的研究状态，通过保留有效知识、修订过时或不完整内容、纳入新信息来增量更新。为此提出 Structured Harness，将报告表示为大纲、章节与支撑证据的结构化集合，并提供结构化检索、持久化结构化证据池以及结构化生成，支持选择性报告更新与证据复用。同时构建覆盖十年的时间评估框架，包含 Single-Step Task 与 Long-Chain Task。

Result: 在 DeepResearch Bench 和 DeepConsult 上，于开源配置（OC）与专有配置（PC）下实验表明，Incremental-OEDR 在保持有竞争力的报告质量同时显著提升报告连续性并降低研究成本。在 DeepResearch Bench 上相比 OEDR，内容级 ROUGE-L F1 最多提升 0.51，大纲级 EM F1 提升 0.63，token 消耗降低 33%，搜索调用减少 61%。

Conclusion: Incremental-OEDR 通过结构化表示与增量更新机制，为需要持续维护的研究报告场景提供了更高效、更连续的解决方案，并具备良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Existing Open-Ended Deep Research (OEDR) systems primarily generate reports from scratch, making them inefficient for scenarios where research reports need to be continuously maintained as new information emerges. We introduce \textbf{Incremental Open-Ended Deep Research (Incremental-OEDR)}, a research setting that treats a report as an evolving research state and incrementally updates it by preserving valid knowledge, revising outdated or incomplete content, and incorporating newly available information. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{Structured Harness}, which represents reports as structured collections of outlines, sections, and supporting evidence, and provides structured retrieval, a persistent structured evidence pool, and structured generation for selective report updating and evidence reuse. We further establish a temporal evaluation framework spanning ten years, with \emph{Single-Step Task} and \emph{Long-Chain Task} to evaluate incremental updates over both individual transitions and long-term update chains. Extensive Experiments on DeepResearch Bench and DeepConsult under both the Open-source Configuration (OC) and Proprietary Configuration (PC) show that Incremental-OEDR maintains competitive report quality while substantially improving report continuity and reducing research costs. As shown in Figure~\ref{fig:profile}, it achieves up to 0.51 higher content-level ROUGE-L F1, 0.63 higher outline-level EM F1, 33\% lower token consumption, and 61\% fewer search calls than OEDR on DeepResearch Bench. For more details, please refer to our project page: https://ioedr-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [219] [Smoothing the Top-k Exposure Boundary for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11575)
*Yunkai Chai,Tong Zhu,Xiaoye Qu,Xuyang Hu,Guanjie Chen,Qipeng Guo,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出弹性专家路由（Elastic Expert Routing），通过在训练时对激活专家预算进行随机采样来软化 top-k 路由的刚性阈值，在保持期望计算成本与推理预算不变的前提下提升稀疏 MoE 模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏 MoE 强制固定的 top-k 专家选择，将连续的路由分布变成刚性阶跃函数，导致在边界附近原本竞争力相近的专家被武断地划入全监督或零反馈区域，路由对分数微小波动过于敏感、脆弱。

Method: 提出弹性专家路由：以 k 为中心的局部离散分布随机采样当前激活的专家预算 k'；由于采样邻域对称，期望计算成本与确定性训练一致，推理预算也保持不变。经过多次迭代训练，原本尖锐的阈值被软化为渐进的概率分布。

Result: 在监督微调下，OLMoE-1B-7B 与 Qwen3-30B-A3B 下游宏平均分别提升 +0.84 和 +2.02 分；在从头预训练中，较静态 top-k 基线在下游任务上平均提升 1.6 分。

Conclusion: 弹性专家路由能有效缓解固定 top-k 带来的路由脆弱性问题，在监督微调和从头预训练两种设定下均稳定优于静态 top-k 基线，且不增加额外的期望计算或推理开销。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts models scale parameter capacity efficiently while maintaining a fixed compute budget per token. However, traditional training paradigms enforce a static choice of top-$k$ experts, which converts a continuous routing distribution into a rigid step function. This constraint introduces a brittle boundary where highly competitive experts are arbitrarily separated into full-supervision and zero-feedback zones based on minor score fluctuations. To address this issue, we propose Elastic Expert Routing, which stochastically samples the active expert budget from a localized discrete distribution centered at $k$. Over multiple training iterations, this mechanism softens the sharp threshold into a gradual probability distribution. Because the sampling neighborhood remains symmetric, this approach matches the expected computational cost of deterministic training, while preserving the inference budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of our method on both supervised fine-tuning and from-scratch pretraining settings. During supervised fine-tuning, elastic routing improves downstream macro-averages on OLMoE-1B-7B and Qwen3-30B-A3B by $+0.84$ and $+2.02$ points, respectively. In addition, in from-scratch pretraining, it outperforms the static top-$k$ baseline by $1.6$ points on average across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [220] [Adapting English Quality Classifiers for Multilingual LLM Pretraining Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11585)
*Vinko Sabolčec,Bettina Messmer,Yassine Turki,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多语言数据质量过滤方法，通过将现有英语质量分类器适配为多语言版本，在100多种语言上实现高质量训练数据筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练数据对LLM性能至关重要，基于模型过滤在高资源语言上有效，但低资源语言因缺乏标注数据而面临挑战。

Method: 在Transformer编码器嵌入之上训练小型多层感知机（MLP），以多语言文本为输入，以英语分类器对机器翻译文本的打分为标签，从而将英语质量分类器转化为多语言变体。

Result: 在1B、3B和8B规模实验中，该方法在下游LLM基准上保持了现有多语言模型过滤基线的性能，且未损害区域和文化知识基准。分类器评分与LLM评分的相关性分析表明，该分类器能学习原始英语变体的评分标准。

Conclusion: 该多语言适配方法可有效扩展质量过滤至100多种语言，并具备良好的跨语言泛化能力，即使对未出现在训练数据中的语言也有效。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language model (LLM) pretraining highlight the role of high-quality training data in improving performance. While model-based filtering has proven effective in selecting high-quality subsets from web-scale corpora, especially for high-resource languages, low-resource languages face challenges due to limited availability of annotated data. This work explores extending quality filtering to over 100 languages by proposing a multilingual adaptation approach that converts an existing English quality classifier into a multilingual variant. Our approach proposes training a small multi-layer perceptron on top of Transformer encoder-only model embeddings, using multilingual text as input and scores obtained from English classifiers applied to machine-translated text as labels. Our 1B, 3B and 8B scale experiments show that our approach maintains the downstream LLM benchmark performance of existing multilingual model-based filtering baselines, without harming regional and cultural knowledge benchmarks. To further evaluate cross-lingual generalization, we compare classifier scores of high-quality synthetic data and web samples, and the correlation of classifier scores with LLM-based ones, revealing that the classifier can learn the scoring criteria of its original English variant, even for languages not included in its training data.

</details>


### [221] [Measuring Cultural Alignment Beyond the Average: A Framework for Evaluating Maternal-Health LLM Interactions in Indian Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11586)
*Umaira Izhar,Gunjan Arora,Pushpendra Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MH-INDIC，一个面向北印度城市与半城市母婴健康交互的文化评估框架，通过十维度行为刻画与 102 名孕产妇的 26 题问卷评估十个大模型，发现模型虽在总体分布上接近人群，但在人口与家庭画像层面上的行为变异显著低于人类；在下游对话生成中，基于人类画像的条件化提示可提升对齐与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗大模型评估主要关注事实正确性、安全性与流畅度，难以判断生成交互是否体现文化情境下的医疗推理。母婴健康决策深受社会与关系规范影响，这一缺口尤为关键，因此需要一个可操作化的文化行为评估框架。

Method: 构建 MH-INDIC 框架，将文化行为操作化为十个母婴健康推理维度；对北印度城市与半城市地区 102 名孕期及产后女性实施 26 题问卷，并据此评估十个大语言模型；区分群体层面的文化对齐与个体画像层面的行为变异。下游应用中，用对齐度最高的闭源与开源模型，在零样本、自我条件化与人类画像条件化三种提示方式下生成文化情境化母婴健康对话。

Result: 多个模型在群体层面的分布上接近人类队列，但所有被评估系统在人口与家庭画像上的行为变异均明显低于人类，显示出总体对齐与画像条件敏感性之间存在差距。在对话生成中，基于人类画像的条件化提示带来更强的画像对齐与更高的对话质量评分。

Conclusion: MH-INDIC 揭示了现有医疗大模型在文化情境化推理上的关键不足：总体分布对齐并不等于对个体画像的敏感性，二者存在系统性脱节。将实测文化画像用于提示条件化，可改善生成交互的文化扎根性，为母婴健康等社会规范敏感的医疗场景提供更细粒度的评估与应用路径。

Abstract: Existing evaluation methods for healthcare LLMs primarily assess factual correctness,safety, and fluency, while providing limited insight into whether generated interactions reflect culturally situated healthcare reasoning. This limitation is particularly important in maternal health, where care decisions are shaped by social and relational norms. We introduce MH-INDIC, a culturally grounded evaluation framework for maternal-health interactions in urban and semi-urban North Indian contexts that operationalises cultural behaviour through ten dimensions of maternal-health reasoning. Using a 26-item survey administered to 102 pregnant and postpartum women from urban and semi-urban North India, we evaluate ten LLMs. We distinguish population level cultural alignment from profile-level behavioural variation. Although several models approximate the human population-level distribution, all evaluated systems exhibit substantially lower variation across demographic and household profiles than the human cohort, revealing a gap between aggregate alignment and profile-conditioned sensitivity. As a downstream application of MH-INDIC, we use the strongest-aligned proprietary and open-source models to generate culturally conditioned maternal-health dialogues under zero-shot, self-conditioned, and human-grounded prompting. Human-grounded conditioning produces stronger profile alignment and dialogue quality ratings, suggesting that measured cultural profiles can improve the cultural grounding of generated interactions

</details>


### [222] [Probing for Long-Horizon Deductive Reasoning Capabilities in Language Models with Prolog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11592)
*Hadeel Al-Negheimish,Jasna Ilieva,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建Prolog逻辑推理合成测试集ProloNg，系统考察前沿大模型在长上下文中的演绎推理能力，发现随着推理深度增加，模型性能大幅下降，多数模型在深度超过10后接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型虽在理论上可处理百万级token的长上下文，但其能否在长上下文中进行超越简单检索的深层推理尚不明确，因此需要实证研究长程推理能力。

Method: 构建名为ProloNg的合成测试平台，用Prolog表达演绎逻辑，系统变化问题的推理复杂度；最难案例推理深度达22、上下文长度62k；评测来自5个前沿模型家族的8个推理模型。

Result: 随着推理深度增长，模型性能显著退化，大多数模型在推理深度超过10之后表现接近随机水平（chance）。

Conclusion: 当前前沿大模型在长上下文中的深层演绎推理能力有限，长上下文处理能力并不等同于长程推理能力。

Abstract: Current frontier LLMs can theoretically process long contexts with 1M tokens or more. But to what extent can they go beyond simple retrieval and perform deeper reasoning over such long contexts? We empirically investigate long-horizon reasoning capabilities of LLMs, focusing on deductive logic expressed in Prolog. We construct ProloNg, a synthetic testbed to probe Prolog Long Reasoning, which systematically varies the complexity (reasoning depth) of problems, where the hardest case has a reasoning depth of 22 and 62k context length. We study 8 reasoning models across 5 families of frontier LLMs, and find that performance degrades substantially as reasoning depth grows, with the majority of models approaching chance beyond depth 10.

</details>


### [223] [Large Language Model Turnover Undermines Screening for Artificial Intelligence-Assisted Scientific Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11599)
*Kazuki Nakajima,Takayuki Mizuno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究量化了大语言模型版本更替对科研手稿AI文本检测的影响，发现检测器在模型代际边界处性能会突然坍塌，其基准准确率只能视为临时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期刊与会议已开始筛查投稿中由大语言模型撰写的文本，但这种筛查依赖针对固定LLM版本集的基准评测，而实际使用的模型版本不断变化，因此需要量化版本更替对检测可靠性的影响。

Method: 将《美国国家科学院院刊》4,000篇ChatGPT之前的摘要，与三家厂商在2023年6月至2026年8月间发布的23个LLM版本的重写文本配对，并在从"每个新版本都重新训练"到"只训练一次、永不更新"的多种维护场景下训练检测器，分析检测迁移与词汇差异的关系。

Result: 仅用某厂商历史版本训练的检测器会在模型代际边界处坍塌：在将人类摘要误判率校准为1%时，最锐边界之前能捕获99%以上的重写，边界之后仅3.8%。用较新版本训练的检测器也会漏检较早版本的重写。词汇差异大致可预测检测迁移成败。在两个模拟筛查场景中，覆盖全部23个版本的筛查要么误标八分之一的人类摘要，要么漏检三分之一最新版本的重写；某商业检测器漏检了最锐边界之后版本的大部分重写，同时几乎不误标人类摘要。

Conclusion: 科研诚信政策应将检测器的基准准确率视为临时性的，每次LLM发布（包括较早版本）后都需重新验证。

Abstract: Journals and conferences have begun to screen submitted manuscripts for text written using large language models (LLMs). The reliability of this screening rests on benchmark evaluations against a fixed set of LLM versions, while the versions in actual use keep changing. Here we quantify how this LLM turnover affects the screening of scientific manuscripts. We paired 4,000 pre-ChatGPT abstracts from the Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences with their rewrites by 23 LLM versions from three vendors, released between June 2023 and August 2026. We then trained detectors under maintenance scenarios ranging from a detector retrained on every new version to one trained once and never updated. Detectors trained only on a vendor's past versions can collapse at the boundaries between model generations: calibrated to falsely flag 1% of human-written abstracts, they catch above 99% of rewrites just before the sharpest boundary and 3.8% just after it. Detectors trained on later versions can also miss rewrites of earlier ones. Vocabulary differences between versions largely track where detection transfers and where it fails. In the two screening scenarios we simulated, screens covering all 23 versions either flagged one in eight human-written abstracts or missed one in three rewrites of the newest version. Indeed, a commercial detector missed most rewrites of the version just after the sharpest boundary while flagging almost no human-written abstracts. Research-integrity policy should therefore treat the benchmark accuracy of a detector as provisional, to be re-verified with every LLM release, including earlier versions.

</details>


### [224] [UXBench Pro: Benchmarking Personalized User Experience in Multi-Turn Dialogue Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11638)
*Mengze Hong,Zeyang Lei,Wenbo Shang,Xia Zeng,Xiying Zhao,Qi Zhu,Chen Jason Zhang,Di Jiang,Taiming Fu,Qiongyi Zhou,Qinghe Chang,Fubao Zhang,Chenxuan Ma,Minlong Peng,Jinfeng Huang,Zineng Zhou,Jindou Wu,Muge Qi,Sijun He,Xin Cui,Di Liang,Yuan Hua,Davey Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 UXBench Pro，包含 1000 个真实用户交互测试实例，覆盖 12 类任务场景与 82 个领域，并配以七维用户画像 FACTORS。作者引入个性化用户奖励模型（URM）与用户模拟器 Sim4Eval 构成的双视角评估范式，并设计 URMBench 与 USimBench 两个元基准来衡量评估器的可靠性，最终得出七项关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于计算的用户体验评估方法（如 UXBench）存在两点不足：一是二元偏好预测提供的洞察有限；二是依赖单一、与用户无关的奖励模型，忽视了用户群体间的异质性，而不同用户的期望可能差异显著。

Method: 构建 UXBench Pro，含 1000 个来自真实用户交互的测试实例（12 类任务场景、82 个领域），每个实例配有一个 FACTORS 用户画像，用七个可解释的行为维度刻画用户并区分用户群体。提出双视角评估范式：个性化 User Reward Model（URM）进行第三人称判断，Sim4Eval 用户模拟器支持多轮交互并在四个认知状态维度上进行第一人称评估。另引入 URMBench 和 USimBench 两个元基准，评估上述评估器复现真实人类偏好与行为的忠实度。

Result: 大量实验得出七项关键发现，表明用户建模与多视角评估的重要性。

Conclusion: 该工作为用户中心式基准测试提供了新视角，并推动了以用户为中心的个性化模型优化。

Abstract: Evaluating user experience (UX) with automated computational methods has gained increasing attention, supported by empirical evidence from UXBench. However, binary preference prediction provides limited insight, while relying on a single user-agnostic reward model overlooks the inherent heterogeneity of users, whose expectations can differ substantially. In this paper, we present UXBench Pro, comprising 1{,}000 test instances derived from real user interactions across 12 task scenarios and 82 domains. Each instance is paired with a FACTORS user profile that characterizes the user through seven interpretable behavioral facets, differentiating user groups. To provide richer evaluation insights, we introduce a dual-perspective paradigm that combines a personalized User Reward Model (URM) for third-person judgment with Sim4Eval, a user simulator that enables multi-turn interactions and provides first-person evaluation across four cognitive state dimensions. To assess the reliability of these based evaluators, we further introduce two meta-benchmarks, URMBench and USimBench, that evaluate how faithfully they reproduce real human preferences and behaviors. Extensive experiments reveal seven key findings that highlight the importance of user modeling and multi-perspective evaluation, offering a fresh perspective on user-centric benchmarking and motivating personalized model optimization.

</details>


### [225] [Phonologically Informed Tokenization for German Speech Recognition: A Cross-Domain Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11646)
*Christopher Witzl,Tobias Bocklet,Korbinian Riedhammer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究比较字符、BPE与音系学分词器在德语端到端语音识别中的表现，发现分词器优劣取决于词表规模而非语言变异类型，且错误拓扑主要由声学编码器决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德语形态丰富且音节结构可被Knuth-Liang断词算法良好预测，作者希望探究具备音系学知识的分词方式能否成为端到端语音识别的有竞争力的目标单元。

Method: 在Omnilingual ASR的wav2vec 2.0+CTC骨干上，对三类分词器（预训练多语言字符表、基于正字法的BPE、由Pyphen音节划分与字素-音素转换得到的音系学单元）进行40次微调，并在三个德语测试集（域内朗读、方言自发、标准德语自发）上评测WER与CER，另做音素级混淆分析。

Result: 域内场景下所有音系学分词器在WER和CER上与BPE及多语言字符基线持平；域偏移下差异沿词表规模而非语言变体划分：小词表时音节感知分词在方言语音上更优，并在自发语音上保持领先；音素级混淆分析显示各分词器犯相同的功能词错误。

Conclusion: 分词器选择可能更多取决于词表预算与部署时期望的分布偏移，而非存在单一普适最优解；错误拓扑主要由声学编码器而非分词器主导。

Abstract: German is a morphologically rich language whose syllable structure is exceptionally well-predicted by the Knuth--Liang hyphenation algorithm. We ask whether phonologically informed tokenization can serve as a competitive target for end-to-end speech recognition. We compare three tokenizer families on the Omnilingual ASR wav2vec 2.0 backbone fine-tuned with CTC: the pretrained multilingual character inventory, a data-driven Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) over orthography, and phonologically informed units from Pyphen syllabification and grapheme-to-phoneme conversion. Across 40 fine-tunes, we evaluate on three German test sets spanning orthogonal shifts: in-domain read speech, dialectal spontaneous speech, and standard-German spontaneous speech. In-domain, all phonologically informed tokenizers match BPE and the multilingual character baseline on both WER and CER. Under domain shift the picture splits along vocabulary size rather than the linguistic axis of variation: at small vocabularies, syllable-aware tokenization improves on dialectal speech, where phonetic surface forms vary but syllable structure is preserved, and stays ahead on spontaneous speech, where new word-forms violate vocabulary closure. A phoneme-level confusion analysis further shows that all tokenizers commit the same canonical function-word errors, indicating that the acoustic encoder, not the tokenizer, dominates the error topology. Our findings suggest that tokenizer choice may depend on the vocabulary budget as much as on the distribution shift expected at deployment rather than reducing to a single universal optimum.

</details>


### [226] [DIAL-OPD: Learning More from Fewer Tokens in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11659)
*Anhao Zhao,Haoran Xin,Junlong Tong,Yingqi Fan,Xuan Lu,Ping Nie,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出DIAL-OPD，一种基于学习价值的token选择方法，通过在log概率与概率空间之间加权奖励幅度（以师生概率的对数均值为权重，并由参数beta控制）来选择监督token。实验表明，仅保留40%的token即可超过全token OPD及多种基线，显著提升数学推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy蒸馏（OPD）通常用token级教师信号监督学生生成的轨迹，采样token变体可降低成本。但研究发现，用更少token训练反而可能优于全token OPD，挑战了"监督越多越好"的直觉，说明应依据学习价值来选择token。现有基于分歧的准则忽略了概率尺度：被师生双方赋予可忽略概率的"低-低token"可能获得较大的对数比奖励，从而妨碍学习。

Method: 提出DIAL-OPD token选择方法，通过以师生概率的对数均值对奖励幅度进行加权，桥接log概率空间与概率空间；用一个参数beta控制该权重，保留得分最高的token进行训练。

Result: 在4组师生对和7个数学推理基准上与9个基线对比：仅保留40% token时，DIAL-OPD优于Vanilla OPD及其全token变体，平均准确率较Vanilla OPD提升达5.25个百分点；AIME25 Pass@16从13.33%翻倍至26.67%；在相同保留率下，较最强token选择基线平均准确率相对提升最高达18%。使用4B教师时，DIAL-OPD在两个学生规模上均超过使用8B教师的最强全token基线，说明有效的监督分配可胜过教师规模扩展。进一步分析表明，适中的beta能在抑制低-低token与保留有用分歧之间取得平衡；token级证据显示DIAL-OPD过滤掉推理价值有限的高奖励token，同时保留对推理正确性至关重要的监督。

Conclusion: 依据学习价值选择token的监督分配策略比单纯扩大监督范围或教师规模更有效；DIAL-OPD通过加权对数比奖励、抑制低-低token，实现了高效且更强的推理蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises student-generated trajectories with token-level teacher signals. Its sampled-token variant avoids the cost of full-vocabulary probabilities. Yet we find that training on fewer tokens can outperform full-token OPD, challenging the intuition that more supervision improves learning. This motivates selecting tokens by learning value. Existing disagreement-based criteria ignore probability scale: tokens assigned negligible probability by both models, termed low-low tokens, can receive large log-ratio rewards and hinder learning. We propose DIAL-OPD, a token-selection method that bridges log-probability and probability spaces by weighting reward magnitude with the logarithmic mean of teacher and student probabilities. A parameter beta controls this weighting, and the highest-scoring tokens are retained. Across 4 teacher-student pairs and 7 mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we compare DIAL-OPD with 9 baselines. Retaining only 40% of tokens, it outperforms Vanilla OPD and its full-token variants, with mean accuracy gains reaching 5.25 percentage points over Vanilla OPD, and doubles AIME25 Pass@16 from 13.33% to 26.67%. It also achieves up to an 18% relative improvement in mean accuracy over the strongest token-selection baseline at matched retention ratios. With a 4B teacher, DIAL-OPD surpasses the strongest full-token baseline using an 8B teacher at both student scales, showing that effective supervision allocation can outweigh teacher scaling. Further analysis shows that moderate beta balances suppressing low-low tokens against preserving useful disagreements. Token-level evidence reveals that DIAL-OPD filters high-reward tokens with limited reasoning value while preserving supervision critical to reasoning correctness.

</details>


### [227] [TRACE: Diagnosing Verifier Brittleness in Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11678)
*Radhika Gaonkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 TRACE 协议，将评分变化从"结论"转为"可检验诊断"，用于区分分数变化反映的是 agent 能力变化还是评测方式变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Verifier 分数既作基准指标又作训练奖励，人们常把分数变化直接解读为能力变化，但它可能只是评测本身发生了变化，因此需要一种能把二者分离的诊断方法。

Method: TRACE 对评测的某一部分施加定向改动，比较配对运行结果，检查 agent 行为是否改变，并对未变轨迹重新评分以判断是否由评分规则导致。实验包括 25 个合成任务、τ²-bench 公开任务的 30 任务初探与 88 新任务大规模复现（每条件多次运行），并对比两个前沿 LLM 评委。

Result: 合成任务中重命名工具使脚本化 agent 分数下降 0.250 而操作完全相同，评分时恢复原名即可消除差距，而同一改动能暴露另一 agent 的真实行为失败；τ²-bench 上 88 任务中重命名工具或重排工具输出对八对 agent-变化中七对影响在 ±0.10 内，而误导性工具名使所有 agent 奖励降 0.20-0.44，证明该设置能检测真实效应；相同重跑会翻转 15-36% 任务结果，单次比较无法区分呈现效应与随机波动；两个 LLM 评委对同一轨迹判定一致，但彼此在 57% 记录上不一致，主因是其一按过程而非结果评分。

Conclusion: TRACE 能区分分数变化所反映的是 agent 本身还是测量方式，提醒在把分数变化当作能力信号前需先排除评测呈现等测量因素。

Abstract: Verifier scores now serve as both benchmark metrics and training rewards for large language model (LLM) agents, and a change in score is routinely read as a change in capability. It may instead reflect a change in the evaluation. We introduce TRACE, a protocol that turns a score change from a verdict into a testable diagnosis: it applies a targeted change to one part of an evaluation, compares paired runs, checks whether the agent's behavior changed, and rescores unchanged trajectories to test whether the scoring rule is responsible. In a controlled suite of 25 synthetic tasks, renaming tools lowers a scripted agent's score by 0.250 even though it performs exactly the same operations; restoring the original names at scoring time closes the entire gap, while the same mutation exposes a genuine behavioral failure in a second agent. On public $τ^2$-bench tasks with four LLM agents, an initial 30-task study finds mixed reward changes whose one clear effect does not replicate. In a larger follow-up on 88 new tasks with repeated runs per condition, renaming tools or reformatting tool outputs leaves reward unchanged to within $\pm$0.10 for seven of eight agent-change pairs, whereas tool names that deliberately mislead lower every agent's reward by 0.20-0.44, showing that the setup can detect real effects. Identical reruns flip 15-36% of task outcomes, so single-run comparisons cannot separate presentation effects from run-to-run variation. Two frontier LLM judges give consistent verdicts when a fixed trajectory is presented differently, yet disagree with each other on 57% of the same records, largely because one grades procedure rather than outcome. TRACE thus separates what a score change says about the agent from what it says about the measurement.

</details>


### [228] [Structured Sentiment Analysis Using Sequence Labeling as Dependency Graph Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11695)
*Muhammad Imran,Ana Ezquerro,Carlos Gómez-Rodríguez,Anders Søgaard,David Vilares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将结构化情感分析转化为依存图解析问题，并通过序列标注方法求解，利用线性化图编码为每个词分配标签以捕捉依存图结构，在5种语言7个数据集上取得与复杂单模型方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化情感分析旨在构建细粒度情感图，其中节点为情感持有者、目标和表达式的片段，弧表示它们之间的关系。传统解析方法较为复杂，作者希望寻找更简洁有效的求解方式。

Method: 将任务建模为依存图解析，但不同于传统解析方法，采用序列标注方式求解。借助线性化图编码的最新进展，为输入中每个词分配一个标签，从而有效捕捉依存图结构。

Result: 在涵盖5种语言（英语、西班牙语、挪威语、巴斯克语和加泰罗尼亚语）的7个数据集上进行实验，性能与领先且更复杂的单模型方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 序列标注结合线性化图编码能够以更简洁的方式有效解决结构化情感分析任务，并在多语言多数据集上达到有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: This study addresses the problem of structured sentiment analysis, whose goal is to obtain a fine-grained sentiment graph where the nodes represent spans of sentiment holders, targets, and expressions, while the arcs define the relationships among them. Our proposed approach casts the task as dependency graph parsing, but departs from traditional parsing methods by solving it through sequence labeling. To do so, we leverage recent advances in linearized graph encodings that allow each word in the input to be assigned a label, effectively capturing the structure of the dependency graph. We conducted experiments on seven datasets spanning five languages (English, Spanish, Norwegian, Basque, and Catalan), showing performance competitive with leading, more complex single-model approaches.

</details>


### [229] [Thinking Inertia: LLMs Keep Thinking When Told Not To](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11765)
*Dianqiao Lei,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Pan Lu,Philip Torr,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型的“非思考”行为，提出三层度量指标（空思考率、问题-前置答案相关性、显式推理率），并在六种提示干预、六个模型和三类任务上评估，发现显式非思考控制无法可靠消除可见推理，模型存在“思考惯性”，且开放任务上答案简洁性与准确率存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益配备显式“思考模式”，但其对立面“非思考”受到较少关注。既有工作通常以禁用思考模式或缺少长推理链作为非思考的代理指标，但这些代理不可靠：禁用思考模式仍可能输出推理，长推理链也可能只是填充而非真实推断，因此需要系统化地测量和评估非思考行为。

Method: 将每个回答归一化为前置推理痕迹与最终答案两部分，并在三个层面评估：(i) 空思考率衡量严格仅答合规；(ii) 指令感知的问题-前置答案相关性衡量问题与前置痕迹的相似度；(iii) LLM作为评判者的显式推理率衡量可见的显式推理。在布尔、多项选择和开放式三类问题上，对六个模型施加六种提示干预进行评测，并通过在相同问题间改写答案空间进行比较。

Result: 显式的非思考控制无法可靠地消除可见推理。模型表现出“思考惯性”：即使施加严格控制，显式推理仍然存在，并随着答案空间开放而更普遍。布尔与多项选择任务的准确率保持稳定，而开放式任务暴露出仅答合规与任务准确率之间的权衡。将同一问题在不同答案空间下改写显示，提供候选答案使仅答回答更易产生。

Conclusion: 非思考是一种不可忽视的能力：仅凭模型设置或指令不能假定能够停止显式推理，它应与推理能力一样接受系统化评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly ship with explicit "thinking modes", yet their counterpart, "no-thinking", has received far less attention. We study LLMs' no-thinking behavior along two axes. a. How to measure no-thinking? Prior work typically defines no-thinking through proxies such as a disabled thinking mode or the absence of long traces. These proxies are unreliable: disabled thinking modes may still emit reasoning, while long traces may contain filler rather than genuine inference. We instead normalize each response into a pre-answer trace and final answer, and evaluate it at three levels: (i) Empty-Thinking Rate for strict answer-only compliance; (ii) instruction-aware Question-Pre-answer Relevance for similarity between the question and pre-answer trace; and (iii) LLM-as-judge Explicit Inference Rate for visible explicit inference. Together, these metrics distinguish answer-only output, relevant but non-inferential text, and explicit inference. b. How does no-thinking vary across tasks and models? We evaluate six prompting interventions on six LLMs across Boolean, multiple-choice, and open-ended questions. We find that explicit no-think controls cannot reliably eliminate visible inference. Models instead exhibit "Thinking Inertia": explicit inference persists even under strict controls and becomes more prevalent as the answer space opens. Accuracy remains stable on Boolean and multiple-choice tasks, whereas open-ended tasks reveal a trade-off between answer-only compliance and task accuracy. Rewriting the same questions across answer spaces shows that supplying candidate answers makes answer-only responses easier to produce. These findings establish no-thinking as a non-trivial capability: stopping explicit reasoning cannot be assumed from model settings or instructions alone and deserves systematic evaluation alongside reasoning ability.

</details>


### [230] [Same Outcome, Different Evidence: Intent Recovery in LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11766)
*Haitong Jiang,Chunlin Liu,Sihan Tang,Chan Wu,Xiaoqing Su,Yuhong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出仅用攻击成功率（ASR）评估大模型安全性会掩盖不同原因导致的"非有害输出"，提出以"操作性理解率（UR）"与ASR配对报告，区分模型是否真正识别并回应了被评估任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同的高安全（非有害）结果可能源于截然不同的机制：模型识别出有害任务并拒绝、根本未识别出该任务、或回答了完全不同的问题。在意图模糊的提示下，低ASR无法说明模型是否真正"接触"了被评估的任务，因此仅凭ASR会被误导。

Method: 将ASR与操作性理解率（UR）配对使用。UR衡量模型回复是否既识别出被评估任务、又将其作为待回答的任务来处理。在不同接口上进行对比分析；并用受控的英文重构实验（将压缩提示逐步显式化）以及FormalLogic数据集作为互补对照。

Result: 配对视角揭示了ASR所掩盖的显著差异：相近的ASR可对应差别很大的任务恢复率。在受控英文重构中，提示越显式，任务恢复率持续上升，而ASR并不遵循同样规律。FormalLogic上则出现高恢复率与频繁有害协助并存的情形。

Conclusion: 非有害结果对模型安全性的信息量并不相同，因此应在LLM安全评估中联合报告"意图恢复（UR）"与ASR，以更准确地刻画模型安全行为。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of large language models commonly summarize harmful-output behavior with attack success rate (ASR). Yet the same non-harmful outcome can arise for very different reasons. A model may recover a harmful task and refuse it, fail to recover the task, or respond to something else entirely. Distinguishing these cases becomes especially important under intent-obscuring prompts, where a low ASR does not reveal whether the evaluated task was actually engaged. To make this distinction explicit, we pair ASR with operative understanding rate (UR), which measures whether a response both identifies the evaluated task and treats it as the task to be answered. Across interfaces, this paired view reveals substantial variation hidden by ASR: similar ASR values can correspond to sharply different recovery rates. Controlled English reconstructions show that recovery consistently improves as compressed prompts become more explicit, whereas ASR does not follow the same pattern. A complementary contrast comes from FormalLogic, where high recovery can still coincide with frequent harmful assistance. Together, these results show that non-harmful outcomes are not equally informative about model safety, motivating the joint reporting of intent recovery and ASR in LLM safety evaluation. Code and experiment inputs are available at https://github.com/kevinjiang0121-cyber/IRIS.

</details>


### [231] [DPPM: Dual-Path Parametric Memory for Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11776)
*Yuhao Chen,Shuochen Liu,Jiayao Shi,Jian Hong,Chen Cheng,Xinyun Ding,Tao Wang,Ya Li,Quan Liu,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双路径参数化记忆（DPPM），通过Evidence路径池化交互历史表示以保留早期证据，通过Delta路径顺序更新关联状态以捕捉偏好变化，融合后生成历史条件LoRA适配器，在跨会话个性化任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期个性化要求语言模型利用交互历史跨会话追踪用户偏好。参数化记忆虽可将历史编码进参数或适配器以减少推理上下文负担，但独立编译的各会话上下文缺乏跨会话整合，而循环式更新又会弱化早期证据。

Method: DPPM包含两条路径：Evidence路径直接池化交互历史的表示以保留早期证据；Delta路径顺序更新关联状态以捕捉变化。两者输出融合后生成历史条件LoRA适配器，实现证据积累与有序修正的结合。

Result: 在多种骨干模型上，DPPM优于所评估的基线，在PersonaMem-v2上达到54.22%，在PrefEval上达到86.79%。

Conclusion: DPPM为跨会话个性化参数化记忆提供了一种简单而有效的设计选择。

Abstract: Long-term personalization requires language models to use interaction history to track users' preferences across sessions. Parametric memory encodes this interaction history into model parameters or adapters, reducing the need to include it in the inference context. However, independent context compilation leaves cross-session integration unspecified, while recurrent updates can attenuate earlier evidence. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Path Parametric Memory (DPPM). Its Evidence path directly pools representations of the interaction history to preserve earlier evidence, while its Delta path sequentially updates an associative state to capture changes. Fusing both outputs produces history-conditioned LoRA adapters that combine evidence accumulation with ordered revision. Across multiple backbones, DPPM outperforms the evaluated baselines, achieving 54.22% on PersonaMem-v2 and 86.79% on PrefEval. These results suggest that DPPM provides a simple and effective design choice for cross-session personalized parametric memory.

</details>


### [232] [From Sparse Representations to Behavioral Insights for Multimodal Depression Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11787)
*Guimin Hu,Zihao Song,Jiachen Luo,Jiayuan Xie,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BehavDep，一种基于稀疏因子的多模态抑郁评估框架，将多模态行为表示分解为稀疏潜在因子，并通过语义桥与行为有意义的概念关联，在弱监督下学习视频级抑郁倾向分数并聚合为用户级评估，兼顾性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态抑郁评估方法依赖稠密且不透明的多模态表示，难以解释预测背后的行为模式，缺乏可解释性。

Method: BehavDep将多模态行为表示分解为稀疏潜在因子，并通过语义桥将其与行为有意义的概念关联；针对用户级标注与异质视频级行为之间的不匹配，在弱监督下学习视频级抑郁倾向分数，并聚合多次观测信息进行用户级评估。

Result: 实验表明BehavDep取得最佳整体评估性能，同时揭示了互补的模态贡献、跨观测的异质行为模式以及对概念级编辑的预测响应。

Conclusion: BehavDep为多模态行为表示提供了一种结构化、可解释的抑郁评估分析方法。

Abstract: Multimodal depression assessment offers a promising approach to analyzing behavioral patterns associated with depression. However, existing methods often rely on dense and opaque multimodal representations, making it difficult to interpret the behavioral patterns underlying their predictions. In this work, we introduce BehavDep, a sparse factor-based framework that decomposes multimodal behavioral representations into sparse latent factors and associates them with behaviorally meaningful concepts through a semantic bridge. To address the mismatch between user-level annotations and heterogeneous video-level behaviors, BehavDep further learns video-level depression tendency scores under weak supervision and aggregates information across multiple observations for user-level assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BehavDep achieves the best overall assessment performance while revealing complementary modality contributions, heterogeneous behavioral patterns across observations, and prediction responses to concept-level editing. These results show that BehavDep provides a structured and interpretable approach to analyzing multimodal behavioral representations for depression assessment.

</details>


### [233] [Easy to anticipate, hard to compute: boundary dependence finds the computed outputs that entropy patching misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11790)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BLT 类字节级模型的"熵跳"分块规则存在系统性盲区：对"类型可预测但取值需计算"的位置（如"=后数字"）会跳过，导致结果准确率崩塌；用无标签的"边界依赖"信号可定位这些位置并显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字节级模型（如 BLT）按字节熵高处分块，让全局算力集中在"下一个字节难预测"的位置。但该规则假设"难预测=重要"，忽略了另一类位置：其类型（token 类别）容易预测，但具体取值必须由模型内部计算得出（如算式"=后"的结果）。这类计算型位置熵值并不一定高，因而在紧张的分块预算下被跳过，准确率急剧下降。作者希望揭示并修复这一盲区。

Method: 1) 在 Meta BLT-1B（10% 字节作为块起点）上对比三种分块规则：熵触发、在每个"="后置边界、熵+无标签边界依赖信号；2) 用低秩微调让 BLT-1B 适配该预算，每规则 3 次运行并做配对检验；3) 从零训练 1M/12M/50M 参数字节模型，各 3 seed，比较边界依赖与熵触发；4) 检验 Scratchpad Patching 的熵触发与随机/答案起始 scratchpad 的差异；5) 提出并评估"边界依赖"信号——即移除某个块起点时模型自身损失的上升幅度，按两字节上下文测量，无需标签即可定位计算型位置。

Result: BLT-1B 在 GSM8K 上：熵规则仅在 16% 的计算结果处设块起点，正确率 19.0%；每个"="后置边界达 51.8%；熵+边界依赖达 67.1%（默认布局 26% 字节为 76.8%）。低秩微调后差距持续（32.9% vs 72.7%，p<1e-200）。从零训练：1M/12M/50M 参数下边界依赖对熵的优势为 -1.6/+10.1/+19.8 点，50M 时计算型结果 35.9% vs 13.9%。50M 下 BLT 熵跳规则对两目标均无帮助。Scratchpad Patching 的熵触发与随机 scratchpad 在最终答案上无差别（5.6% vs 5.9%，5 seed），答案起始 scratchpad 为 38.1%。该效应专属于计算型取值：复制与查找获益甚微，模型无法计算的取值也无提升。

Conclusion: 熵触发的分块规则存在"类型可预测但取值需计算"的系统性盲区，且随模型规模放大。无标签的边界依赖信号能自动定位这些位置，与熵结合后甚至优于人工规则，为字节级模型的分块策略提供了更合理的依据。

Abstract: Byte-level language models such as the Byte Latent Transformer (BLT) group bytes into patches and run their large global model once per patch. BLT starts a patch where a small model's next-byte entropy is high, so global compute goes where the next byte is hard to predict. We show that this rule has a systematic blind spot: positions whose type is predictable but whose value must be computed, such as the number after "=" in a worked math solution. Under tight patch budgets, entropy-triggered layouts skip these positions, and accuracy on them collapses. In Meta's BLT-1B with patch starts on 10% of bytes, the entropy rule puts a patch start at 16% of the computed results in GSM8K solutions and gets 19.0% of them exactly right; a boundary after each "=" at the same patch count gets 51.8%, and entropy combined with a label-free boundary-dependence signal gets 67.1% (default layout at 26% of bytes: 76.8%). The gap survives adapting BLT-1B to the budget with low-rank fine-tuning (32.9% vs 72.7%, three runs per rule, paired p < 1e-200) and grows with model size in byte models trained from scratch at a 10% budget: at 1M, 12M and 50M parameters, boundary dependence beats entropy on final answers by -1.6, +10.1 and +19.8 points, and at 50M it gets 35.9% of computed results against 13.9% (3 seeds each). BLT's entropy-jump rule helps neither target at 50M. The entropy trigger of Scratchpad Patching is likewise indistinguishable from random scratchpads on final answers (5.6% vs 5.9%, 5 seeds), while answer-start scratchpads give 38.1%. The effect is specific to computed values: copies and lookups gain little, and values the model cannot compute gain nothing. Boundary dependence, the rise in the model's own loss when a patch start is removed, measured per two-byte context, finds these positions without labels: combined with entropy it beats the hand-written rule on computed results.

</details>


### [234] [Detecting Spin in Clinical Trials with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11845)
*Tjaš Ajdovec,Marko Robnik-Šikonja,Simon Šuster*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个自动检测临床试验结果切换（outcome switching）的系统，利用语义相似度数据与开源LLM（结合提示工程、token概率分类和多数投票），在2496例测试集上取得F1=0.78、准确率0.90，优于基线文本相似度模型，但逊于微调BERT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验中的"spin"（歪曲性报告）会误导结果呈现，在未达统计显著性的随机对照试验中超过50%存在此问题。对比主要结局与报告结局是检测outcome switching等spin类型的关键，因此需要自动化的outcome switching检测方法。

Method: 使用300对标注了语义相似度的结局数据开发系统。评估了基线文本相似度模型和开源LLM，采用生成的相似度分数及Youden指数确定分类阈值。所提方法包括提示工程、基于token概率的分类，以及用于最终决策的多数投票。

Result: 在包含2496个样本的测试集上，F1为0.78、准确率为0.90，优于基线文本相似度模型，但低于微调版BERT。此外，用LLM为分类实例生成自然语言解释，并人工评估了其质量。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自动检测方法可有效识别outcome switching，性能超过基线相似度模型，但尚未超越微调BERT；LLM生成的解释在人工评估下具备一定质量，为spin检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Spin in clinical trials includes reporting practices that distort the presentation of results. This is particularly critical in medicine, where spin is present in more than 50% of randomized controlled trials that fail to reach statistical significance. The comparison of primary and reported outcomes is crucial for detecting several types of spin, including outcome switching. We used 300 pairs of outcomes labeled with semantic similarity to develop a system for automatic detection of outcome switching. We evaluated baseline text similarity models and open-source LLMs using generated similarity scores and the Youden index to determine the classification threshold. The proposed approach involves prompt engineering, classification based on token probabilities, and majority voting for the final decision. The results on the test set of 2,496 examples with an F1 score of 0.78 and an accuracy of 0.90 outperform baseline text similarity models but trail behind fine-tuned versions of BERT. We used LLMs to generate natural language explanations for the classified instances and manually assessed their quality.

</details>


### [235] [Forms of LLM-Integrated Applications from LLM-Chats to Autonomous AI Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11899)
*Irene Weber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地分析了大语言模型在软件系统中以chatbot、copilot、RAG、workflow、coding agent、AI agent等标签嵌入的形态，论证这些标签并非纯粹的营销用语，而是承载了真实的架构含义，并用统一的"智能体与工具"词汇描述七种反复出现的架构形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正越来越多地作为组件嵌入软件系统，市场上出现chatbot、copilot、RAG、workflow、coding agent、AI agent等多种标签。这些标签究竟代表真实的架构形态，还是仅为品牌营销术语，此前尚未被系统性地评估。本文试图填补这一空白。

Method: 作者调研研究文献与厂商文档，采用统一的"智能体（agents）与工具（tools）"词汇，归纳出七种反复出现的形态：LLM聊天、自定义智能体、检索增强生成（RAG）、AI增强工作流、copilot、编码智能体，以及部分意义上的智能体化RAG。每种形态沿四个结构性维度刻画（智能体化RAG仅部分适用）：架构模式、执行控制与用户干预点、每任务智能体调用次数、工具使用。并以22个来自研究出版物和厂商文档的系统构成的示例语料库作为支撑。

Result: 在所调研的来源中，标签确实带有架构内容，在厂商用法中尤为明显：copilot表示一种路由器-工作者（router-worker）架构，在用户逐步确认下操作宿主应用；而近期的"agent"标签转向由AI规划的多步骤执行，用户仅看到最终结果。四家主要厂商的编码智能体共享同一架构，即一个推理-行动（reason-and-act）循环并委派给子智能体。示例语料库验证了这些描述，同时揭示了它们的适用边界。

Conclusion: LLM相关标签并非纯粹的品牌术语，而是具有可辨识的架构内涵。通过统一词汇和四个结构性维度对七种形态进行刻画，可为理解、比较和设计基于LLM的软件系统提供系统化框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded as components in software systems, marketed under labels such as chatbot, copilot, retrieval-augmented generation, workflow, coding agent and AI agent. Whether these labels denote genuine architectural forms or serve as branding has not been assessed systematically.
  In the sources surveyed, labels do carry architectural content, most clearly in vendor usage: copilot denotes a router-worker architecture operating a host application under step-by-step user confirmation, while the more recent shift to the label agent coincides with AI-planned multi-step execution of which the user sees only the outcome. The coding agents of four major providers share one architecture, a reason-and-act loop delegating to subagents.
  This survey describes seven recurring forms---LLM chats, custom agents, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), AI-enhanced workflows, copilots, coding agents, and, in part, agentic RAG---in a common vocabulary of agents and tools. Each is characterized along four structural dimensions (agentic RAG only partially): the architectural pattern, the control of execution and the point of user intervention, the number of agent calls per task, and tool use. An illustrative corpus of 22 systems from research publications and vendor documentation grounds the descriptions and shows where they reach their limit.

</details>


### [236] [Can Decision Models Understand Stance? Evaluating Jev Against General-Purpose LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11901)
*Xing Li,Jinzhong Ning,Yijia Zhang,Liang Yang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估专用决策模型Jev在立场检测任务上的表现，并与四个通用大模型及两个微调模型对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立场检测需识别作者对特定目标的态度，有时依赖对话上下文。Jev作为面向结构化决策的专用模型，可能成为通用大模型的替代方案，因此需要评估其在立场检测中的适用性。

Method: 在两个立场检测数据集上进行评估：VAST（英文文本）和ZS-CSD（中文对话），将Jev与四个通用大语言模型和两个微调模型进行比较。

Result: Jev在VAST上表现具有竞争力，与GPT-5.6相当并优于其他通用大模型；但在ZS-CSD上落后于更强的LLM，尤其是在区分"支持"与"反对"方面。

Conclusion: Jev在立场检测上兼具潜力与局限；其不足可能与理解回复关系和立场方向有关，而非仅由对话长度导致。

Abstract: Stance detection requires identifying an author's attitude toward a given target, sometimes based on conversational context. Jev, a specialized decision model designed for structured decision-making, offers an alternative to general-purpose large language models (LLMs). In this work, we evaluate Jev on two stance detection datasets, VAST (English texts) and ZS-CSD (Chinese conversations), comparing it with four general-purpose LLMs and two fine-tuned models. Results show that Jev achieves competitive performance on VAST, matching GPT-5.6 and outperforming the other general-purpose LLMs. However, it falls behind stronger LLMs on ZS-CSD, particularly in distinguishing favor from against. Further analysis suggests that this limitation may be related to understanding reply relationships and stance direction rather than conversation length alone. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of Jev for stance detection.

</details>


### [237] [Not Every Change Is Necessary: Recoverable Drift in Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11915)
*Xunlei Chen,Qinghui Gong,Jingkun Xue,Qihe Liu,Shijie Zhou,Fei Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Propose-Then-Project Unlearning (PTP-U) 框架，通过先进行局部解析编辑削弱目标知识关联，再对齐非目标输出分布以恢复能力，在三个基准上实现了遗忘与保留之间最优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型机器遗忘需要在移除不需要知识的同时保留模型其余能力。现有方法虽使用保留目标或限制编辑位置，但达到理想遗忘水平时仍会产生附带改变，损害非目标行为。作者通过恢复对比发现，这些改变中的一部分可以在保持遗忘效果的同时被逆转。

Method: PTP-U 框架分为两个阶段：首先应用局部解析编辑（local analytic edits）削弱目标知识关联；然后将非目标输出分布与原始模型对齐，以在固定遗忘约束下恢复能力。两个阶段共同服务于同一目标：满足遗忘要求，同时保持流畅生成和非目标任务性能。

Result: 在三个基准测试中，PTP-U 在所评估方法中取得了最强的遗忘-保留权衡，遗忘率达到 81.22%-91.03%，平均保留 94.20% 的非目标效用。在相同遗忘水平下，PTP-U 始终保留更高的非目标效用。

Conclusion: PTP-U 通过结合定向遗忘与非目标能力恢复，有效解决了机器遗忘中遗忘效果与非目标能力保留之间的权衡问题，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in large language models aims to remove unwanted knowledge while preserving the model's remaining capabilities. Although existing methods use retention objectives or restrict where edits occur, achieving the desired forgetting level can still leave collateral changes that impair non-target behavior. Our recovery comparisons suggest that some of these changes can be reversed while preserving observed forgetting performance. In this work, we present Propose-Then-Project Unlearning (PTP-U), a framework that combines targeted forgetting with the recovery of non-target capabilities. PTP-U first applies local analytic edits to weaken target knowledge associations, then aligns non-target output distributions with those of the original model to recover capabilities while maintaining fixed forgetting constraints. Both stages serve a common goal: satisfying the forgetting requirements while preserving fluent generation and performance on non-target tasks. Across three benchmarks, PTP-U achieves the strongest forgetting-retention trade-off among evaluated methods, reaching 81.22%-91.03% forgetting while preserving 94.20% non-target utility on average. At matched forgetting, PTP-U consistently retains higher non-target utility.

</details>


### [238] [Event-Centric Memory with Query-Aware Graph Augmentation for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11920)
*Yichen Liu,Chunfeng Yuan,Haowei Liu,Wenjuan Li,Zefeng Lin,Bing Li,Xu Chen,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 QGMem，一种受人类记忆启发的记忆构建与激活框架，将长对话历史转为事件索引的原子记忆单元，并在查询时通过混合检索、查询感知重排激活紧凑工作记忆，同时用图建模工作记忆关系，提升长期对话智能体的记忆与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向持久化、个性化对话智能体，现有记忆系统存在两类局限：扁平结构化记忆轻量但未显式建模事件关系与状态更新；图结构记忆虽显式建模结构，但构建成本高，且长历史中易引入无关关系。

Method: QGMem 将长对话历史转化为事件索引的原子记忆单元，保留个体经验，并将相关单元整合为动态记忆痕迹以保存状态轨迹和当前状态；查询到来时，通过混合记忆检索收集互补候选，并用查询感知重排激活最相关单元，形成紧凑工作记忆；进一步将工作记忆组织为局部图，编码为图 token，与文本工作记忆一同输入 LLM，以增强证据利用和冲突感知推理。

Result: 在六个基准上实验验证了该框架，检索、多跳证据组合、冲突解决和超长对话推理均取得一致提升，同时保持紧凑上下文和适中推理成本。

Conclusion: QGMem 通过事件化记忆、动态记忆痕迹、混合检索与图结构工作记忆的结合，缓解了扁平与图结构记忆各自的不足，为持久个性化对话智能体提供了高效且有效的记忆与推理方案。

Abstract: For persistent and personalized conversational agents, memory systems can enable them to remember, update, and reason over long histories by storing past interactions and retrieving relevant information. Existing memory systems typically follow two paradigms: flat-structured memory and graph-based memory. The former is lightweight but leaves event relations and state updates implicit, while the latter explicitly models memory structure but incurs additional construction cost and introduces irrelevant relations over long histories. To address these limitations, we propose QGMem, a novel memory construction and activation framework motivated by human memory, in which experience is organized into events and query-relevant events are modeled by graph as working memory. QGMem converts long dialogue histories into event-indexed atomic memory units that preserve individual experiences and consolidates related units into dynamic memory traces that retain state trajectories and current states. When a query arrives, hybrid memory retrieval gathers complementary candidate memories, and query-aware reranking activates the most relevant units as a compact working memory. To expose relational dependencies in the working memory and support conflict-aware reasoning, QGMem organizes the working memory as a local graph, which is then encoded as a graph token and provided to the LLM together with the textual working memory to improve evidence utilization during answer generation. Experiments across six benchmarks validate the framework and show consistent gains in retrieval, multi-hop evidence composition, conflict resolution, and ultra-long dialogue reasoning with compact contexts and moderate inference cost.

</details>


### [239] [When History Helps and Hurts: Selective History Use across Multimodal Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11948)
*Shuoyang Sun,Kerui Gu,Hao Fang,Shaoli Huang,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReTurn基准，用7000个多模态任务分离"历史使用需求"与"问题难度"，评测多模态模型的选择性历史使用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话中，历史信息的使用需有选择性：旧问题可能仍相关但其旧答案已过时，当前请求也可能依赖历史证据而新观察相冲突。现有评测很少将这种历史使用需求与底层问题难度分离，缺乏可控评测框架。

Method: 构建ReTurn基准，含7000个基础任务，覆盖视觉与音频证据。分两类：任务承载历史（Reconfirm/Reground，将历史问题应用于当前媒体并变化历史一致性）；证据承载历史（Retrieve/Rebind，用历史媒体回答当前问题并变化当前媒体竞争）。每对保持目标问题、媒体、答案不变，支持开放式与选择题评测，并配有匹配的单轮对照作为可答性参照。

Result: 在13个全模态、视觉语言和音频语言模型上，开放式准确率中位数从直接输入的93.7%降至对话中的72.3%。行为探测显示高问题召回率可与较弱任务应用并存，竞争性媒体可使答案偏离历史目标。监督式适配仅带来部分提升。

Conclusion: ReTurn提供了一个受控框架，用于评估多模态模型是否能选择并使用每个请求所需的历史信息。

Abstract: Reliable multimodal interaction depends on selective use of conversational history: an earlier question may remain relevant while its previous answer is outdated, whereas a current request may depend on historical evidence despite conflicting new observations. Existing multi-turn evaluations rarely separate these history-use demands from underlying question difficulty. To address this gap, we introduce ReTurn, a benchmark of 7,000 base tasks spanning visual and audio evidence for evaluating selective history use. For task-carrying history, Reconfirm/Reground require applying a historical question to current media while varying historical agreement; for evidence-carrying history, Retrieve/Rebind require answering a current question using historical media while varying current-media competition. Each pair preserves the target question, media, and answer. Tasks support open-ended and multiple-choice evaluation, with matched single-turn counterparts serving as answerability references. Across 13 omni-modal, vision-language, and audio-language models, median model-level open-ended accuracy falls from 93.7% with direct input to 72.3% in conversation. Behavioral probes show that high question recall can coexist with weaker task application, while competing media can redirect answers away from historical targets. Supervised adaptation yields only partial gains. ReTurn provides a controlled framework for assessing whether multimodal models select and use the historical information required by each request.

</details>


### [240] [MiMo-V2.6: Scaling Reinforcement Learning Towards Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11959)
*Xiaomi LLM-Core Team,:,Zongming Qiao,Ziyue Hua,Zirui Ou,Zihao Yue,Zihan Jiang,Zhuo Huang,Zhiyang Chen,Zhixian Zheng,Zhipeng Xu,Zhengrui Ma,Yuyang Hu,Yuhang Dong,Yuechen Zhang,Yudong Wang,Yuanxin Liu,Yixin Yang,Yishuo Cai,Yikai Zhao,Yihan Yan,Yifan Zhang,Yifan Song,Xiyu Wei,Xing Zhang,Xin Zhang,Xiaoqian Liu,Xiaodong Ji,Xiangwei Deng,Xueyu Guo,Wenhan Ma,Weimin Xiong,Weikun Wang,Weiji Zhuang,Shuo Liu,Shuhuai Ren,Shuhao Gu,Shimao Chen,Shijie Cao,Shihua Yu,Shicheng Li,Shengjie Zhou,Shaolei Zhang,Rang Li,Qiying Wang,Qingkai Fang,Qianli Chen,Minzheng Wang,Liwen Wang,Linli Yao,Linghao Zhang,Liangyu Cheng,Liang Zhao,Lei Li,Jinhao Dong,Jinyu Xiang,Jianyu Wei,Jiangshan Duo,Huaqiu Liu,Huanjie Fan,Hongyi Guan,Hongshen Xu,Hao Tian,Hanyu Li,Hailin Zhang,Gang Wang,Fuli Luo,Feng Wei,Dong Zhang,Dawei Zhu,Chiheng Lou,Chenhong He,Chenhao He,Chenghua Liu,Bowen Ye,Bowen Shen,Boshen Xu,Bo Yang,Bingquan Xia,Bangjun Xiao,Baixuan Xu,Zhouxiang Mao,Zhiyang Zhang,Zhixiang Xu,Zhenru Lin,Zhengju Tang,Zhaojun Huang,Yuzhe Weng,Yuxing Xiang,Yuxiao Li,Yuheng Yang,Yuhang Wang,Yuchen Liu,Yuanyuan Tian,Yuanliang Dong,Yu Cheng,Yongzhe He,Yongshun Liang,Yong Wang,Yiyan Wang,Yitian Gong,Yijie Zhang,Yanshu Xin,Xun Zhang,Xingjian Zhao,Wenyu Yang,Wenshan Huang,Wenhao Li,Tingwei Huang,Tianyu Yu,Tianyang Lu,Taoyu Yang,Sinan Du,Shutong Tian,Shulin Du,Shengfan Wang,Shanchuan Fang,Qihao Zhang,Qibin Yang,Qian Yu,Qian Tu,Pengrong Xie,Peipei Wang,Peidian Li,Minkun Guo,Mingchen Shao,Luohan Gao,Lijie Wang,Liang Shi,Kaiqi Chen,Kaiming Liu,Kaifei Wang,Kai Yang,Jinlong Xue,Jiechen Zhang,Jiaxuan Liu,Hongxu An,Hao Peng,Hanglong Lü,Guonan Wang,Feiyu Yang,Fanyu Cao,Fangyue Liu,Fan Cui,Cong Wang,Chun Chen,Chenxu Bai,Chengxuan Zhu,Chenghua Wang,Boyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本报告介绍 MiMo-V2.6 系列全模态模型，通过从规模、环境多样性与评分算力三个维度扩展强化学习算力，并结合稳定性与反奖励攻击机制，推动模型智能与自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习是推动大型基础模型走向自我改进的核心训练范式，但如何有效扩展 RL 算力以持续提升模型智能仍是关键挑战。因此作者旨在通过系统性地放大 RL 计算规模来推进模型智能前沿。

Method: 前期在广泛多模态语料上进行中期训练以提供探索空间，并在预训练混合 SWA 架构上搭建基础设施。RL 算力沿三个维度扩展：（1）更大批次与更高吞吐，异步训练每步消耗 1,568 个样本、2.7–3.7B tokens，上下文长度最高达 1M；（2）更复杂多样的环境，覆盖代码、通用、视觉与网络等领域及多种 agent harness；（3）更多评分算力，采用分组式 agentic 评分以获得更准确的长程任务奖励信号。同时冻结 MoE 路由器并构建多层防御抵御奖励攻击，并搭建混合任务 agentic RL 基础设施（统一轨迹表示、高并发多框架 rollout、控制与数据平面解耦、训练-推理一致性）。

Result: 实现了大规模 RL 算力扩展下的稳定训练，获得更准确的奖励信号，并引导模型产生更短、更节省 token 的解决方案。

Conclusion: 开源训练动态、RL 环境与 RL 框架，以促进规模化 RL 与模型自我改进的复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is the central training paradigm for advancing large foundation models towards self-improvement. This report introduces the MiMo-V2.6 series, an omni-modal family that pushes the frontier of model intelligence by scaling RL compute. Prior to RL, we conduct mid-training on a broad multimodal corpus to provide ample exploration space, and build a solid infrastructure on the pretrained hybrid-SWA architecture to support subsequent scale-up. We scale RL compute along three dimensions: (1) larger batches and higher throughput, with an asynchronous training that consumes 1,568 samples and 2.7-3.7B tokens per step at context lengths of up to 1M; (2) more diverse and complex environments, spanning code, general, visual, and cyber domains under a mixture of agent harnesses; and (3) more grader compute, via groupwise agentic grading that yields more accurate reward signals for long-horizon tasks and steers the model towards shorter, more token-efficient solutions. To keep training stable at scale, we freeze the MoE router and establish a multi-layer defense against reward hacking. We further build infrastructure for mixed-task agentic RL, including a unified trajectory representation, high-concurrency multi-framework rollout, decoupled control and data planes, and training-inference consistency. We open-source the training dynamics, RL environments, and RL framework to facilitate reproduction and further research on scaled RL and model self-improvement.

</details>


### [241] [Specialized Decision Models vs. General-Purpose LLMs: Benchmarking Jev Across Knowledge, Reasoning, and Multilingual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11978)
*Xing Li,Qingcheng Chang,Jinzhong Ning,Changfeng Xu,Shenlong Zhang,Yijia Zhang,Ling Luo,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估专门化决策模型 Jev 与通用大语言模型在13个多选基准上的表现，发现 Jev 在知识类任务上可媲美前沿模型，但在数学推理上明显落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一个只返回选项、不生成文本的"System One"专门化决策模型，与通用大语言模型相比是否具有竞争力。

Method: 在13个涵盖知识、推理和多语言理解的多选基准上评估 Jev，并与三个层级（前沿、代表性、小型）共19个 LLM 进行对比。

Result: Jev 在知识和常识基准上与前沿 LLM 相当，在 MMLU-Redux 和 ARC-Challenge 上取得最佳成绩；除数学外优于多数代表性 LLM 和全部小型 LLM；但在 MathQA 上比前沿中位数低17.7分，低于全部19个 LLM。

Conclusion: 专门化决策模型在主要依赖知识的决策上可匹敌通用 LLM，但在需要多步计算的决策上无法胜任。

Abstract: Jev is a "System One" model that returns a choice among given options instead of generating text. We study how such a specialized decision model compares with general-purpose large language models (LLMs). We evaluate Jev on 13 multiple-choice benchmarks covering knowledge, reasoning, and multilingual understanding, and compare it with 19 LLMs in three tiers: frontier, representative, and small. Jev is competitive with frontier LLMs on knowledge and commonsense benchmarks and obtains the best score on MMLU-Redux and ARC-Challenge. Outside mathematics, it also outperforms most representative LLMs and all small LLMs. However, it falls behind on mathematical word problems: on MathQA, it is 17.7 points below the frontier median and scores lower than all 19 LLMs. These results indicate that a specialized decision model can match general-purpose LLMs on decisions that rely mainly on knowledge, but not on decisions that require multi-step calculation.

</details>


### [242] [Examining Social Attribution in LLM Reasoning: A Theory-Guided Probing Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12022)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Qingchao Kong,Dajun Zeng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统探究大语言模型（LLM）的社会归因能力，围绕责任与责备判断构建了包含7,639个问题的社会归因基准，评估32个LLM并揭示其判断与人类存在部分一致性，且与模型规模正相关，同时通过探针方法发现关键归因维度可从隐状态中解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会归因（将外部事件归因于行为主体的社会行为原因）在社会技术系统中至关重要，归因理论在心理学中已有深入研究，但在AI尤其是LLM的社会推理中仍未被充分探索，因此需要系统研究LLM的社会归因判断及其内部机制。

Method: 以归因理论为指导，构建包含Vignette子集（基于经典归因理论情景）和Reality子集（基于真实社会叙事）的社会归因基准，共7,639个责任/责备判断问题；评估32个代表性LLM和5个非LLM基线；并提出基于探针的方法，研究5个关键归因维度在隐空间中的表征及其对LLM判断影响与人类归因理论的一致性。

Result: 当前LLM与人类的责任和责备判断呈可测量但不完全的一致性，且该一致性与模型规模正相关；部分归因维度可从LLM隐状态的特定位置系统性地解码，且其对最终判断的影响与人类归因理论一致。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统探索LLM社会归因，发现其判断与人类部分一致并随规模提升，归因维度的隐空间表征与人类理论相符，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sociotechnical systems where social attribution, the reasoning process attributing external events to the causes and reasons of agents' social behaviors, plays a critical role. These processes involve judgments of social cause, responsibility, and blame/credit to agents. Although attributional models are well-studied in social psychology and cognition through Attribution Theory, social attribution remains underexplored in AI, particularly LLM social reasoning. This paper provides the first systematic exploration of LLM social attribution. Our work focuses on responsibility and blame attributions, examining current LLMs' judgments and their underlying internal mechanisms. Guided by attribution theory, we construct a social attribution benchmark consisting of a Vignette subset based on classic scenarios from attribution theory research and a Reality subset based on real-world social narratives, yielding 7,639 responsibility/blame judgment questions. On this basis, we evaluate 32 representative LLMs and 5 basic non-LLM baselines. To further explore the internal mechanisms underlying the LLM judgment process, we develop a probing-based methodology to investigate the latent-space representations of 5 key attribution dimensions and the consistency of their influences on LLM judgments compared to those in human social attribution. Our research findings reveal that current LLMs exhibit measurable but incomplete agreement with human responsibility and blame judgments, and meanwhile, this agreement is positively correlated with model size.
